Lus Qhia Ua Ntej

'Bhavishya Malika' yog ib phau ntawv sau ntau pua phau uas Panchasakhas (tsib tus phooj ywg/koom tes) ntawm Vaj Ntxuj Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu tau sau kwv yees li 600 xyoo dhau los. Cov ntawv no tau zais cia ntau pua xyoo, thiab lub sijhawm ntawd, feem ntau cov neeg tsis paub txog lawv. Txawm li cas los xij, cua daj cua dub loj heev uas ntaus Odisha xyoo 1999 tau rub ntau tus neeg txoj kev xav mus rau 'Bhavishya Malika' vim nws tau hais tias cov lus faj lem txog cua daj cua dub ntawd twb tau sau tseg rau hauv cov ntawv ntawd lawm.

Txawm li cas los xij, vim nws sau ua lus Odia, Bhavishya Malika tsuas nyob hauv Odisha xwb lub sijhawm ntawd. Tsis tshua muaj leej twg sab nraud Odisha paub txog cov ntawv no. Tab sis maj mam, lub sijhawm hloov. Nyob rau xyoo 2020, tus kab mob COVID-19 tau rov rub cov neeg txoj kev xav mus rau Bhavishya Malika dua. Tsuas yog tom qab tus kab mob no xwb thiaj pib txhawb nqa thiab tshaj tawm cov ntawv Malika sab nraud Odisha, thiab txoj kev no tau txuas ntxiv mus txij thaum ntawd los.

Vim li no, ntau tus neeg tau pib kawm txog Bhavishya Malika nyob rau ob peb xyoos dhau los no. Txawm li cas los xij, raws li feem ntau tshwm sim, nrog rau qhov kev tshaj tawm zoo no, kev txhais lus tsis raug thiab cov lus tsis tseeb txog qee cov nqe Malika kuj tau pib nthuav tawm. Piv txwv li, qee tus neeg tau pib tshaj tawm lus xaiv tias Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog yog 10,000 xyoo; tias tus huab tais kawg ntawm Puri lub npe yuav yog Mukunda Dev; tias cov xwm txheej tseem ceeb ntawm Malika thiab Vaj Ntxuj Kalki cov kev ua Vajtswv rhuav tshem (Sanhaar-Leela) yuav tshwm sim thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani Yoga tom ntej (lub sijhawm ntawm 2054-57); tias thaj chaw Khandagiri tsis nyob hauv Bhubaneswar tab sis nyob rau lwm qhov chaw; thiab tias tseem muaj ntau xyoo ntxiv ua ntej Satyayuga pib, thiab lwm yam.

Cov lus tsis tseeb zoo li no tau pib tsim ntau yam kev tsis ntseeg hauv cov neeg ntseeg lub siab. Vim tsis muaj kev daws teeb meem zoo rau cov kev tsis ntseeg no, lawv txoj kev ntseeg hauv Malika tau pib poob qis. Lub hom phiaj tseem ceeb ntawm tsab xov xwm no tsis yog tsuas yog muab cov ntaub ntawv raug thiab tseeb txog Bhavishya Malika rau cov neeg nyeem tshiab xwb tab sis kuj yog los daws cov kev tsis ntseeg uas tshwm sim hauv cov neeg ntseeg lub siab. Nws cia siab tias los ntawm kev tau txais cov ntaub ntawv raug txog cov ntsiab lus tseem ceeb no, cov neeg ntseeg yuav muaj peev xwm txhawb nqa lawv txoj kev ntseeg hauv Malika kom ruaj khov dua.

Cov Ntsiab Lus

Lus Qhia Ua Ntej i

1 Bhavishya Malika – Kev Taw Qhia Txog

1.1 Kev Taw Qhia

Nyob nruab nrab ntawm xyoo pua 15 thiab 16, tsib tus vajtswv dawb huv loj uas yug hauv Odisha (Shishu Ananta Das, Yashobanta Das, Achyutananda Das, Balarama Das, thiab Jagannatha Das) tau sau ntau pua phau ntawv uas muaj ntau yam lus faj lem. Cov ntawv no tau hu ua 'Bhavishya Malika'. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu, yug hauv xyoo 1486 thiab suav hais tias yog Bhagawan Sri Krishna thiab Jaganmata Radharani txoj kev los yug ua ob, muaj kev sib raug zoo nrog tsib tus vajtswv dawb huv no. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu hu lawv tsib leeg ua 'Panchasakha' (Tsib Tus Phooj Ywg). Cov Panchasakhas no tau sau cov ntawv Malika los ntawm Mahaprabhu tus kheej txoj lus txib. Dhau li ntawm Panchasakhas, lwm tus vajtswv dawb huv loj kuj tau ua raws li kev sau Malika. Piv txwv li, tus neeg pe hawm Salabega yug hauv xyoo pua 17, Saint Arkhit Das thiab Saint Hadidas yug hauv xyoo pua 18, Saint Bhima Bhoi ntawm xyoo pua 19, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa ntawm xyoo pua 20, thiab lwm yam.

Cov lus faj lem ntxaws ntxaws tau sau rau hauv cov ntawv Malika no txog cov ncauj lus xws li lub hnub nyoog ntawm Kaliyuga, kev yug los ntawm Tus Tswv Kalki, kev rhuav tshem cov neeg phem, kev tiv thaiv cov neeg pe hawm, kev tsim tsa Dharma, thiab kev tuaj txog ntawm Satyayuga.

1.2 Panchasakhas yog leej twg?

Ib tug ntawm Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, tau sau rau hauv ib phau ntawv Malika ntawm nws:

Odia Sloka:

ବଳବନ୍ତ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମେ ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Balabanta Yaśhobanta Ananta Achyuta,
Jagannātha Dāsa Pañchasakhā nāme khyāta.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Balarama Das, Yashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, thiab Jagannatha Das tau nto npe ua Panchasakha.

Nws yog ib qho tseem ceeb uas keeb kwm ntawm Panchasakhas yog laus heev. Thaum kawg ntawm txhua lub sijhawm (Yuga), cov Panchasakhas nqes los rau hauv ntiaj teb, yug los ntawm ib feem ntawm Tus Tswv, los muab lawv txoj kev pab cuam tseem ceeb rau Tus Tswv txoj haujlwm rov tsim tsa Dharma.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ପଞ୍ଚକୁଳରେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଆଜ୍ଞାରେ।
ଯୁଗ ଯୁଗରେ ଯେ ଜନମ ହେଉ,
କୃଷ୍ଣଲୀଳାମାନ ମୁଖରେ ଗାଉ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ambhe Pañchasakhā pañchakulare,
janama hoilu Kṛṣhṇa ājñāre.
yuga yugare je janama heu,
Kṛṣhṇalīḷāmāna mukhare gau.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hauv nqe no, Saint Achyutananda Das hais tias: "Peb, tsib tus phooj ywg, yug los hauv tsib tsev neeg sib txawv los ntawm Tus Tswv Krishna txoj lus txib. Hauv txhua lub sijhawm, peb yug los thiab hu nkauj txog Krishna txoj kev ua si dawb huv (Leelas) los ntawm peb lub qhov ncauj."

1.3 Kev Tshwm Sim ntawm Panchasakhas Hla Plaub Lub Sijhawm

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’, piav qhia txog cov duab uas tsib tus vajtswv dawb huv tau coj los hauv txhua lub Yuga plaub:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଦିଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ଅନ୍ତରେ, ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରବେଶ ହୋଇଲା ମହୀରେ।
ଅସୁର ବିନାଶ କରିବା ପାଇଁ, ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିଲେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣେ ଯାଇଁ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଋଷିତନୁ ବହିଲେ, କୃପାଜଳ ଋଷି ଗାଗର୍ବ ହେଲେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଯେ ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ନାରଦ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ କହିଲି ଭେଦ।
ରାମ ଜନମ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଗୁଣ କହିଲେ ନ ସରେ।
ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନଳ ନୀଳ ସୁଷେଣ, ଜାମ୍ବବ ହନୁ ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନ।
ତ୍ରେତୟା ଅନ୍ତେ ଦ୍ୱାପର ପ୍ରବେଶ, ଗୋପାଳ ମୂରତି ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବେଶ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନେ ହେଲେ ଜନମ, ସୁବଳ ସୁବାହୁ ସୁଦାମ ଦାମ।
ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଘେନି ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ହେଲେ, ଦ୍ୱାପର ଲୀଳାମାନ ସାଙ୍ଗ କଲେ।
ଅବନୀ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ନାମ ସ୍ଥାପିଲେ, ହରିନାମରେ ଜଗତ ମୋହିଲେ।
ସ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଶୂଦ୍ର ଆଦି ପାଷାଣ୍ଡ ଯେତେ, ହରିନାମ ଶୁଣି ହେଲେ ମୁକତେ।
ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ ହେଲୁ, ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନାମ ବହିଲୁ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୋଇଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ମେଳ ବଳରାମରେ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ନାମରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ, କହିଦେଲୁ ବାବୁ ତୋର ଆଗେଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Adi Ananta Yugaṭi Antare, Satya Prabesha Hoilā Mahire.
Asura Bināsha Karibā Pāiñ, Prabhu Janamile Brāhmaṇe Jāiñ.
Pañchasakhā Ṛuṣi Tanu Bahile, Kṛupājala Ṛuṣi Gāgarba Hele.
Mārkaṇḍa Je Swayambhū Nārada, Pañchasakhā Janama Kahili Bheda.
Rāma Janama Tretayā Yugare, Tretayā Guṇa Kahile Na Sare.
Tretayāre Naḷa Nīḷa Suṣeṇa, Jāmbaba Hanu Pañchasakhāmāna.
Tretayā Ante Dwāpara Prabesha, Gopāḷa Mūrati Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Besha.
Pañchasakhāmāne Hele Janama, Subaḷa Subāhu Sudāma Dāma.
Shrībatsa Gheni Pañchasakhā Hele, Dwāpara Līḷāmāna Sāṅga Kale.
Abanī Madhyare Nāma Sthāpile, Harināmare Jagata Mohile.
Strī Shūdra Ādi Pāṣāṇḍa Jete, Harināma Shuṇi Hele Mukate.
Ambhe Pañchasakhā Janama Helu, Ananta Achyuta Nāma Bahilu.
Jagannātha Jashobanta Hoile, Pañchasakhā Meḷa Baḷarāmare.
Emanta Nāmare Ambhe Janama, Kahidelu bābu tora āgeṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:

Qhov ntawd yog, thaum ub, thaum kawg ntawm Ananta Yuga thaum Satyayuga pib, Tus Tswv Tshaj Plaws tau yug los ua Brahmin los rhuav tshem cov dab. Thaum ntawd, tsib tus vajtswv dawb huv tau tshwm sim los ntawm Tus Tswv lub cev ua Sages: Kripajala, Garga (Gagarba), Markandeya, Swayambhu, thiab Narada.

Tom qab Satyayuga, hauv Tretayuga, thaum Tus Tswv tau ua ntau yam kev ua si dawb huv hauv Nws txoj kev los yug ua Rama, tsib tus phooj ywg no tau nqes los rau hauv ntiaj teb hauv cov duab ntawm Nala, Nila, Sushena, Jambavan (Jambeba), thiab Hanuman.

Thaum kawg ntawm Tretayuga, Dwaparyuga tau tuaj txog, thiab Tus Tswv tau yug los ua tus tub yug nyuj, Shri Krishna. Lub sijhawm no, Subala, Subahu, Sudama, Dama, thiab Shribatsa tau los ua Panchasakhas thiab koom nrog kev ua si dawb huv nrog Shri Krishna.

Tsis tas li ntawd, hauv Kaliyuga, thaum Tus Tswv tshwm sim hauv Nws txoj kev los yug ua Chaitanya, tsib tus phooj ywg no tau yug los mus tshaj tawm lub npe dawb huv (Hari-naam), tsim txoj kev yooj yim thiab ncaj nraim rau kev cawm seej rau txhua tus. Hauv Kaliyuga, lawv tau nto npe ua Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, Jagannatha Das, Yashobanta Das, thiab Balarama Das.

1.4 Cov Keeb Kwm Ntxaws Ntxaws ntawm Panchasakhas Hla Lub Sijhawm

Thaum tus thwjtim Ramadas nug txog cov npe tshwj xeeb ntawm Panchasakhas hla plaub lub sijhawm, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau muab cov lus piav qhia ntxaws ntxaws no rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Udaya Kahani’.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ଋଷି କୃପାଜଳ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ସଖାଟି ଅଟଇ ନଳ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁଦାମ ସଖାଟି ହୋଇ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲାଇ।
ଗାର୍ଗବ ଋଷି ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ହେଲେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଜାମ୍ବବ ନାମ ବହିଲେ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁବାହୁ ବ୍ରଜ ଅଟନ୍ତି, ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ନାମ କଳିରେ ପାନ୍ତି।
ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସେ ଜାଣେ, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ଅଟେ ସେହି ସୁଷେଣ।
ଗୋପରେ ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ଜାତ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ନାମ ଶିଶୁ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସଖା ନାରଦ ଋଷି, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନୀଳ ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶି।
ଦାମ ଗୋପାଳ ଦ୍ୱାପର ଯୁଗରେ, ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ନାମ କଳିରେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଋଷି ଅଟେ ହନୁମାନ, ସୁବଳ ସଖାଟି ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ପୁଣ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ନାମ କଳିରେ ହେଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶ କଲେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Satya Yugare Ṛuṣi Kṛupājala, Tretayā Sakhāṭi Aṭai Naḷa.
Dwāpare Sudāma Sakhāṭi Hoi, Kaḷi Yugare Achyuta Bolāi.
Gārgaba Ṛuṣi Satya Yugare Hele, Tretayā Jāmbaba Nāma Bahile.
Dwāpare Subāhu Braja Aṭanti, Jashobanta Nāma Kaḷire Pānti.
Swayambhū Satya Yugare Se Jāṇe, Tretayāre Aṭe Sehi Suṣeṇa.
Gopare Shrībatsa Dwāpare Jāta, Kaḷi Yugare Nāma Shishu Ananta.
Satya Yugare Sakhā Nārada Ṛuṣi, Tretayāre Nīḷa Nāma Prakāshi.
Dāma Gopāḷa Dwāpara Yugare, Baḷarāma Dāsa Nāma Kaḷire.
Mārkaṇḍa Ṛuṣi Aṭe Hanumāna, Subaḷa Sakhāṭi Dwāpare Puṇa.
Jagannātha Nāma Kaḷire Hele, Pañchasakhā Nāma Prakāsha Kale.

Kev Txhais Cov Cim Npe:
  • Achyutananda Das: Yog Sage Kripajala hauv Satyayuga, Nala hauv Tretayuga, thiab Sudama hauv Dwaparayuga.
  • Yashobanta Das: Yog Sage Garga hauv Satyayuga, Jambavan hauv Tretayuga, thiab Subahu hauv Dwaparayuga.
  • Shishu Ananta Das: Yog Sage Swayambhu hauv Satyayuga, Sushena hauv Tretayuga, thiab Shribatsa hauv Dwaparayuga.
  • Balarama Das: Yog Sage Narada hauv Satyayuga, Nila hauv Tretayuga, thiab Dama hauv Dwaparayuga.
  • Jagannatha Das: Yog Sage Markandeya hauv Satyayuga, Hanuman hauv Tretayuga, thiab Subala hauv Dwaparayuga.

1.5 Lub Hwj Chim Tshwj Xeeb thiab Kev Txawj Ntse ntawm Panchasakhas

Cov Panchasakhas yog cov vajtswv dawb huv uas paub txhua yam, paub tus kheej, thiab paub Vajtswv. Lawv yog cov Yogis siab tshaj plaws, cov neeg nrhiav, thiab cov neeg pe hawm, txhua tus muaj peev xwm tshaj plaws hauv ib qho kev kawm txog sab ntsuj plig. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das piav qhia qhov no hauv nws Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଗମ୍ୟ ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ,
ଗାର କଟା ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଜାଣେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ଆଗତ ଅନାଗତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭଣେ,
ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ବଖାଣେ।
ଭକ୍ତିର ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମହନ୍ତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Āgamya Bhāba Jāṇe Jashobanta,
Gāra Kaṭā Jantra Jāṇe Ananta.
Āgata Anāgata Achyuta Bhaṇe,
Baḷarāma Dāsa Tattwa Bakhāṇe.
Bhaktira Bhāba Jāṇe Jagannātha,
Pañchasakhā E Oḍiśā Mahanta.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Ntawm cov Panchasakhas uas yug los hauv Odisha:
  • Yashobanta Das yog tus paub txog tej yam zais cia tob tob ntawm yav tag los thiab yav tom ntej (Agam-Nigam).
  • Shishu Ananta Das yog tus kws tshaj lij hauv kev tshawb fawb txog cov duab kos zais cia thiab tshuab (Yantra-Vigyan).
  • Achyutananda Das yog tus pom yav tag los, tam sim no, thiab yav tom ntej (Trikal-Darshi).
  • Balarama Das yog ib tug kws tshawb fawb tob tob txog qhov tseeb kawg nkaus thiab kev xav (Tattwa-Vigyan).
  • Jagannatha Das tsis muaj leej twg sib npaug hauv kev hlub tshua dawb huv (Bhakti-Bhaav).

1.6 Chaw tsim tsa (Ashrams) ntawm cov Panchasakhas

Cov Panchasakhas tau tsim lawv cov Peethas thiab cov chaw nyob ntsiag to ntawm tsib qhov chaw sib txawv thoob plaws Odisha. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das piav qhia qhov no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅଢ଼ଙ୍ଗେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ, ବାଲିପାଟଣାରେ ରହିଲେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସାତ ଲହଡ଼ିରେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ରହି, କ୍ଷେତ୍ରବରେ ବଳବନ୍ତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Nemāḷe Achyuta Aḍhaṅge Jashobanta, Bālipāṭaṇāre rahiḷe Ananta.
Sāta Lahaḍire Jagannātha rahi, Khetrabare Baḷabanta.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
  • Achyutananda Das tau tsim nws lub chaw nyob ntawm Nemalo (hauv cheeb tsam Cuttack).
  • Yashobanta Das tau tsim nws lub Peetha ntawm Adhanga (hauv cheeb tsam Jagatsinghpur).
  • Shishu Ananta Das (Ananta) tau tsim nws lub tsev teev ntuj hauv Balipatna (hauv cheeb tsam Khurda).
  • Jagannatha Das tau tsim nws lub Ashram hauv Sata Lahadi.
  • Balarama Das (Balabanta) tau ua Shrikhetra (Puri) nws qhov chaw nyob.

1.7 Panchasakhas – Vajtswv Txoj Kev Los Yug Ua Neeg ntawm tus Tswv

Cov Panchasakhas tau hais ntau zaus hauv lawv cov ntawv Malika tias lawv yog ib feem ntawm Vajtswv Txoj Kev Los Yug Ua Neeg (Anshavatar) ntawm tus Tswv Tshaj Plaws Nws Tus Kheej. Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv 'Chumbak Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲିଣ କଳିଯୁଗେ ନାମ ସୁଦାମ ସଖା ଅଟନ୍ତି,
ଶ୍ରୀଅଙ୍ଗରୁ ଜାତ କଟିରୁ ଭକତ ଶୁଣ ହୋ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଚେତି।
ମୁଖୁଁ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୃଦୁଁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜାତ ବାହୁ ମୂଁଳୁ ବଳରାମ,
ରାଧାଙ୍କ ହାସ୍ୟରୁ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରେ ଘେନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Achyuta boliṇa Kaḷiyuge nāma Sudāma sakhā aṭanti,
Shrī-aṅgaru jāta kaṭiru bhakata shuṇa ho Bāraṅga cheti.
Mukhuṃ Jashobanta hṛuduṃ ambhe jāta bāhu mūṃḷu Baḷarāma,
Rādhāṅka hāsyaru Jagannātha Dāsa Bāraṅga heture ghena.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hauv Kaliyuga, Achyutananda Das yog tus phooj ywg Sudama los ntawm Dwaparayuga thiab tau yug los ntawm tus Tswv lub duav (Kati). Yashobanta Das yug los ntawm Mahaprabhu lub qhov ncauj dawb huv (Shri-mukh). Shishu Ananta Das tshwm sim los ntawm tus Tswv lub siab (Hriday), thiab Balarama Das yug los ntawm tus Tswv txhais caj npab (Bahu). Tsis tas li ntawd, Jagannatha Das los ntawm Niam Radharani txoj kev luag (Haasya).

Tsis yog Shishu Ananta Das xwb, tiam sis Mahapurush Achyutananda Das kuj tau sau txog qhov no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika, 'Chakada Madana'. Tus Mahapurush sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ନିରାକାର ଦେହୁ ପାଞ୍ଚଟି ଜ୍ୟୋତି,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅନନ୍ତ ପଞ୍ଚକ ମୂରତି।
ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ବଳରାମ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ ଏ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ଧାମ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Nirākāra dehu pāṃchaṭi jyoti,
Achyuta Anaṃta paṃchaka mūrati.
Jashobaṃta Jagannātha Balarāma,
janama hoilu e martya dhāma.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, los ntawm lub cev ci ntsa iab ntawm tus Tswv uas tsis muaj cev, peb tsib tus Panchasakhas tau los, thiab peb tau yug los rau hauv lub ntiaj teb no raws li lub npe Achyutananda, Shishu Ananta, Jashobanta, Jagannatha, thiab Balarama.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv phau ntawv Malika, 'Barnatika':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଶରୀର ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତରୁ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନ୍ମ କହିଛି ଗୁରୁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shrī Kṛiṣhṇa sharīra paṃchabhūtaru,
paṃchasakhā janma kahichi guru.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, kev yug los ntawm cov Panchasakhas tau tshwm sim los ntawm tsib yam khoom ntawm tus Tswv Shri Krishna lub cev.

1.8 Cov lus faj lem los ntawm Vajtswv Txoj Lus Txib

Cov nqe lus ntawm Malika raug suav hais tias yog Mahaprabhu cov lus Nws Tus Kheej. Ib tug ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୁଁ କହୁଛି ବୋଲି ମୋର କଥା ନୁହେଁ ଆଦ୍ୟୁଁ ଖଞ୍ଜିଛନ୍ତି ଧାତା,
କର୍ମବାମ ହେଲେ କଳ୍ପନା ଗ୍ରାସିବ ପ୍ରକୃତି କାଟିବେ ଚିତା।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Muṃ kahuchi boli mora kathā nuheṃ ādyuṃ khañjichanti dhātā,
Karmabāma hele kalpanā grāsiba prakṛuti kāṭibe chitā.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus Mahapurush hais tias: "Txawm kuv sau dab tsi los, cov no tsis yog kuv cov lus. Cov no yog txhua yam uas tus Tswv tau txiav txim siab ua ntej los ntawm qhov pib."

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das kuj tau qhia nws tus thwjtim hauv ‘Patta Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରଖ ଯାକୁସାର,
ଭାବବିନୋଦିଆ ହରିଙ୍କ ଏ ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ସିନା ଅଗୋଚର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Āhuri kathāe shuṇare Bāraṅga heturakha jākusāra,
Bhābabinodiā Hariṅka e bāṇī aṭe sinā agochara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Au tus thwjtim Baranga, mloog! Cov no tsis yog kuv li, tiam sis yog tus Tswv Shri Hari cov lus."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika, 'Anakara Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

ଛତିଶ ସଂହିତା ଅଠସ୍ତରୀ ଗୀତା ବଂଶାନୁ ସପ୍ତବିଂଶରେ,
ଉପବଂଶାନୁ ଦ୍ୱାଦଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ବେନି ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଶତ ଖଣ୍ଡ ରେ।
ପଦ ପଦ୍ୟାବଳି ଲକ୍ଷେକ ଯେ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ସବୁ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ମହିମା,
ତୋ ଆଗେ କହିଲୁଁ ବ୍ରଜ କୁମର ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ସାରସ୍ୱତ ସୀମା।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chatisha saṃhitā aṭhastarī gītā baṃshānu saptabiṃshare,
upabaṃshānu dwādasha khaṃḍa beni bhabiṣhya shata khaṃḍa re.
Pada padyābali lakṣheka je graṃtha sabu Shrīkṛiṣhṇa mahimā,
to āge kahilu braja kumara brahma sāraswata sīmā.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das hais tias cov ntawv nws tau sau muaj xws li 36 Samhitas, 78 Gitas, 27 Banshanucharitras, 24 Upabanshanucharitras, 100 Malika texts, thiab ntau pua txhiab nqe lus (padas). Tus Mahapurush kuj tau hais ntxiv tias tag nrho cov ntawv no tau sau los ntawm tus Tswv Shri Krishna lub hwj chim lossis kev hlub.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀରଙ୍ଗା ଚରଣ ବିନୁ ନ ଜାଣଇ ନୀଳାଚଳେ ମନ ମୋର,
ତାଙ୍କ କଥାମାନ ଉଲ୍ଲେଖ କରଇ ନ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଛାର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shrī raṃgācharaṇa binu na jāṇai nīlāchale mana mora,
tāṃka kathāmāna ullekha karai na kahe Achyuta chāra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hauv nqe sloka no, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das hais tias nws lub siab tsis txuas nrog lwm yam dab tsi tsuas yog tus Tswv Tshaj Plaws ko taw xwb. Nws lub siab tseem nyob hauv Nilachala Dhama, Puri, uas yog, hauv kev hlub tshua rau tus Tswv. Nws kuj hais ntxiv tias txawm nws sau dab tsi hauv cov ntawv Malika, lawv yog txhua tus Tswv cov lus hais tiag tiag; nws tsis tau sau dab tsi los ntawm nws tus kheej lub siab nyiam.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das rov sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଲା ଯେପରି,
ଆମ୍ଭେ ଲେଖିଲୁ ପୋଥି ସେହିପରି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Prabhunkara ājñā helā jepari,
āmbhe lekhilu pothi sehipari.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, raws nraim li tus Tswv Tshaj Plaws tau hais thiab txib, kuv tau sau phau ntawv Malika raws li qhov ntawd.

Achyutanandaji sau hauv nws phau 'Agata Bhabishya Prabandha Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ମୁଖରୁ ଯାହା ମୋତେ ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଉଅଛି,
ସେହି ପଦମାନ ସବୁ ପୁରାଣେ ଲେଖୁଛି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shrī mukharu jāhā mote ājñā heuachi,
sehi padamāna sabu purāṇe lekhuchi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, los ntawm tus Tswv Tshaj Plaws txoj lus txib, los ntawm Nws txoj kev qhia, kuv tab tom sau Malika. Txawm dab tsi los ntawm tus Tswv, kuv tab tom piav qhia cov slokas ntawd hauv Malika Purana.

1.9 Malika yog Phau Ntawv Zais Cia thiab Nws Cov Lus Faj Lem Yog Zais

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋଗୀମାନେ ଯୋଗ ଅନ୍ତ ନ ପାଇବେ ଆଉ କେବା ସାମରଥା।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Yogīmāne yoga anta na pāibe āu kebā sāmarathā.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txawm cov Yogis thiab cov neeg txawj ntse loj loj los tsis tuaj yeem nrhiav pom qhov zais cia ntawm cov teeb meem zais cia no los ntawm Yoga, tsis tas yuav hais txog lwm tus neeg.

Achyutananda Das sau ntxiv txog qhov zais cia ntawm phau ntawv:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଲଟ ପାଲଟ କକ୍ଷା ଏହି ଅଙ୍କ, କର ହୃଦେ ତୁହି ଠାବ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଲୀଳା ଶେଷ ଯେ ହୋଇବ, ମସିହାରେ ନାହିଁ ଠାବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Olaṭa pālaṭa kakshā ehi aṅka, kara hṛude tuhi ṭhāba.
Kaḷiyuga līḷā shesha je hoiba, masihāre nāhiṃ ṭhāba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Cov lus faj lem yog sau raws li kev suav lej, thiab cov lej no yog zais cia los ntawm kev hloov lawv rov qab. Cov xwm txheej yuav tshwm sim tab sis tsis yog raws li cov xyoo txheej txheem; lawv ua raws li kev suav cim zais cia.

Hais txog Kalki txoj kev yug los, nws sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହେବୁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସନ୍ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବୁ।
ଗୁପତ ଭକତ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଯାହା,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ଏ ଅଟେ ଏହା।
ଗୁପତରେ କେତେ ଖେଳ କରିବୁ,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କକୁ ଚଳାଇ ଥିବୁ।
ମୋର ଭକତଟି ଜାଣିବେ ସେହି,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ବୋଲିଟି ଯେ ଏହି।
ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ,
କହୁଅଛନ୍ତି ସେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Gupatare ambhe janama hebu,
gupatare san sāla chālibu.
Gupata bhakata jāṇanti jāhā,
gupata aṅka e aṭe ehā.
Gupatare kete kheḷa karibu,
gupata aṅkaku chaḷāi thibu.
Mora bhakataṭi jāṇibe sehi,
gupata aṅka boliṭi ye ehi.
Gupata gupata ati gupata,
kahuachanti se dīna Achyuta.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus Tswv txoj kev yug los yuav yog zais cia. Cov sijhawm thiab xyoo yuav mus zais cia. Tsuas yog cov neeg ntseeg zais cia thiaj yuav paub qhov no vim tias cov lej yog zais cia.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika, 'Chakada Madana' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଆନେ ନ କହିବୁ ତୁହି,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଗୀତରେ କହଇ ମୋ ପାଦକୁ ଥାଅ ଛୁଇଁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata āne na kahibu tuhi,
dīna Achyuta je gītare kahai mo pādaku thāa chuiṃ.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov lus no tsis yog lus zais xwb, tiam sis yog cov lus zais tshaj plaws uas Achyutananda Das ji tau sau los ntawm kev nco txog tus Tswv thiab xav txog Nws txhais taw paj rwb.

Hauv lwm phau ntawv, tus Mahapurush hais rau nws tus thwjtim Ramadasa tias:

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେଲେ ତୋହ ମୋହ ଦେଖା,
ତେରଜଣ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଥିବୁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା।
ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ ଅଟଇ ହେ ରାମ,
କହୁଅଛି ବ୍ରଜ ବାଳ ହେତୁରେ ତୁ ଘେନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kalki prakāsha hele toha moha dekhā,
terajaṇa madhye thibu āmbhe pañchasakhā.
Gupata ati gupata aṭai he Rāma,
kahuachi braja bāla heture tu ghena.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Tus Tswv Kalki yuav los yug, Nws txoj kev tshwm sim dawb ceev yuav tshwm sim, thiab peb cov Panchasakhas yuav tau lub sijhawm zoo los pabcuam thiab tso siab rau Nws txhais taw. Tiam sis cov kev ua si dawb ceev no yuav tshwm sim tsis yog zais cia xwb, tiam sis yuav zais cia heev.

Tus Mahapurush sau dua tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଶୁଣ ତୁ ରେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର,
ବ୍ରହ୍ମା ଶଙ୍କରକୁ ଭେଦ ତ ନଥିବ ନ ଜାଣିବେ ଇନ୍ଦ୍ର ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata shuṇa tu re Rāmachandra,
Brahmā Shaṅkaraku bheda ta nathiba na jāṇibe Indra Chandra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Au tus thwjtim Ramachandra, cov lus ntawm Malika tsis yog lus zais xwb, tiam sis yog lus zais tshaj plaws. Txawm Prajapati Brahma thiab Tus Tswv Shankar los kuj yuav tsis muaj peev xwm nkag siab lawv cov lus zais, tsis tas hais txog Tus Tswv Indra thiab Tus Tswv Chandra.

Sant Bhima Bhoi kuj tau sau txog qhov no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika, 'Stuti Chintamani' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ସଂସାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ କେମନ୍ତେ ଜାଣିବେ ନର ଅଙ୍ଗେ ଦେହ ବହି,
ଗତ ଆଗତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଜଣାନାହିଁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Saṃsāra madhyare kemaṃte jāṇibe nara aṅge deha bahi,
gata āgata yugara byabasthā samastaṃku jaṇānāhiṃ.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, txhiab txhiab tus txiv neej thiab poj niam hauv ntiaj teb yuav tsis muaj peev xwm tau txais cov ntaub ntawv txog kev txheeb ntawm lub sijhawm, kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, Malika, thiab nws cov lus faj lem. Tsuas yog cov neeg uas Tus Tswv pub Nws txoj kev hlub, uas yog Tus Tswv cov neeg pe hawm hauv lub sijhawm dhau los, thiaj yuav muaj peev xwm paub txog cov lus faj lem tob heev no.

Tam sim no lo lus nug tshwm sim, cov neeg pe hawm Tus Tswv los ntawm lub sijhawm dhau los yog leej twg uas Tus Tswv yuav pub Nws txoj kev hlub rau lawv thiab leej twg yuav ntseeg cov lus ntawm Malika, muaj peev xwm paub txog lawv, thiab muaj kev paub tseeb txog kawg ntawm Kali Yuga thiab Kalki avatar!

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau teb lo lus nug no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'. Tus Mahapurush sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

କୋଟିକେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଏ ରସ ତିନି ସସ୍ରେ ଗଣାସେହି,
ମହିମା ପ୍ରକାଶ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ସେହି ଦାସ ଆନେ ମୁଁ କହନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Koṭike goṭie jāṇaṃti e rasa tini sasre gaṇāसेhi,
mahimā prakāsha nishche sehi dāsa āne muṃ kahaṃti nāhiṃ.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tsuas yog ib tus neeg hauv ib crore (kaum lab) thiaj yuav muaj peev xwm paub txog cov lus faj lem no, thiab tag nrho cov naj npawb ntawm cov neeg zoo li no hauv ntiaj teb yuav tsuas yog 3,000 tus.

1.10 Cov Lus Faj Lem ntawm Malika yuav tsis hloov

Bhavishya Malika muaj cov lus ntawm Tus Tswv Nws tus kheej, yog li ntawd tsis muaj lus nug txog lawv txoj kev ua tsis tiav. Hauv phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ପୃଥିବୀ ଦୁଇଫାଳ ହେଲେ ହୋଇବ, ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମେ ଯିବ।
ପର୍ବତ ଶିଖରେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ମିଥ୍ୟା ନୁହଇଁ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତର ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ କ୍ଷମତା କାହାର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Pṛuthibī duiphāḷa hele hoiba, pūrba sūrya abā pashchime jiba.
Parbata shikhare phuṭiba kaiñ, Achyuta bachana mithyā nuhaiñ.
Achyutara bāṇī aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ khyamatā kāhāra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Ntiaj teb yuav tawg ua ob, lub hnub uas sawv hauv sab hnub tuaj yuav sawv hauv sab hnub poob, paj rwb yuav tawg paj rau saum roob—tiam sis cov lus ntawm Achyutananda yeej tsis muaj cuav. Lawv zoo li kab sau rau hauv pob zeb, thiab tsis muaj leej twg muaj hwj chim los ua pov thawj tias lawv tsis yog."

Hauv ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’, nws sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ସୀତା ଠାକୁରାଣୀ ଅସତୀ ହୋଇବେ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ତିଳେ ନ ଟଳିବ ପାଷାଣର ଗାର ଏହି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Sītā ṭhākurāṇī asatī hoibe parbate phuṭiba kaiñ,
Achyuta bachana tiḷe na taḷiba pāṣāṇara gāra ehi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hauv Kaliyuga, tib neeg tej zaum yuav hu Niam Sita tias tsis ncaj ncees, thiab paj rwb tej zaum yuav tawg paj rau saum roob, tiam sis cov lus ntawm Achyutananda Das yeej yuav tsis hloov, vim lawv zoo li kab kos rau hauv pob zeb.

1.11 Hauv ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, hais txog cov lus ntawm Tus Tswv tsis muaj cev (Nirakar), nws sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଥା ଯେବେ ନ ହୋଇବ, ଆମ୍ଭ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ମିଥ୍ୟା ହେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

E kathā jebe na hoiba, ambha Shrīmukha mithyā heba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hauv nqe no, Tus Tswv tsis muaj cev Nws tus kheej hais tias: "Yog tias cov lus ntawm Malika tsis muaj tseeb, ces kuv lub qhov ncauj dawb ceev yuav raug ua pov thawj tias cuav." Vim tias cov no yog cov lus los ntawm tus Tswv lub qhov ncauj, lawv yeej tsis muaj cuav.

Tus Mahapurush sau dua hauv 'Chakada Madana' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ବାଣୀ ପଥରର ଗାର ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମକୁ ଯିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ ଏହି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shrī Achyuta bāṇī patharara gāra parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
pūrba sūrya abā pashchimaku jiba mo bachana satya ehi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, lub hnub tej zaum yuav sawv hauv sab hnub poob es tsis yog sab hnub tuaj, paj rwb tej zaum yuav tawg paj rau saum roob, tiam sis cov lus ntawm Achyutananda yeej tsis muaj peev xwm hloov tau. Cov lus ntawm Achyutananda yog kos rau hauv pob zeb; lawv yog qhov tseeb tiag tiag.

Ib qho kev los yug ntawm Achyutananda Das ji, Sant Hadi Das, uas yug hauv xyoo pua 18 nyob ze Chatia hauv Jajpur koog tsev kawm ntawv ntawm Odisha, kuj tau sau ntau phau ntawv Malika.

Hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Kali Chautisha', nws sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳିବ ନାହିଁଟି ଏ କଥା ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହୋଇବ ଦିନେ,
ଟାକି ରହିଛନ୍ତି ଭରସା କରି ଭକତମାନେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ṭaliba nāhiṃṭi e kathā nishche hoiba dine,
ṭāki rahichaṃti bharasā kari bhakatamāne.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov khoom sau hauv phau ntawv Malika yeej yuav tsis hloov – lawv yeej yuav tshwm sim ib hnub. Tag nrho cov neeg pe hawm tab tom tos nrog kev ntseeg rau cov xwm txheej no.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika, 'Agata Bhabishyat Malika' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ ବାରମ୍ବାର,
ବୁଝାଏ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଧୀର।
ନମଣ ଏହାକୁ ଯେ ଆନ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ କରେ ଧ୍ୟାନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶଯିବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା,
ସାରିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକାଡୋଳା।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Achyuta dāsa bārambāra,
bujhāe bhakataṃku dhīra.
Namaṇa ehāku je āna,
Achyuta dāsa kare dhyāna.
Kali kalmaṣha nāshajiba,
Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Anaṃta Achyutanka līlā,
sāribe prabhu chakāḍolā.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Nws sau tias kuv tab tom piav qhia rau cov neeg pe hawm nrog kev ua siab ntev. Cov neeg pe hawm yuav tsum tsis txhob xav tias cov lus no yog cuav, vim Achyuta tau sau cov ntawv no thaum nws tab tom xav txog Vajtswv. Kev txhaum thiab kev txom nyem ntawm Kali Yuga yuav raug rhuav tshem, thiab kev ua si dawb ceev ntawm Tus Tswv Kalki yeej muaj tseeb. Tus Mahapurush sau ntxiv tias nws cov lus yeej yuav tsis hloov.

Tus Mahapurush sau dua hauv ib phau ntawv Malika ntawm nws tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଅମୃତ ଭକ୍ଷି ଯାଇପାରେ ପ୍ରାଣ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ନୋହିବ ଆନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Amṛita bhakṣhi jāipāre prāṇa,
Achyuta bachana nohiba āna.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, txawm yog qhov ua tsis tau zoo li poob txoj sia es tsis yog tau txoj sia nyob mus ib txhis los ntawm kev haus dej cawv (Amrit) los kuj yuav ua tau, tiam sis cov lus ntawm Achyutananda Das yeej tsis muaj cuav.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau 'Udaya Kahani' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଆନ ନୋହିବ,
କହିଲେ ଅପ୍ରାଧ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପାଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Achyuta bachana āna nohiba,
kahile aparādha nishche pāiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov lus ntawm Achyutananda Das yeej tsis muaj cuav. Leej twg hu cov lus no tias cuav yuav yeej koom nrog qhov kev txhaum ntawd.

Tus Mahapurush sau hauv 'Chakada Madana' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୋ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗାଇଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହଇ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mo shrīmukha bāṇī anyathā nohiba parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
Nemāla baṭaku laya lagāiṇa dīna Achyutī kahai.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, kuv cov lus yeej yuav tsis hloov, txawm yog paj rwb tawg paj rau saum roob los xij! Cov lus no tau sau thaum tab tom xav txog Vajtswv nyob hauv qab Nemal banyan ntoo (Nemal Vata).

Hauv phau ntawv Malika 'Chakada Madana' nws tus kheej, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau dua tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀଫାଟି ଜଳୁପଛେ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଅନଳ,
ଯାହା କହିଛନ୍ତି ତାହା ସର୍ବଦା ଅଚଳ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mahīphāṭi jalupache pralaya anala,
jāhā kahichaṃti tāhā sarbadā achala.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, hluav taws tawg tom qab ntiaj teb tawg yuav coj kev puas tsuaj (Pralaya) rau tag nrho ntiaj teb, tiam sis kuv cov lus yeej tsis muaj cuav. Kuv cov lus yeej tsis muaj cuav thiab hloov tsis tau.

2 Lub Hom Phiaj ntawm Malika – Kev Cawm Dim ntawm Cov Neeg Pe Hawm

Feem ntau, ntau tus neeg ntshai tom qab nyeem cov lus faj lem ntawm Malika thiab tso tseg lawv tias yog "kev ntshai." Txawm li cas los xij, qhov kev xav no tsis yog kiag li. Lub hom phiaj tseem ceeb yog kev tiv thaiv sab ntsuj plig thiab kev coj ntawm cov neeg pe hawm los ntawm kev hloov pauv ntawm lub sijhawm.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ହେତୁ ରସାଇବା ପାଇଁକି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶାହାସ୍ତ୍ର ପୁରାଣ କଲା,
କଳିକାଳଠାରୁ ବଳି କାଳ ଯହିଁ ହକ କଥାଟା ଲେଖିଲା।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Hetu rasāibā pāiṃki Achyuta shāhāstra purāṇa kalā,
kalikālaṭhāru bali kāla jahiṃ haka kathāṭā lekhilā.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, los tsim tsa kev nco qab uas tsaug zog ntawm cov neeg pe hawm, nws tab tom sau cov ntawv Malika no. Lub hom phiaj yog tias hauv Kaliyuga tob, thaum kev tsaus ntuj ntawm kev tsis paub txog Vajtswv nyob txhua qhov chaw, Tus Tswv cov neeg pe hawm tuaj yeem mus txog Mahaprabhu Kalki los ntawm Malika, tso cai rau kev sib koom ua ke ntawm tus neeg pe hawm thiab Vajtswv. Vim li no, nws tab tom sau cov lus faj lem loj hauv cov ntawv Malika los ntawm Kaliyuga mus rau Sangam Yuga (Lub Sijhawm Hloov Pauv) thiab ntxiv mus rau Ananta Yuga (Adi Satyayuga) uas yuav los.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau dua tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଅବନୀକୁ ଚେତେଇବା ପାଇଁ ଚେତାବନୀ,
ଅବନୀ ହିତେ ଲେଖା ଏ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Abanīku cheteibā pāiñ chetābanī,
Abanī hite lekhā e bhabishya bāṇī.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub hom phiaj ntawm cov lus faj lem uas kuv tau sau hauv cov ntawv Malika yog los ceeb toom rau ntiaj teb. Nws piav qhia tias cov lus faj lem no yog sau rau kev noj qab nyob zoo ntawm ntiaj teb.

Tus Mahapurush sau dua hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ପୂର୍ବର ବାସନା ମୂଳେ ଜ୍ଞାନ ଉଦୟ ହେଲା,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହିତରେ ମାଳିକା ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଲେଖି ରଖିଲା।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Pūrbara bāsanā mūḷe jñāna udaya helā,
Bhaktanka hitare Māḷikā grantha lekhi rakhilā.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Vim yog 'vasana' (kev nco) yav dhau los, txhais tau tias kev pe hawm Tus Tswv los ntawm kev yug dhau los, kev paub tau tshwm sim rau kuv hauv lub neej no, thiab tom qab ntawd kuv tau sau cov ntawv Malika rau kev pab thiab kev noj qab nyob zoo ntawm cov neeg pe hawm.

Tus Mahapurush sau dua hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ସତର୍କ ଘଣ୍ଟି ଯେ ଚେତାବନୀ ଏହି ଜାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa satarka ghaṇṭi je chetābanī ehi jāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Cov lus hauv cov ntawv Malika yog rau kev ceeb toom; paub tias lawv yog lus ceeb toom.

Mahapurush sau ntxiv tias:

Odia Sloka:

ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଏହି ଚେତାବନୀ ପୁଣ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ଭକ୍ତ ହିତେ ପୁଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Sāraswata grantha ehi chetābanī puṇa,
bāraṇa beḷe prakāsha bhakta hite puṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Cov nqe Malika yog lus ceeb toom rau tib neeg. Lawv tau sau los rau kev noj qab nyob zoo rau cov neeg pe hawm thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga. Yog li ntawd, peb yuav tsum nkag siab tias Malika tsis txhob tsim kev ntshai, tab sis qhib txoj kev cawm seej. Lub hom phiaj tseem ceeb ntawm Bhavishya Malika yog kev noj qab nyob zoo rau lub ntiaj teb.

2.1 Malika tsis yog rau txhua tus neeg

Peb yuav tsum paub zoo tias Malika tsis yog rau txhua tus. Cov Panchasakha Mahapurushas tau txwv lawv cov thwjtim hauv cov ntawv Malika kom tsis txhob tshaj tawm cov lus no rau cov neeg ruam. Mahapurush hais tias cov no tsis yog rau txhua tus; tsis yog txhua tus neeg yuav nkag siab tau. Tsuas yog cov neeg pe hawm tus Tswv thiaj yuav ntseeg tau.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ କଳିପୁଣି ଆଗତ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ,
ଭକତ ହିତରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲି ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଭଣି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Lakshe pañchāashī grantha kaḷi puṇi āgata bhabishyabāṇī,
Bhakata hitare prakāsha karili dīna Achyuta je bhaṇi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Kuv tau sau tag nrho 185,000 phau ntawv thiab dhau ntawd, sau ntau yam lus faj seeb yav tom ntej. Kuv tab tom tshaj tawm tag nrho cov no rau cov neeg pe hawm tau txais txiaj ntsig.

Achyutananda Das ces hais rau nws tus thwjtim Ramadas hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଏ ପରମତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ମୂର୍ଖକୁ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ତତେ ସପ୍ତବାର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି କହଇ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ତିନି ଅଠର ମାଳିକା କଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ରଖିଛି ଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଜନଙ୍କ ପୀୟୂଷ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra e paramatattwa mūrkhaku na kara prakāsha,
tate saptabāra niyama deuchi kahai dīna Achyuta.
Lakshe pañchāashī tini aṭhara mālikā kali prakāsha,
bāraṇa beḷaku e grantha rakhichi jñānī janaṅka pīyūṣa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mloog, tus thwjtim Ramadas, cov no yog lus zais. Tsis txhob qhia rau cov neeg ruam, txwv tsis pub koj yuav ua txhaum. Cov ntawv no yog lus ceeb toom rau tib neeg uas yuav zoo li dej cawv rau cov neeg pe hawm thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ହୋଇବ ଡାକିନୀ ଗୋଳ ପଡ଼ିବ ଚହଳ,
ଅବନୀ କମ୍ପିବ ସଦା ହୋଇ ଟଳଟଳ।
ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ମାନ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Hoiba ḍākinī goḷa paḍiba chahaḷa,
Abanī kampiba sadā hoi taḷataḷa.
Nānā utpāta māna antarikṣe heba,
Bhakta binā anya kehi jāṇi na pāriba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, thaum kev tsim tsa Dharma, yuav muaj kev ua tsov rog phem heev, thiab yuav muaj kev kub ntxhov hauv ntiaj teb. Lub ntiaj teb yuav tshee hnyo. Yuav muaj ntau yam kev cuam tshuam hauv huab cua. Tab sis tsuas yog cov neeg pe hawm xwb, tsis muaj leej twg yuav nkag siab tau tias cov no yog tus Tswv txoj kev ua si uas rhuav tshem.

Mahapurush hais rau tus thwjtim Ramadas hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାବୁରାମ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ କହିବୁ ନାହିଁ,
ଘୋର କଳିକାଳ ଟାହି ଯେ ଟାପରା ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା ହେବ ତହିଁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shuṇa Bāburāma Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhanku kahibu nāhiñ,
Ghora Kaḷikāḷa ṭāhi je ṭāparā bānā nindā heba tahiñ.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Au tus thwjtim Ramadas, tsis txhob qhia cov lus hauv phau ntawv ‘Chakada Madana’ no rau cov neeg ruam. Hauv Kaliyuga tob, cov neeg ruam yuav thuam lawv, uas yuav ua rau tus Tswv raug thuam.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau dua hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇଟି ବିଷ ଜ୍ଞାନୀଙ୍କୁ ଅଟେ ପିୟୂଷ,
ଅଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲେ ମୋ ଅମୃତେ ହେବ ବିଷ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mūrkhanku e grantha aṭaiṭi biṣa jñānīnku aṭe piyūṣa,
Ajñānī āgare prakāsha karile mo amṛute heba biṣa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Rau cov neeg ruam, cov lus Malika no zoo li tshuaj lom, hos rau cov neeg txawj ntse lawv zoo li 'Piyush' lossis dej cawv. Qhia cov lus no rau cov neeg tsis paub yog zoo li sib tov tshuaj lom rau hauv dej cawv.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau ib zaug ntxiv hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କହୁଛି ଏହି ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଅଟଇ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଏହା ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ଖେଳ ହୋଇବ ତୋତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଗଣ୍ଠିଆ ଭକତ ଅଟଇ ଗଣ୍ଠି କଥାରେ ମନ,
ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ ପୁରାଣ।
ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ହୋଇବ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକତ,
ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଦାସରେ ମୋର ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra kahuchi ehi niṣṭhā bachana,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa aṭai bhaktaṅka gaṇṭhidhana.
Bāraṇa beḷaku rakhichi ehā bhaktaṅka pāiṃ,
Gupatare kheḷa hoiba tote deli chetei.
Gaṇṭhiā bhakata aṭai gaṇṭhi kathāre mana,
Sātasha bāstarī parārddha bhabishyata purāṇa.
Dharma saṃsthāpana hoiba bhakta hebe mukata,
Shuṇa Rāmachandra dāsare mora bachana satya.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mloog tus thwjtim Ramadas, cov lus Malika no yog zoo li 'Ganthidhana' lossis kev nplua nuj muaj nuj nqis rau cov neeg pe hawm. Tus Tswv yuav tsim tsa Dharma thiab cov neeg pe hawm yuav dim. Cov kev ua si no yuav tshwm sim zais cia heev. Tsuas yog rau kev cawm seej ntawm cov neeg pe hawm kuv thiaj sau cov ntawv Malika no. Lawv yuav muaj txiaj ntsig rau lawv thaum ‘Barana Bela’ lossis kawg ntawm Kaliyuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau ntxiv hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗେଇ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଖେଦା ଲେଖଇ।
ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇ ଏହି,
ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ।
ଉତ୍ତମ ଶିଷ୍ୟରେ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Nemāḷa baṭaku laya lagei,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa khedā lekhai.
Sāraswata grantha aṭai ehi,
Beḷaku rakhichi bhaktaṅka pāiñ.
Uttama shiṣyare kara prakāsha,
Bānā nindā kahe Achyuta Dāsa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hauv qab tsob ntoo Banyan ntawm Nemalo, Kuv tab tom sau phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ no. Nws yuav muaj txiaj ntsig rau cov neeg pe hawm thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga. Tsuas qhia rau cov neeg pe hawm zoo tshaj plaws xwb; txwv tsis pub, yuav muaj kev thuam cov lus no thiab tus Tswv.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau ib zaug ntxiv hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗି ଅଲଗାରେ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଭକ୍ତ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ ଜାଣ,
ଭକତ ଜାଣିବେ ଏହାର ଯେ ଭେଦ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଅଛି ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ପ୍ରକାଶ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟ ଯେ ସାଧୁ ପଦାଶ୍ରିତ ଭଣେ ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Lāgi alagāre Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa bhakta gaṇṭhidhana jāṇa,
bhakata jāṇibe ehāra je bheda shūnyare achi pramāṇa.
Bāraṇa beḷare e grantha prakāsha mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
Nemāḷa baṭa je sādhu padāshrita bhaṇe pāmara Achyuta.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Chakada Madana yog kev nplua nuj rau cov neeg pe hawm. Thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, tsuas yog cov neeg pe hawm thiaj yuav nkag siab txog nws cov ntsiab lus thiab cov lus zais. Tsis txhob qhia rau cov neeg ruam.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଚେତୁଆ ଭକତମାନଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ତିନି ଅଠର ଖଣ୍ଡ,
ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଲେଖିଲା ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଦୁରୁଦଣ୍ଡ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chetuā bhaktamānaṅka nimante tini aṭhara khaṇḍa,
Bhabishya lekhilā pāmara Achyuta na jāṇanti durudaṇḍa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush tau sau 318 phau ntawv Malika lus faj seeb tsuas yog rau kev noj qab nyob zoo rau cov neeg pe hawm uas tau sawv. Cov neeg ruam thiab cov neeg txhaum yuav tsis paub txog cov lus zais hauv cov ntawv no.

Nws sau dua tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ବିନାଶି ପ୍ରଭୁ ମେଦିନୀ ରଖିବେ,
ସନାତନ ଧର୍ମ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପ୍ରଚାର କରିବେ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନରହରି,
ନାମ ଭଜଏ ଘୋର କଳିରେ ଯିବ ତରି।
ବିପଦ କାଳରେ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ରକ୍ଷା ପାଇଁ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ରଚଇ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mlechha bināshi Prabhu medinī rakhibe,
Sanātana Dharma nishche prachāra karibe.
Kaḷiyuga sheshare prakāsha Narahari,
Nāma bhajae ghora kaḷire jiba tari.
Bipada kāḷare bhaktaṅka rakṣā pāiñ,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa grantha Achyuta rachai.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, tus Tswv yuav los yug. Nws yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab cov neeg txhaum los tiv thaiv lub ntiaj teb thiab yuav rov tsim tsa Sanatana Dharma. Mahapurush sau tias thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, rau kev coj cov neeg pe hawm thiab rau lawv txoj kev tiv thaiv, nws tab tom sau cov ntawv Malika. Hauv nqe no, Mahapurush tau sau meej meej tias lub hom phiaj ntawm kev sau Malika yog los tiv thaiv cov neeg pe hawm thaum muaj kev kub ntxhov thiab qhib txoj kev cawm seej rau lawv.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau ntxiv hauv nws cov ntawv Malika, 'Chakada Madana' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ପାପ ନାଶନ,
ଖେଦା ଚେତାବନୀ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଜ୍ଞାନ।
ସବୁ ଲେଖିଗଲି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଭକ୍ତ ମୋହର ଯେ ସୋଦର ଭାଇ।
ଭକତ ମୋ ପଞ୍ଚ ପ୍ରାଣଟି ଜାଣ,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ଖେଦା ଲିଖନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dharma saṃsthāpana pāpa nāshana,
khedā chetābanī bhabishya jñāna.
Sabu lekhigali bhaktanka pāiṃ,
bhakta mohara je sodara bhāī.
Bhakata mo pañcha prāṇaṭi jāṇa,
bhaktanka nimaṃte khedā likhana.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov lus faj seeb kuv tau sau hauv kuv cov ntawv Malika txog kev xaus kev txhaum thiab kev tsim tsa Dharma thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, yog tag nrho rau cov neeg pe hawm vim cov neeg pe hawm zoo li kuv cov kwv tij. Cov neeg pe hawm yog kuv tsib txoj sia (Pancha-prana), thiab kuv tau sau tag nrho cov ntawv Malika no tsuas yog rau lawv xwb.

Mahapurush sau dua tias:

Odia Sloka:

ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଅଟଇ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଜାଣ,
ଘୋର କଳିଯୁଗ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନ ଛନ୍ତି ଡର ଲାଗୁଅଛି ପୁଣ।
ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନ କର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି ମୁହିଁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Sātasha bāstarī parārddha aṭai Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa jāṇa,
ghora kaliyuga duṣhṭajana chaṃti ḍara lāguachi puṇa.
Mūrkhanka āgare prakāsha na kara niyama deuchi muṃhi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tus thwjtim Rama, muaj 772 Parardhas lossis tshooj hauv phau ntawv Malika no hu ua 'Chakada Madana'. Tsis txhob qhia rau cov neeg ruam. Muaj ntau tus neeg phem hauv Kali Yuga phem heev; khaws nws kom deb ntawm lawv txhua tus.

Hauv 'Chakada Madana' nws tus kheej, Mahapurush sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନୋହିଲା ନୋହିଲା ପଡୁଥିବ ହୁରି ଭକ୍ତ ଜାଣିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shuṇa Rāmadāsa Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
nohilā nohilā paḍuthiba huri bhakta jāṇiba abashya.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Au tus thwjtim Ramadasa, tsis txhob qhia cov lus faj seeb Malika no rau cov neeg ruam. Lawv yuav hais tias cov lus no yog lus cuav, tias cov xwm txheej no yuav tsis tshwm sim. Yog li ntawd, tsuas qhia rau cov neeg pe hawm xwb; tsuas yog lawv thiaj yuav ntseeg tau qhov no.

3 Malika Cov Lus Faj Lem Uas Tau Muaj Tseeb Lawm

Bhavishya Malika muaj cov lus ntawm Mahaprabhu nws tus kheej, thiab yog li ntawd nws tsis yog qhov xav tsis thoob tias ntau yam lus faj seeb hauv Malika tau muaj tseeb txog tam sim no. Peb yuav tham txog qee yam lus faj seeb zoo li no nrog koj uas tau ua pov thawj tias muaj tseeb 100%.

3.1 Lus Faj Seeb Txog Atal Bihari Vajpayee Ji

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika 'Kalikalpa Gita' thiab 'Achyuta Thara' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ ଯେବେ, କଳ୍କୀଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା ଲାଗିବ ତେବେ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ରାଜା, ଫେରିବ କିଛିତାହାର ତରିଜା।
କିଛି ଦିନ ଭୋଗ କରିବ ମହୀ, ତେର ଦିନ ସେହି ଛତ୍ର ଉଡ଼ାଇ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇଲେ ଯେବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଯେ ରାଜା, ତାଙ୍କ ସମୟରେ କିଛି ହୋଇଲା ତରିଜା।
ତେରଦିନ ଛତ୍ର ସେହି ଉଡ଼େଇଣ ଦେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ମହୀ ଭୋଗ କଲେ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
ପ୍ରଥମେ ତେର ଦିନ ପ୍ରଧାନ ମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ହେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ଶାସନ କରିଲେ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ ଶକତି କାହାର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Tera dina pakṣa hoiba jebe, Kaḷkīṅka līḷā lāgiba tebe.
Khetratre hoiba Brāhmaṇa rājā, pheriba kichhitāhāra tarijā.
Kichhi dina bhoga kariba mahī, tera dina sehi chhatra uḍāi.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Khetratre hoile jebe Brāhmaṇa je rājā, tāṅka samayare kichhi hoilā tarijā.
Teradina chhatra sehi uḍeiṇa dele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa mahī bhoga kale.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Prathame tera dina pradhāna mantrī hele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa shāsana karile.
Achyuta bachana aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ shakati kāhāra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Ntau pua xyoo dhau los, Mahapurush tau sau txog ib tus nom tswv tseem ceeb ntawm Is Nrias teb ywj pheej. Nws hais tias thaum ib tug Brahmin kav Is Nrias teb, yuav muaj ntau yam kev hloov pauv tshwm sim hauv ntiaj teb. Ua ntej, nws yuav kav tau 13 hnub, thiab tom qab ntawd nws yuav tuav hwj chim tau tsib xyoos. Mahapurush sau tias tus neeg no uas kav los ntawm Delhi yuav hu ua "Atal Bihari Vajpayee". Raws li keeb kwm qhia, Shri Vajpayee tau ua Tus Thawj Fwm Tsav Tebchaws tau 13 hnub hauv xyoo 1996 thiab tom qab ntawd tau ua haujlwm tag nrho 5 xyoos txij xyoo 1999 txog 2004.

3.2 Lus Faj Seeb Txog Mahatma Gandhi

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, 600 xyoo dhau los hauv nws cov ntawv nto moo 'Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta', tau ua ib qho lus faj seeb ntev txog Mahatma Gandhi.

Odia Sloka:

ଜନମ ହୋଇ ଗୁଜରାଟରେ, ମୋହନ ମୋହିବ ରାମ ନାମରେ।
ଅହିଂସାକୁ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର କରିବ ଜାଣ, ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ ହଟିବେ ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣ।
ତା ଅନ୍ତେ ଧର୍ମ ହୋଇବ ହାଣି, ମୋହନ ରାଜୁତି ହୋଇବ ହାଣି।
ଗୁଳି ଚୋଟେ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଯିବ, ଗଡସେ କଳିମଞ୍ଜି ପୋତିବ।
ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବେ ସଂସାର ପ୍ରାଣୀ, ଟେକି କହୁଥିବେ କଥା ନ ଜାଣି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Janama hoi Gujarāṭare, Mohana mohiba Rāma nāmare.
Ahinsāku astra kariba jāṇa, phiriṅgī haṭibe se kāḷe jāṇa.
Tā ante dharma hoiba hāṇi, Mohana rājuti hoiba hāṇi.
Guḷi choṭe tāra prāṇa jiba, Gaḍse kaḷimañji potiba.
Jāṇi na pāribe saṃsāra prāṇī, ṭeki kahuthibe kathā na jāṇi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Nws yuav yug hauv lub xeev Gujarat. Nws lub npe yuav yog 'Mohan' thiab nws yuav hu nkauj Rama lub npe tas li. 'Ahimsa' (Kev tsis ua phem) yuav yog nws rab riam phom, thiab vim nws txoj kev siv zog, cov British (Firingis) yuav raug yuam kom tawm hauv Is Nrias teb."

Tus Mahapurush tau sau ntxiv tias Mohan yuav tsis tuav hwj chim nws tus kheej, thiab tom qab nws tuag, Dharma yuav poob qis. Nws tau hais meej tias ib tug neeg hu ua "Godse" yuav tua nws nrog "mos txwv" (Guli), thiab ua li ntawd, yuav cog cov noob Kali rau hauv lub tebchaws. Nws txawm tau kwv yees tias ntau tus neeg yuav tsis nkag siab Gandhi kom raug thiab yuav txhawb tus neeg tua neeg qhov kev ua los ntawm kev tsis paub. Raws li peb paub, txhua lo lus ntawm qhov kev tshaj tawm no txog Leej Txiv ntawm Lub Tebchaws tau muaj tseeb.

3.3 Txoj Kev Tshaj Tawm Txog Xyoo 1999 Cua Daj Cua Dub Loj Heev thiab Kev Tawg ntawm Kalpavata

Hauv nws phau ntawv 'Jai Birachit Chautisha', Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau kwv yees txog xyoo 1999 cua daj cua dub 600 xyoo ua ntej.

Odia Sloka:

ସମୁଦ୍ର ଉଠି ଆସିବ ବତାସ ବହିବ, ଶ୍ରୀ କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଭାଙ୍ଗି ପକାଇବ।
ସତ୍ୟଭାବ ହେ, ସାଲ ଅନେଶତ କର ଠାବ ହେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Samudra uṭhi āsiba batāsa bahiba, Shrī Kaḷpabaṭa mora bhāṅgi pakāiba.
Satyabhāba he, sāla aneshata kara ṭhāba he.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Hiav txwv yuav nce thiab cua daj cua dub yuav tshuab. Ceg ntoo ntawm Kalpavata dawb huv (tsob ntoo Banyan qub nyob hauv Jagannath Temple) yuav tawg." Tus Mahapurush tau hais tias qhov xwm txheej no yuav tshwm sim rau xyoo "Anesata" (99).

Thaum Lub Kaum Hli 29, 1999, ib qho Cua Daj Cua Dub Loj Heev tau tsoo Odisha nrog cua nrawm 250 km/h. Nws ua rau muaj kev puas tsuaj loj heev, tua ntau tshaj 10,000 tus neeg. Raws li qhov kev tshaj tawm, ib ceg ntoo loj ntawm tsob ntoo Kalpavata qub nyob hauv Puri temple tau tawg thaum lub sijhawm cua daj cua dub no.

3.4 Txoj Kev Tshaj Tawm Txog Kev Ywj Pheej ntawm Is Nrias Teb (1947)

Hauv phau ntawv 'Garuda Kalpa', Saint Achyutananda tau kwv yees tias Is Nrias teb yuav dim ntawm txoj kev tswj hwm Askiv rau xyoo 1947 siv tus lej zais.

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ଭକ୍ତି ବେଦ ବାରକୁ ଥୋଇ, ଏ ଠାରେ ବିଦେଶୀ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Brahma bhakti beda bāraku thoi, e ṭhāre bideshī jibe paḷāi.

Kev Txhais Tus Lej Zais:
Tus Mahapurush siv cov lus cim rau cov lej:
  • Brahma: Sawv cev los ntawm 1 (Raws li hauv "Ekam Brahma").
  • Bhakti: Sawv cev los ntawm 9 (Cuaj hom Navadha Bhakti).
  • Veda: Sawv cev los ntawm 4 (Plaub Vedas).
  • Baar: Sawv cev los ntawm 7 (Xya hnub ntawm lub lim tiam).

Thaum muab tso ua ke (1-9-4-7), nws tsim 1947. Qhov kev tshaj tawm hais tias rau xyoo no, cov neeg txawv tebchaws (Askiv) yuav khiav tawm ntawm thaj av no.

3.5 Txoj Kev Tshaj Tawm Txog Kev Rho Menyuam Ntxhais

Hauv 'Jaiphula Malika', tus Mahapurush tau kwv yees txog kev phem kev qias hauv zej zog thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga:

Odia Sloka:

କନ୍ୟା ରତ୍ନକୁ ଗର୍ଭର, ନଷ୍ଟ କରିବ ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ହୃଦୟେ ନ କରି ପାପ ବିଚାର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kanyā ratnaku garbhara, naṣṭa kariba tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
hṛudaye na kari pāpa bichāra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Cov neeg yuav rhuav tshem tus menyuam ntxhais (Kanya Ratna) hauv plab (Garbha) rov ua dua yam tsis muaj kev txhaum hauv lawv lub siab." Qhov no qhia tau meej txog lub sijhawm tsaus ntuj ntawm kev rho menyuam ntxhais uas tau ua rau zej zog raug kev txom nyem nyob rau ob peb xyoo dhau los.

3.6 Txoj Kev Tshaj Tawm Txog Xyoo 2020 Kev Sib Kis Thoob Ntiaj Teb (COVID-19)

Mahapurush Hadidas, ib qho kev rov qab los yug ntawm Panchasakhas yug rau xyoo pua 18, tau sau hauv 'Agata Bhavishyat Chautisha':

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ବିଂଶ ଯେବେ ବର୍ଷ ବୋଲାଇବ, ବହୁ ବିତିପାତ ହେବ।
ଅଜଣା ବ୍ୟାଧିରେ ବହୁ ନାଶ ଯିବେ, ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dui biṃsha jebe barsha bolāiba, bahu bitipāta heba.
Ajaṇā byādhire bahu nāsha jibe, hāhākāra paḍijiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Thaum xyoo 'Dui-Binsha' (Neeg Ob Caug / 2020) los txog, yuav muaj kev kub ntxhov loj. Cov neeg yuav tuag los ntawm kab mob tsis paub (Ajana Vyadhi) thiab yuav muaj suab quaj ntsuag (Haahakaar) nyob txhua qhov chaw." Qhov no yog ib qho kev piav qhia tseeb heev txog COVID-19 kev sib kis thoob ntiaj teb uas tau ua rau ntiaj teb raug kev txom nyem rau xyoo 2020.

3.7 Txoj Kev Tshaj Tawm Txog Hirakud Dam

Hauv phau ntawv 'Chakada Madana' thiab 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa', Saints Achyutananda thiab Arkhit Das tau kwv yees txog kev tsim ib lub pas dej loj heev hauv Odisha.

Odia Sloka:

ଛବିଶ ଠାବରେ ହୀରାକୁଦ ଉଛୁଳିବ, ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଅର୍ଦ୍ଧଭାଗ ଜଳମଗ୍ନ ହେବ।
ଧନଜନ ନଷ୍ଟ ହେବ କହିଲି ବୁଝାଇ, ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ବେଶୀ ବେଳ ନାହିଁ।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲିଣଟି ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକାକାର ହେବ।
ସେହି ବେଳେ ଗଙ୍ଗା ପୁଣି ଆସିବେଟି ମାଡ଼ି, ବୈତରଣୀ, ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣୀ ଯେ ଦେବେ ଘୋର ରଡ଼ି।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ବଡ଼ ବନ୍ଧ ତିଆରି ଯେ ଥିବ, ସେଇବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁଦ୍ୱାରା ଭାଙ୍ଗିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ଛଅ ଜିଲ୍ଲା ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇ, ଯିବାପାଇଁ ବାଟ ନ ପାଇବେ ପୁଣି କେହି।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲି ଗ୍ରାମର ଯେ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chabiṣha ṭhābare Hīrākuda uchuḷiba, e Oḍiśhā arddhabhāga jaḷamagna heba.
Dhanajana naṣhṭa heba kahili bujhāi, dīna Achyuta kahai beśhī beḷa nāhiñ.
Hīrākuda boliṇaṭi grāma nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu ekākāra heba.
Sehi beḷe Gaṅgā puṇi āsibeṭi māḍi, Baitaraṇī, Brāhmaṇī je debe ghora raḍi.
Oḍiśhāre baḍa bandha tiāri je thiba, seibeḷe śhatrudwārā bhāṅgiṇa je jiba.
Oḍiśhāre chha-a jillā jaḷārṇnaba hoi, jibāpāiñ bāṭa na pāibe puṇi kehi.
Hīrākuda boli grāmara je nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu eka je hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Achyutananda Das ji hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Chakada Madana' thiab Brahma Avadhut saint Arakhita Das, uas yug rau ib nrab xyoo pua kaum yim thiab tau ua tiav sadhana hauv Olashuni cave, hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' tau sau tias yuav muaj ib lub pas dej loj tsim hauv xeev Odisha uas nws lub npe yuav yog "Hirakud". Nws tau hais ntxiv tias thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, los ntawm kev tawm tsam ntawm yeeb ncuab, lub pas dej ntawd yuav tawg, los ntawm qhov ntawd rau lub nroog ntawm xeev Odisha yuav raug dej nyab, kev thauj mus los yuav nres, thiab yuav muaj kev poob nyiaj txiag thiab tib neeg loj heev. Cov Mahapurushas tau hais tias thaum lub sijhawm ntawd Ganga yuav qhia nws daim duab hnyav thiab Baitarani, Brahmani, thiab lwm yam dej ntws kuj yuav ua rau muaj kev puas tsuaj loj heev.

Raws li paub, rau xyoo 1948 hauv xeev Odisha hauv Is Nrias teb, kev tsim kho lub pas dej hu ua Hirakud tau pib, uas tau ua tiav rau xyoo 1953. Thaum Lub Ib Hlis Ntuj xyoo 1957, los ntawm tus Thawj Fwm Tsav Tebchaws Pandit Jawaharlal Nehru, kev qhib lub pas dej no tau ua tiav. Nrog rau tag nrho qhov ntev 26 km, tsim hla Mahanadi river hauv Sambalpur district ntawm Odisha, lub pas dej no niaj hnub no yog lub pas dej ua los ntawm tib neeg ntev tshaj plaws hauv ntiaj teb. Ua li no, qhov tseeb ntawm thawj feem ntawm qhov kev tshaj tawm no tau raug pov thawj.

Tsis ntev ntiaj teb yuav pom qhov thib ob ntawm qhov kev tshaj tawm ntawd kuj yuav muaj tseeb thaum, thaum lub sijhawm kev ua tsov rog Is Nrias teb-Suav, los ntawm Suav lub foob pob hluav taws tawm tsam, lub pas dej no yuav tawg thiab xeev Odisha yuav raug dej nyab loj heev.

3.8 Txoj Kev Tshaj Tawm Txog Pakistan Yug Los Ntau Pua Xyoo Ua Ntej

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das thiab lwm tus tau hais txog kev tsim thiab lub npe ntawm Pakistan 600 xyoo dhau los hauv cov ntawv xws li 'Gupta Kheda Malika' thiab 'Bhavishyat Malika'. Ib yam li ntawd, xyoo pua 18 saint Arkhit Das tau hais txog nws hauv 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ପାକିସ୍ତାନର ଦଶା ଯାହା ହେବ ସହସା, ମନ କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦଇ ଥରେ ଶୁଣ ସେ ଭାଷା।
ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ତାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ, ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପ୍ରାଚ୍ୟ ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ଗଣ ଏହିପରି ମିଳିଣ, ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ଲାଗି ସର୍ବେ କରିବେ ରଣ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟଗୋଟି ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ହାହାକାର ଶୁଭୁଣ ଯେ ଥିବ।
ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ, ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟ ଗୋଟା ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ପଛ ବେଳକୁ ତ ପୁଣି ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଯେ ସ୍ଫୁରିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Pākistānara dashā jāhā heba sahasā, mana karṇṇa dei thare shuṇa se bhāṣā.
Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākistānaṭi raṅge, mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Prāchya pāshchātya gaṇa ehipari miḷiṇa, Pākistāna lāgi sarbe karibe raṇa.
Pākistāna rājyagoṭi chhārakhāra heba, chāriāḍe hāhākāra shubhuṇa je thiba.
Chīna Pākistāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa, Ruṣiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmana Jāpāna.
Pākistāna rājya goṭā chhārakhāra heba, pachha beḷaku ta puṇi buddhi je sphuriba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Ib lub tebchaws hu ua Pakistan yuav tshwm sim sai sai. Thaum kawg ntawm lub sijhawm, Is Nrias teb yuav ntsib kev ua tsov rog hnyav nrog Pakistan. Cov tebchaws Middle East thiab Western yuav koom tes; cov tebchaws Muslim, cov tebchaws Western, Suav, thiab Amelikas yuav txhawb Pakistan, thaum Lavxias, Lub Tebchaws Yelemees, thiab Nyij Pooj yuav pab Is Nrias teb. Pakistan yuav raug rhuav tshem tag nrho (Chara-khara) hauv kev ua tsov rog no, thiab nyob rau hauv kev poob siab thoob ntiaj teb, nws yuav thaum kawg paub nws qhov yuam kev."

Nws tsim nyog sau tseg tias lo lus 'Pakistan' tsuas yog tsim tawm rau xyoo 1933, tab sis cov neeg dawb huv no tau hu nws lub npe ntau pua xyoo ua ntej.

3.9 Cov Kev Tshaj Tawm Txog Lub Nroog Bhubaneswar

Lub hauv paus ntawm Bhubaneswar niaj hnub no tau tsim rau xyoo 1948 los ntawm PM Nehru. Txawm li cas los xij, nws tau hais tias yog lub peev xwm tshwm sim hauv cov ntawv Malika xws li 'Chakada Madana' thiab 'Kali Agata Bhabishyant'.

Odia Sloka:

ଭୈରବୀ ଡାକିବ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର, ଚୈତ୍ର ମାସ ଯେ ମଙ୍ଗଳବାର।
ପୃଥିବୀରେ ଗୋଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଆରମ୍ଭିବ ଯେବେ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସୈନ୍ୟ ମାଡ଼ି ଯେ ଆସିବେ।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ, ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ତ ପୁଣି ରାଜଧାନୀ ଜାଣ, ବିଦେଶୀ ଶତ୍ରୁଙ୍କ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି ପଡ଼ିବଟି ପୁଣ।
ଭୈରବ ଡାକ ହେବ ଜାଣ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରଟି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhairabī ḍākiba Bhubaneshwar, Chaitra māsa je Maṅgaḷabāra.
Pṛuthibīre goḷa yuddha ārambhiba jebe, Bhubaneshwaraku sainya māḍi je āsibe.
Bhubaneshwarare lāgiba goḷa, Ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Bhubaneshwar ta puṇi rājadhānī jāṇa, bideshī shatruṅka dṛuṣṭi paḍibaṭi puṇa.
Bhairaba ḍāka heba jāṇa, Bhubaneshwarati pramāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Cov neeg dawb huv tau kwv yees tias rau hnub Tuesday hauv lub hlis Chaitra, yuav hnov lub suab nrov nrov (Bhairavi lub suab nrov) hauv Bhubaneswar. Thaum Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb Zaum Peb, cov tub rog yeeb ncuab yuav mus txog Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Bana). Cov ntawv tau hais meej tias Bhubaneswar yuav yog lub peev xwm thiab yog lub hom phiaj rau cov yeeb ncuab txawv tebchaws.

3.10 Txoj Kev Tshaj Tawm Txog Tus Xib Hwb Ntsuj Plig Muaj Npe Nrov Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, hauv nws cov ntawv Malika sau ze li 600 xyoo dhau los, tau kwv yees txog kev yug los ntawm "Yugapurusha" (Tus Txiv Neej ntawm Lub Sijhawm) uas yuav ua ntau yam kev ua si dawb huv thiab nws lub koob meej yuav nthuav dav thoob ntiaj teb.

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳାମାନ ହେବ କୀର୍ତ୍ତି ରହି ଯିବ, ଅନୁକୂଳ ନାମ ଧରି।
ଦେଶ ବିଦେଶରୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିବେ, ସତସଙ୍ଗ ଆଶ୍ରା କରି।
ସତନାମ ଗୋଟି ଉଚ୍ଚାରି ବୋଲିବେ, ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହେବ ମେଳ।
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ମାର୍ଗେ ରହି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦେଖିବ, ଯୁଗପୁରୁଷଙ୍କ ଖେଳ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Līḷāmāna heba kīrtti rahi jiba, Anukūḷa nāma dhari.
Desha bidesharu bhakata āsibe, sat-saṅga āshrā kari.
Satanāma goṭi uchchāri bolibe, bhaktaṅka heba meḷa.
Shūnya mārge rahi Achyuta dekhiba, Yugapuruṣaṅka kheḷa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus Mahapurush tau sau tias ib tug neeg dawb huv hu ua "Anukul" yuav tshwm sim, thiab cov neeg pe hawm los ntawm Is Nrias teb thiab txawv tebchaws yuav tuaj nrhiav chaw nyob hauv nws "Satsanga." Lub npe dawb huv (Satnaam) yuav raug hu nkauj, thiab yuav muaj kev sib sau ua ke ntawm cov neeg pe hawm loj heev. Achyutananda Das tau hais ntxiv tias nws tus kheej, nyob hauv lub ntiaj teb tsis pom/khoob (Shunya Maarga), yuav pom kev ua si dawb huv ntawm tus Yugapurusha no.

Ua raws li qhov kev qhia ua ntej no, Anukulchandra Chakravarty yug rau lub Cuaj Hlis 14, 1888, hauv cheeb tsam Pabna ntawm Bengal uas tsis tau muab faib (tam sim no yog Bangladesh). Nws yog tus paub thoob ntiaj teb hu ua Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra, nws tau tsim lub 'Satsang Ashram' hauv Deoghar, Jharkhand, xyoo 1946. Ntau lab tus neeg raws nws qab thoob ntiaj teb tau koom nrog nws txoj haujlwm, feem ntau hu nws ua 'Yuga Purushottam.'

3.11 Kev hais txog Lub Foob Pob Atom hauv Malika Cov Ntawv

Thaum lub foob pob atom tau tsim nyob rau nruab nrab xyoo pua 20, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das (hauv 'Gupta Kheda Malika' thiab 'Bhavishyat Malika') thiab Balarama Das (hauv 'Kali Tandaba Gita') tau hais txog nws ntau pua xyoo dhau los.

Odia Sloka:

ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା କରିଦେବେଟି ଜମା, ପୋଡ଼ିଜାଳି ଦେବା ପାଇଁ ଭାରତ ସୀମା।
ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା ଯାହା ଲାଗି ଗାରିମା, ଦେଖାଇ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ଆଜି ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ସେନା।
ତାହା ଫୁଟିବ ନାହିଁ କେଣେ ଯିବ ମିଳାଇ, ଏହା ଦେଖି ବିଦେଶୀଏ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।
ଅଗ୍ନିର ଦାହିକା ଶକ୍ତି ଲୋ ବଉଳ ପ୍ରଭୁ କରିବେ ହରଣ, ପରମାଣୁ ବୋମା ଯାହାଲାଗି ଗାରିମା ଶୀତଳ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Paramāṇu je bomā karidebeṭi jamā, poḍijāḷi debā pāiñ Bhārata sīmā.
Paramāṇu je bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā, dekhāi huanti āji pāshchātya senā.
Tāhā phuṭiba nāhiñ keṇe jiba miḷāi, ehā dekhi bideshīe jibe paḷāi.
Agnira dāhikā shakti lo bauḷa Prabhu karibe haraṇa, paramāṇu bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā shītaḷa hoiba jāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Cov neeg dawb huv tau sau tias thaum Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb Thib Peb, Is Nrias teb cov yeeb ncuab yuav npaj yuav rhuav tshem Is Nrias teb cov ciam teb siv Foob Pob Atom (Paramanu Bomba). Txawm li cas los xij, lawv txoj kev npaj yuav ua tsis tiav vim Vajtswv Kalki yuav nqus tau lub zog hlawv/rhuav tshem (Dahika Shakti) ntawm cov foob pob no. Cov riam phom uas cov tub rog Western thiab yeeb ncuab khav theeb yuav dhau los ua "txias" (tsis muaj zog) thiab tsis muaj qhov cuam tshuam rau Is Nrias teb thaj av. Pom qhov kev ua tsis yooj yim no, cov yeeb ncuab txawv teb chaws yuav khiav tawm hauv kev ntshai. Cov ntawv ceeb toom tias yuav muaj kev ua tsov rog nuclear txaus ntshai (Anu-Paramanu Yuddha) thaum kawg tshwm sim, ua rau lub ntiaj teb yuav luag tsis muaj neeg nyob, uas cov neeg pe hawm yuav pom.

3.12 Kev hais txog Madan Mohan Malaviya, Mahatma Gandhi, thiab Motilal Nehru

Yug xyoo 1850, tus neeg dawb huv loj Bhima Bhoi (tus raws li Panchasakhas kev lig kev cai) tau muab cov lus qhia txog cov thawj coj ntawm Is Nrias teb txoj kev ywj pheej hauv nws cov ntawv 'Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ମଦନମାଲବ୍ୟ ନାମ ବୋଲିକରି ଏକ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବ ଜନ୍ମି,
ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ସହିତରେ ଅହିଂସା ଧର୍ମରେ ନିରନ୍ତରେ ଥିବେ ପୁଣି।
ଚୁଡ଼ଙ୍ଗ ବଂଶରେ ଉତପତ୍ତି ହୋଇ ମୋତିଲାଲ ଯେ ନେହେରୁ,
ମହାପ୍ରତାପୀ ଅଟଇ ସେହୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିଛନ୍ତି ଶୂନ୍ୟପୁରୁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Madanamālabya nāma bolikari eka bhakta thiba janmi,
Gāndhī sahitare ahinsā dharmare nirantare thibe puṇi.
Chuḍaṅga baṃshare utpatti hoi Motilāl je Neheru,
mahāpratāpī aṭai sehu bhakta āsichanti Shūnyapuru.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Cov ntawv tau qhia ua ntej tias yuav yug ib tug neeg pe hawm hu ua "Madan Mohan Malaviya" uas, nrog rau "Mahatma Gandhi," yuav coj ib txoj kev tawm tsam raws li kev ntseeg ntawm kev tsis ua phem (Ahimsa). Tsis tas li ntawd, nws tau hais txog kev yug ntawm tus neeg pe hawm muaj hwj chim loj "Motilal Nehru." Tag nrho peb tus tau dhau los ua cov thawj coj tseem ceeb ntawm Indian National Congress thiab txoj kev tawm tsam kev ywj pheej.

3.13 Kev qhia ua ntej txog Mahatma Gandhi txoj kev tua los ntawm Nathuram Godse

Hauv 'Jaiphula Malika', Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau qhia ua ntej txog Mahatma txoj kev tuag.

Odia Sloka:

ମୋହନ ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ମରଣ, ନାଥୁରାମ ହସ୍ତେ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏ କଥା ସିଦ୍ଧ ଫଳ ରେ ନିପୁଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mohana Gāndhī maraṇa, Nāthurāma haste hoiba jāṇa lo jāiphula,
e kathā siddha phaḷa re nipuṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Mohan Gandhi txoj kev tuag yuav tshwm sim los ntawm Nathuram txhais tes." Raws li keeb kwm sau tseg, rau lub Ib Hlis 30, 1948, Nathuram Vinayak Godse tau tua Mahatma Gandhi.

3.14 Kev ywj pheej ntawm Is Nrias teb raws li Mahatma Gandhi txoj kev coj

Hauv 'Kali Bhagabata', Mahapurush Abhiram Paramahansa (tus neeg dawb huv xyoo pua 20 hauv Malika kev lig kev cai) tau piav qhia txog kev hloov pauv kev nom kev tswv.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟାଗ୍ରହୀ ହୋଇଲେ ମେଳି ।
ଅନ୍ତର ହୋଇଯିବ କଳି ।।
ଇଉରୋପିଆନ ଯିବେ ଛାଡ଼ି ।
ମୋହନ ସେନା ଦେବେ କାଢ଼ି ।।
ପ୍ରଥମ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ସେନାପତି ।
ହେବେ ମୋହନ ସୈନ୍ୟ ଘେତି ।।
ମୋହନ ବୋଲି ଯାଙ୍କୁ କହି ।
ମହତ ଆତ୍ମା ଅଟେ ସେହି ।।
ସେହି ନାରଦ ମହାମୁନ୍ୟ ।
ତାଙ୍କୁ କରନ୍ତି ସର୍ବେ ମାନ୍ୟ ।।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Satyāgrahī hoile meḷi,
antara hoijiba kaḷi.
Iuropiāna jibe chhāḍi,
Mohana senā debe kāḍhi.
Prathama yuddhe senāpati,
hebe Mohana sainya gheti.
Mohana boli jāṅku kahi,
mahata ātmā aṭe sehi.
Sehi Nārada mahāmunya,
tāṅku karanti sarbe mānya.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus neeg dawb huv tau qhia ua ntej tias los ntawm kev sib sau ua ke ntawm Satyagrahis, cov neeg European yuav raug yuam kom tawm mus. Nws tau txheeb xyuas "Mohan" (Gandhi) ua tus thawj coj/tus thawj ntawm qhov kev tawm tsam loj thawj zaug no rau kev ywj pheej. Nws tau piav qhia ntxiv txog Mahatma Gandhi ua tus ntsuj plig zoo (Mahatma) thiab yog ib tug Narada tus cev xeeb tub, uas nws cov lus yuav raug hwm los ntawm txhua tus. Qhov no tau ua tiav raws li Gandhi txoj kev coj tau dhau los ua lub zog tseem ceeb uas ua rau cov British tawm ntawm Is Nrias teb thaj av.

4 Lub Hnub Nyoog ntawm Kaliyuga

Raws li Sanatan cov vajluskub, Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog tag nrho yog 432,000 xyoo. Bhavishya Malika kuj lees paub qhov no lub sijhawm tag nrho, tab sis nws ntxiv ib qho kev qhia ua ntej tseem ceeb: vim tib neeg ua txhaum ntau dhau, Kaliyuga yuav tsis ua tiav nws lub sijhawm tag nrho.

4.1 Txoj Cai Vajluskub rau 432,000 Xyoo

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv 'Adi Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିରେ ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯାଏ, ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନିର୍ଣ୍ଣୟ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷire chāri lakṣa jāe, batiśa sahasra nirṇaya.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Hauv Kaliyuga, plaub puas peb caug ob txhiab (432,000) xyoo raug txiav txim siab ua nws lub neej ntev."

Tom qab ntawd Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws cov ntawv, 'Dashapatala Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଭୋଗ ପ୍ରମାଣ ସାର,
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ହଜାର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaliyuga bhoga pramāṇa sāra,
chārilakṣha je batisha hajāra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Kali Yuga lub neej ntev uas tau teev tseg yog plaub puas peb caug ob txhiab xyoo.

Nws sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶି ସହସ୍ର କଳି,
କରିଲି ତିନି ଯୁଗଠାରୁ ବଳି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chārilakṣha batishi sahasra kali,
karili tini yugaṭhāru bali.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Kali Yuga lub neej ntev yog plaub puas peb caug ob txhiab xyoo.

Txawm li cas los xij, cov Mahapurushas tsis tau nres tom qab hais li no xwb; lawv kuj tau hais hauv lawv cov ntawv Malika tias vim tib neeg ua txhaum ntau yam, Kaliyuga yuav tsis muaj peev xwm ua tiav nws lub sijhawm tag nrho.

4.2 Vim li cas Kaliyuga yuav xaus ua ntej

Cov Panchasakhas qhia meej tias thaum lub hnub nyoog theoretical yog 432,000 xyoo, lub "Bhoga" (lub sijhawm tiag tiag uas tau ntsib) yuav luv dua vim qhov hnyav ntawm kev txhaum. Hauv 'Dashapatala Malika', nws tau sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର, ଭୋଗ ନୋହିବ କହ ପୀତବାସ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra, bhoga nohiba kaha Pītabāsa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Au Vajtswv Pitabasa (Jagannath)! Qhia rau peb tias Kaliyuga yuav ua li cas thiaj tsis muaj peev xwm ntsib nws lub sijhawm tag nrho ntawm 432,000 xyoo."

Tus Mahapurush sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika, 'Kalikalpa Gita':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ବର୍ଷ ଆଉ,
ଭୋଗ ନହେବ ପାପଭାରା ଯହୁଁ।
ଆପଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଉଦିତ,
ପାପୀମାନଙ୍କୁ କରିବେ ମୁକତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chāri lakṣha batisha sasra barṣha āu,
bhoga naheba pāpabhārā jahuṃ.
Āpaṇe prabhu hoibe udita,
pāpīmānaṃku karibe mukata.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, vim qhov hnyav dhau ntawm kev txhaum hauv Kali Yuga, Kali Yuga yuav tsis muaj peev xwm ntsib nws lub neej ntev ntawm 4,32,000 xyoo. Txawm tias ua ntej lub sijhawm no, Vajtswv yuav los cev xeeb tub, cov neeg txhaum yuav raug rhuav tshem, thiab Dharma yuav raug tsim.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau dua hauv ib qho ntawm nws cov ntawv Malika:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ,
ପାପ ଯୋଗୁଁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kali chāri lakṣha batisha sasra nohiba bhoga,
pāpa joguṃ kṣhaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Kali Yuga yuav tsis muaj peev xwm ntsib nws lub sijhawm teev tseg ntawm 4,32,000 xyoo. Vim kev txhaum, nws lub neej ntev yuav lwj, txhais tau tias, nws yuav tsis muaj peev xwm raug ntsib tag nrho.

4.3 Yog tias tsis yog 4,32,000 xyoo, ces Kali Yuga lub neej ntev npaum li cas?

Niaj hnub no, qee tus neeg tab tom koom nrog kev tshaj tawm, hais txog Malika, tias Kali Yuga lub neej ntev yog 10,000 xyoo. Cia li paub tias qhov no yog qhov tsis raug kiag li.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶ ସହସ୍ରରେ କଳିନାଶ ହେବ ଏ ଅଟେ ବିଷମ ବାଣୀ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dasha sahasrare kalināsha heba e aṭe biṣhama bāṇī.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Kali Yuga yuav xaus hauv 10,000 xyoo – qhov no yog ib qho lus tsis sib haum xeeb lossis tsis tseeb.

Cov Panchasakha Mahapurushas tau sau ntau qhov chaw hauv lawv cov ntawv Malika tias Kali Yuga lub neej ntev yuav yog 5,000 xyoo, tom qab ntawd Vajtswv Kalki yuav los cev xeeb tub thiab Satya-Kali lossis Ananta Yuga lossis Adya Satya Yuga yuav los txog.

Los, cia peb kawm ntxiv txog cov ncauj lus no ib zaug dhau ib zaug los ntawm Malika cov slokas.

Ua ntej tshaj plaws, cia peb paub tias Vajtswv Shri Krishna Nws tus kheej tau hais li cas txog Kali Yuga lub neej ntev.

4.4 Lub Sijhawm Tiag Tiag: 5,000 Xyoo

Tsis zoo li cov lus tsis tseeb uas qee tus tau tshaj tawm tias Kaliyuga kav 10,000 xyoo, cov ntawv Malika tau hais rov qab tias Kali txoj kev kav tiag tiag yuav xaus tom qab 5,000 xyoo, thiab tom qab ntawd yuav muaj lub sijhawm hloov pauv.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau hais Tus Tswv Krishna cov lus rau Garuda hauv 'Agata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika' tias:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ, ପାପ ଯୋଗୁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେ ପଚାଶ ଭୋଗ ହେବ ନିକର, ଏଥୁ କିଛି ଉଣା ହୋଇବ ଶୁଣ ଗରୁଡ଼ ବୀର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷi chāri lakṣa batiśa sahasra nohiba bhoga, pāpa jogu kṣaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.
Pāñcha sahasra je pachāsha bhoga heba nikara, ethu kichi uṇā hoiba shuṇa Garuḍa bīra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Au tus Garuda siab loj! Mloog, Kaliyuga yuav tsis kav 432,000 xyoo. Vim kev txhaum, nws lub sijhawm yuav luv zuj zus. Nws yuav xaus ze li 5,050 xyoo.

4.5 Piv Txwv Txog Ravana

Odia Sloka:

ରାମଦାସ ବୋଲଇ ହୋ ଶୁଣ ଗୁରୁଦେବ, କଳିଯୁଗ କେତେ ପୁଣ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ଯେ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ, କେମନ୍ତେ ଯେ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ହୋଇବଟି ଶେଷ।
ଏଡ଼େ ବଡ଼ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ସଂଶୟ ଫେଡ଼ିଣ ମୋତେ କହ ଗୁରୁଦେବ।
ରାମଦାସ ବାଣୀ ଶୁଣି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହନ୍ତି, ତୋତେ କହୁଅଛି ରାମ ଶୁଣ ଦେଇ ମତି।
ରାବଣ ଯେ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ ତପ କଲା, ଛପନ ଗଣ୍ଡା ଯୁଗକୁ ବର ସେ ପାଇଲା।
ପାପେ ଗଲା ତାର ତିନି ଭାଗ ଯେ ବିନାଶ, ଏକଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସ୍ୱର୍ଗେ ପଶି ଅପସରୀ ମାନଙ୍କୁ ହରିଲା, ପର ଦ୍ରବ୍ୟ ହରି ଯେବେ ପାଦେ ଖଟାଇଲା।
ଆପେ ଜଗତମାତା ଯେ ଜନକ ନନ୍ଦିନୀ, ତାଙ୍କୁ ହରି ନେଲା ଗର୍ବେ ମୂଢ଼ ବିଂଶପାଣି।
ସୁରାପାନ କଲା ସେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିଙ୍କୁ ବଧିଲା, ବ୍ରହ୍ମହତ୍ୟା ଆଦିକରି କେତେ ପାପ କଲା।
ପାପରୁ ଯେ ତିନି ଭାଗ ହୋଇଲା ବିନାଶ, ଏକ ଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସେହିପରି କଳିଯୁଗ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ପାଞ୍ଚସସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅନ୍ୟ ପାପେ ଯିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Rāmadāsa bolai ho shuṇa gurudeba, kaliyuga kete puṇa bhoga je hoiba.
Chārilakṣha batisha je sahasra baraṣha, kemaṃte je yuga puṇa hoibaṭi sheṣha.
Eḍe baḍa yuga puṇa bināsha hoiba, saṃshaya pheriṇa mote kaha gurudeba.
Rāmadāsa bāṇī shuṇi Achyuta kahaṃti, tote kahuachi Rāma shuṇa dei mati.
Rābaṇa je tretayā yugare tapa kalā, chapana gaṇḍā yugaku bara se pāilā.
Pāpe galā tāra tini bhāga je vināsha, ekabhāga bhoga kalā Bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Swarge pashi apasarī mānaṃku harilā, para drabya hari jebe pāde khaṭāilā.
Āpe jagatamātā je Janaka naṃdinī, tāṃku hari nelā garve mūḍha biṃshapāṇi.
Surāpāna kalā se kṣhatriṃṃku badhilā, brahmahatyā ādi kari kete pāpa kalā.
Pāparu je tini bhāga hoilā bināsha, eka bhāga bhoga kalā bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Sehipari kaliyuga bināsha hoiba, pāṃchasasra bhoga heba anya pāpe jiba.

Hauv 'Kalpatika', Achyutananda Das piav qhia txog qhov tshwm sim no los ntawm kev siv Ravana ua piv txwv. Ravana tau ua kev txhiv txoj kev txhaum loj heev thiab tau txais lub neej ntev txog "56 Ganda" (lub sijhawm cosmic loj heev). Txawm li cas los xij, vim nws tau nyiag Niam Sita, thuam cov ntxhais vajtswv saum ntuj, thiab ua txhaum loj heev xws li Brahman-hatya, nws tsuas tau txais ib feem ntawm nws txoj koob hmoov, thaum peb feem ntxiv raug rhuav tshem los ntawm nws tej kev txhaum.

Ib yam li ntawd, Kaliyuga lub sijhawm 432,000 xyoo tab tom raug "siv" los ntawm kev nce nrawm ntawm kev txhaum thoob ntiaj teb, ua rau nws xaus ntxov tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm lub sijhawm tau dhau los, qhib txoj hauv kev rau Ananta Yuga lossis Satya Yuga.

4.6 Kaliyuga Lub Hnub Nyoog: Kev Tshawb Fawb Kom Paub Meej

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv ib qho ntawm nws cov ntawv Malika tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗର ଅଟଇ ଆୟୁଷ।
ପାପଭାରରେ ଆୟୁ କଟିଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra,
Kaḷiyugara aṭai āyuṣa.
Pāpabhārare āyu kaṭijiba,
Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi bhoga hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog yog plaub puas peb caug ob txhiab (432,000) xyoo, tab sis vim muaj kev txhaum, lub hnub nyoog no yuav raug txiav luv thiab yuav xaus tsuas yog tsib txhiab (5,000) xyoo.

Ib tug kev los yug ntawm Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, yug rau xyoo pua 18th hauv Jajpur cheeb tsam ntawm Odisha, Saint Mahapurush Hadi Das tau tham txog Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog hauv nws cov ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Kali Chautisha’. Nws sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ସଂବତ୍ସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗ ଆଦ୍ୟ ହୋଇବ ଶୁଭଯୋଗେ ପ୍ରବେଶ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Saṃbatsara pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hoiba sheṣa,
Satya yuga ādya hoiba shubhajoge prabesha.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, hauv "Sambatsara Paancha Sahasra" (5,000 xyoo), Kaliyuga yuav xaus, thiab tom qab ntawd, Adya Satya Yuga yuav pib nrog lub sijhawm zoo.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv lwm cov ntawv Malika tias:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ଯେ ହଜାର ଥାରୁ ମହାଭୟ ହେବ,
ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସମ୍ରାଟକୁ ଆସି ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଗାନ୍ଧାର ସେନାଟି ଗୋଳ କରିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର ପଚାଶ କଳିର ଆୟୁଷ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Pāñcha je hajāra thāru mahābhaya heba,
Dillī samrāṭaku āsi bipada paḍiba.
Gāndhāra senāṭi goḷa kariba abashya,
Pāñcha hajāra pachāsha Kaḷira āyuṣa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush sau ntawm no ib yam nkaus tias tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga, yuav muaj huab cua txaus ntshai heev; kev puas tsuaj yuav poob rau tus huab tais ntawm Delhi lossis tus thawj tswj hwm ntawm lub teb chaws. Cov tub rog Gandhara (Taliban) yeej yuav ua tsov rog tawm tsam Is Nrias teb. Mahapurush tseem sau ntxiv tias tag nrho cov no yuav tshwm sim tom qab Kaliyuga xaus, piv txwv li, tom qab 5,050 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga tau dhau los.

Achyutananda Das ji kuj tau sau ib zaj paj lug hauv nws cov ntawv Malika uas nws tsis tau hais ncaj qha txog Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog yog 5,000 lossis 5,050, tab sis tau hais tsis ncaj qha los ntawm cov lus qhia:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶେ ମସିହା ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କରିବେ କଳି ବିନାଶ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Biṃshe masiha hoiba sheṣa,
Ananta karibe Kaḷi bināsha.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, nyob rau hauv '20s'—txij xyoo ob txhiab (2000) mus txog ob txhiab cuaj caum cuaj (2099)—nyob rau qee lub sijhawm no, Kaliyuga yuav xaus. Tus Tswv Ananta yuav rhuav tshem Kaliyuga hauv ntiaj teb.

Ib tug ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, tau piav qhia txog Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog kom paub meej hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Aagata Bhavishya Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବୋଲଇ ଶୁଣିମା ଗୋସାଇଁ କୁହ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବିଚାର,
କେତେବେଳେ କଳ୍କୀ ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ଶୁଣଇଁ ମୁଖୁ ତୁମ୍ଭର।
ଶିଶୁ ବୋଲନ୍ତି ହେ ଶୁଣିମା ବାରଙ୍ଗ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ତୁହି,
ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଯେବେ ଯିବ ଭୋଗ ହୋଇ।
ଜେସେନେକେ ନିଶି ପାହିଲେ ପ୍ରଭାତ ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ଏହା ଜାଣ,
ଯେମନ୍ତ ସମୟେ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ସେ ନାରାୟଣ।
ସମକ୍ଷର ବତା ଶୁଣି ଆଣିକରି ପ୍ରମାଣ ଏହାକୁ କର,
ସବୁ ଏକଠାବେ ମିଶାଇ କରିଣ କରିବୁ ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର।
ଏହି ସମୟକୁ ତଳେ କରିଥିବୁ କହଇ ହୋ ବାବୁ ତୋତେ,
ଠିକ ରେ ଏ କଥା ଦେଖାଇ କହିଲୁ ରଖିଥିବୁ ହୃଦଗତେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bāraṅga bolai shuṇimā gosāiṃ kuha bhabiṣya bichāra,
ketebeḷe Kalkī swarūpa hoibe shuṇaiṃ mukhu tumbhara.
Shishu bolanti he shuṇimā Bāraṅga Kaḷaṅki swarūpa tuhi,
yugashandhi pāñcha sahasra baraṣa jebe jiba bhoga hoi.
Jeseneke nishi pāhile prabhāta yugashandhi ehā jāṇa,
jemanta samaye Kaḷaṅki swarūpa hoibe se Nārāyaṇa.
Samakṣara batā shuṇi āṇikari pramāṇa ehāku kara,
sabu ekaṭhābe mishāi kariṇa karibu pāñcha hajāra.
Ehi samayaku taḷe karithibu kahai ho bābu tote,
Ṭhika re e kathā dekhāi kahilu rakhithibu hṛudagate.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Thwjtim Baranga Das hais rau Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das—"Au Guru Dev, qhia rau kuv txog yav tom ntej. Kuv xav hnov los ntawm koj lub qhov ncauj tias thaum twg Tus Tswv yuav tshwm sim hauv Nws daim Kalki."

Thaum hnov li no, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das qhia nws tus thwjtim siab tshaj Baranga tias thaum 'Yuga-Sandhi' (kev hloov pauv ntawm lub sijhawm) tshwm sim, tsuas yog thaum ntawd Tus Tswv thiaj yuav los yug ua Nws daim Kalki. Piav qhia ntxiv, nws hais tias Yuga-Sandhi no yuav tshwm sim thaum Kaliyuga tau dhau 5,000 xyoo. Ib yam li sawv ntxov los tom qab hmo ntuj, lwm lub sijhawm los tom qab ib lub sijhawm. Qhov nruab nrab ntawm ob lub sijhawm hu ua Yuga-Sandhi. Thiab nyob rau hauv Yuga-Sandhi no, Narayana yuav los ua Nws daim Kalki. Yuga-Sandhi no yuav los tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga tau dhau los.

Achyutananda ji, thaum hais txog Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Kaliyuga Malika’, hais tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଚହଟିବ ଲୀଳା ତୁ ଚାରିରେ ମିଶା ଏକ,
ଚଢ଼ା ତିନି ଶୂନ ତହିଁ ଯେତେ ହେଲା ଠିକ।
ଚଳିଯିବ ଘୋର କଳି ଦଳି ଦେବେ ମିଳି,
ଚେତାଇ ଗୀତ ଛନ୍ଦଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ସେ ଭାଳି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

chahaṭiba līḷā tu chārire mishā eka,
chaḍhā tini shūna tahiñ jete helā ṭhika.
Chalijiba ghora Kaḷi daḷi debe miḷi,
chetāi gīta chhandai Achyuta se bhāḷi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, ntxiv ib rau tus lej plaub (4+1=5) thiab muab peb lub xoom tso tom qab tus lej ntawd; tus lej tau txais sawv cev rau lub sijhawm tom qab uas lub sijhawm Kaliyuga txaus ntshai yuav xaus. Hauv zaj paj lug saum toj no, Mahapurush tab tom qhia Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog ua cim. Nws hais 4+1=5, thiab tom qab ntawd muab peb lub xoom tso tom qab 5 txhais tau tias tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga tau dhau los, lub sijhawm ntawd yuav xaus.

Hauv lwm cov ntawv Malika ‘Bhavishya Chautisha’, nws hais rau nws tus thwjtim Ramachandra tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅମରପୁର,
ଠାକୁର ତହିଁରୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ, ଠାରି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଧର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ṭhikaṇā Amarapura,
Ṭhākura tahiñru hebe bāhāra, Rāmachandra he,
ṭhāri pāñcha sahasra dhara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, Au thwjtim Ramachandra, Tus Tswv Kalki txoj kev los yug yuav tshwm sim hauv Amarapur (thaj chaw uas tsis tuag—muaj nyob ua ntej Satya Yuga, piv txwv li, Jajpur), thiab qhov no yuav tshwm sim tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga tau dhau los.

Tom qab ntawd, hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Kali Bayalisha’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das qhia txog Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog ua cim. Mahapurush hais tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶରଥ ସୁତ ମୁଣ୍ଡରେ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କ ଚଢ଼ିବ,
ବଜ୍ର ଅଙ୍କ ଦେଇ ଏଗାର ଅଙ୍କ ତହୁଁ ଫେରିବ।
ଶେଷ ଡାହାଣ ଭାଗ ଯାହା ହେଲା ଗାଣ୍ଡିବ ଗୁଣ,
କଳି ଶେଷ ଭୋଗ ଏହିଟି କହିଲି ଅରଜୁନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dasharatha suta muṇḍare tini aṅka chaḍhiba,
bajra aṅka dei egāra aṅka tahuṃ pheriba.
Sheṣa ḍāhāṇa bhāga jāhā helā gāṇḍiba guṇa,
kaḷi sheṣa bhoga ehiṭi kahili Arajuna.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, sau tus lej 3 saum tus lej tub uas Vaj Ntxwv Dasharatha muaj (piv txwv li, 4^3 = 64). Mahapurush tom qab ntawd hais kom hloov rov qab thiab rho 11 tawm ntawm tus lej ntawd (Hloov 64 rov qab tau 46, thiab 46 - 11 = 35). Mahapurush hais kom muab tus lej nyob sab xis ntawm tus lej tau txais no los sib npaug nrog 'Gandiva' (piv txwv li, 1000) thiab sau tias Kaliyuga yuav xaus rau tus lej tau txais lossis 5,000 xyoo.
Lus Cim: Ntawm no, los ntawm 'Gandiva', Mahapurush hais txog nws qhov kev ntaus tau deb, uas yog 1,000 Yojanas.

Achyutananda Das ji sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Adi Sanhita’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ ଯେ ସୁବାହୁ ଯୁଗ କଳି,
କ୍ଷୀଣ ଆୟୁଷ ମହାବଳି।
ପାପେ ସକଳ କ୍ଷୟ ଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

E je subāhu yuga kaḷi,
kṣīṇa āyuṣa mahābaḷi.
pāpe sakaḷa kṣaya jiba,
pāñcha sahasra bhoga heba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog 432,000 xyoo yuav luv zuj zus mus rau tsuas yog 5,000 xyoo vim kev txhaum.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଭୋଗ ହେବ,
ସେହି ଦିନୁ ସତ୍ୟକଳି ପ୍ରକାଶ ପାଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷi pāñchasahasra baraṣa bhoga heba,
sehi dinu Satyakali prakāsha pāiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, Kaliyuga txoj kev dhau los yuav yog rau 5,000 xyoo. Tom qab ntawd, 'Satyakali'—lub teeb ntawm Adya Satya Yuga nyob rau hauv Kaliyuga—yuav tshwm sim.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାରରେ ଗଙ୍ଗାଦେବୀ ମେଳ କଟକ ଠାରେ,
ଭକ୍ତ ଦେଖିବେ ନେତ୍ରରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷi pāñchahajārare Gaṅgādebī meḷa Kaṭaka ṭhāre,
bhakta dekhibe netrare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga tau dhau los, Vajtswv poj niam Ganga yuav hla dhau Cuttack; txhais tau tias, txhua yam los ntawm Kolkata mus rau Cuttack yuav raug dej nyab; yuav muaj dej nyab loj heev.

Mahapurush tau sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହଟେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ,
ମୋର ତଥ୍ୟମାନ ସେତେବେଳେଟି ହୋଇବ।
ପୂର୍ବ ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଆବର ଦକ୍ଷିଣ,
ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେବେ କଳଙ୍କି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hāte satya upujiba,
mora tathyamāna setebeḷeṭi hoiba.
pūrba pashchima uttara ābara dakṣiṇa,
prakāsha hebe Kaḷaṅki Achyuta bachana.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga tau dhau los, Kaliyuga yuav xaus thiab Qhov Tseeb (Satya) yuav los txog. Tom qab ntawd, Tus Tswv Kalki lub teeb yuav tshwm sim nyob rau txhua qhov chaw—Sab Hnub Tuaj, Sab Hnub Poob, Sab Qaum Teb, thiab Sab Qab Teb.

Saint Achyutananda Das sau dua ib zaug ntxiv hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ସପ୍ତ ବିଂଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଆୟୁଷ ହେବ ବିନାଶ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରଟି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ,
ସତ୍ୟଯୁଗ ପୁଣି ଉଦୟା ହେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chāri lakṣa sapta biṃsha sahasra,
Kaḷiyuga āyuṣa heba bināsha.
pāñcha sahasraṭi bhoga hoiba,
Satyayuga puṇi udayā heba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, 427,000 xyoo yuav raug rhuav tshem los ntawm Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog 432,000 xyoo; tsuas yog 5,000 xyoo yuav raug dhau los, tom qab ntawd Satya Yuga yuav pib.
