Tsis yog Mahapurush Achyutananda Das xwb, tab sis lwm tus ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, kuj tau tham txog Kaliyuga lub hnub nyoog hauv nws cov ntawv Malika sib txawv. Thaum piav qhia rau nws tus thwjtim Baranga hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’, nws sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଏବେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ଠିକ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବିଚାର ଚିତ୍ତରେ ଘେନ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ebe pāñcha ṭhika kahibā shuṇa,
Bāraṅga bichāra chittare ghena.
pāñcha sahasra jetebeḷe heba,
sampūrṇa līḷā prakāsha hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, mloog zoo zoo thwjtim Baranga, tuav tus lej 5. Thaum 5,000 xyoo tau dhau los, tsuas yog thaum ntawd Tus Tswv txoj Leela thiaj yuav tshwm sim tag nrho.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das tau sau dua hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଗଲେ,
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଜନମ ଯେ ହୋଇବେ ଭଲେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷiyuga pāñcha sahasra gale,
Viṣṇu janama je hoibe bhale.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga tau dhau los, Tus Tswv Vishnu txoj kev los yug yuav tshwm sim.

Piav qhia tias Kaliyuga yuav xaus tom qab tsib txhiab xyoo tau dhau los thiab Tus Tswv txoj kev ua yeeb yam dawb huv yuav tshwm sim, Mahapurush Balarama Das tau sau hauv nws cov ntawv Malika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ହସ୍ତିନା କଟକ ଯେବଣ ଭୂଇଁ,
ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସହର ସେହି।
ପୂର୍ବର ଚରିତ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ଦେବାପି ଅଛନ୍ତି ତପସ୍ୟାରେଣ।
ଉତ୍ତର ଖଣ୍ଡେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରନ୍ତି,
ତପସ୍ୟା ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ହେ ହୋଇ ନାହାନ୍ତି।
ପଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରେ ବରଷ ପୁଣ,
ତପସ୍ୟା ତାଙ୍କର ହୋଇବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ।
ବାରୁଣା କୁଳେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଭେଟିବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଶ୍ରୀହସ୍ତେ ସିରୀପା ବାନ୍ଧିବେ।
ଶ୍ରୀପଦ୍ମ ହସ୍ତ ତା ମୁଣ୍ଡେ ଲଦିବେ,
ହସ୍ତିନା ରାଜ୍ୟ ନଗ୍ରରେ ସ୍ଥାପିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Hastinā kaṭaka jebaṇa bhūiṃ,
bartamāna Dillī sahara sehi.
Pūrbara charita kahibā shuṇa,
Debāpi achhanti tapasyāreṇa.
Uttara khaṇḍe tapasyā karanti,
tapasyā pūrṇa he hoi nāhānti.
Pañcha sahasre baraṣa puṇa,
tapasyā tāṅkara hoiba pūrṇa.
Bāruṇā kuḷe prabhuṅku bheṭibe,
Prabhu shrīhaste sirīpā bāndhibe.
Shrīpadma hasta tā muṇḍe ladibe,
Hastinā rājya nagrare sthāpibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Lub ntsiab lus yog, thaj av uas yog Hastinapur Cuttack yog lub nroog Delhi niaj hnub no. Mloog zaj dab neeg yav dhau los—Devaapi tab tom ua kev txhiv txoj kev txhaum. Nws tab tom ua kev txhiv txoj kev txhaum hauv Kalapa zos hauv Uttarakhand cheeb tsam ntawm Is Nrias teb, uas yuav ua tiav tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga tau dhau los. Tom qab kev txhiv txoj kev txhaum tiav lawm, nws yuav ntsib Tus Tswv. Tus Tswv yuav muab ib lub kaus mom rau nws nrog Nws tus kheej txhais tes paj lotus thiab ua rau nws yog Vaj Ntxwv ntawm Hastinapur lossis Delhi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଦାରୁ ଚଢ଼ି ଦାରୁରୂପ ତେଜିକରି କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହୋଇବ,
ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ ମୋହର ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳିରେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଘଟିବ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dāru chaḍhi dārurūpa tejikari Kalkī rūpa hoiba,
niṣṭhā bachana mohara Rāmachandra Kaḷire nishche ghaṭiba.
āhuri kathāe shuṇa Rāmadāsa Kaḷi pāñcha sahasrare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga dhau mus, Vajtswv Jagannatha yuav tso Nws tus 'Daaru-rupa' lossis Jagannatha daim duab tseg thiab hloov mus ua tus Kalki daim duab.

Los ntawm tag nrho cov nqe lus saum toj no, nws yog qhov tseeb tias lub hnub nyoog ntawm Kaliyuga uas tau dhau los tsuas yog 5,000 xyoo xwb. Nws tsis yog 432,000 xyoo los yog 10,000 xyoo.

Tab sis muaj ib lo lus nug tseem ceeb tshwm sim ntawm no— Raws li peb paub, 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga twb dhau lawm thiab tam sim no yog xyoo 5,127. Raws li qhov kev suav no, Kaliyuga yuav tsum tau xaus lawm thiab 'Adya Satya Yuga' yuav tsum tau pib lawm! Tab sis Kaliyuga txoj kev cuam tshuam tseem pom thoob plaws. Kev txhaum thiab kev tsis ncaj ncees muaj nyob txhua qhov chaw. Vim li cas ho muaj qhov tsis sib haum xeeb no?

Cov lus teb yog raws li nram no— Ua ntej, 5,000 xyoo yog ib nqe lus dav dav, tsis yog qhov tseeb tiag. 5,000 xyoo txhais tau tias kwv yees li 5,000 xyoo. Qhov no yog vim li cas ntau qhov chaw, 5,050 xyoo kuj tau sau.

Thib ob, raws li Malika, lub sijhawm ntawm Kaliyuga twb xaus lawm thiab tam sim no, 'Sandhikala' (lub sijhawm hloov pauv) ntawm Kaliyuga thiab Satya Yuga tab tom pib. Raws li Malika, nws yog nyob rau lub sijhawm Sandhikala no uas cov xwm txheej ntawm Vajtswv Kalki yug los thiab Nws tsim Dharma—uas yog, rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab cawm cov neeg ntseeg—yuav tshwm sim. Qhov no yog vim li cas kev txhaum thiab kev tsis ncaj ncees thiaj li muaj ntau heev txawm tias Kaliyuga lub sijhawm twb xaus lawm. Yuav tsum paub tias thaum Dharma raug tsim tiav lawm, Kaliyuga txoj kev cuam tshuam yuav xaus tag nrho thiab Qhov Tseeb, Dharma, thiab Kev Ncaj Ncees yuav raug tsim dua tshiab.

Achyutananda Das kuj tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika txog thaum twg qhov kev tsim Dharma no yuav tshwm sim raws li Vajtswv Kalki txoj kev coj.

4.7 Kev Sib Koom Ua Ke ntawm Meena-Shani

Mahapurush tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv ‘Aagata Bhavishyat Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲେ,
ଠ’ ତିନି ବାମେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ରଖିଲେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ,
ଠକିଯିବ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭଲେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ṭhikaṇā Achyuta bole,
ṭha’ tini bāme pāñcha rakhile Rāmachandra he,
ṭhakijiba mīna shani bhale.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, yog tias koj muab peb lub xoom thiab tus lej 5 ua ntej lawv (5,000), nws txhais tau tias tom qab 5,000 xyoo ntawm Kaliyuga dhau mus, kev sib koom ua ke ntawm Meena-Shani (Saturn hauv Pisces) yuav tshwm sim, uas yuav tseem ceeb heev.

5 Meena-Shani Yoga; Raws li Malika

5.1 Meena-Shani yog dab tsi?

Hauv kev tshawb fawb hnub qub, Saturn (Shani) raug suav hais tias yog lub ntiaj teb uas txav qeeb tshaj plaws, nyob hauv ib lub cim zodiac tau kwv yees li 2.5 xyoo. Muaj tag nrho 12 lub cim zodiac hauv lub voj voog zodiac, thiab Saturn nyob hauv txhua lub cim tau li 2.5 xyoo. Qhov no txhais tau tias Saturn siv sijhawm kwv yees li 12 × 2.5 = 30 xyoo los hla tag nrho lub voj voog zodiac.

Piv txwv tias Saturn nkag mus rau hauv ib lub cim zodiac hnub no; nws yuav rov qab mus rau tib lub cim ntawd tom qab kwv yees li 30 xyoo tom qab ua tiav tag nrho txoj kev hla. Meena-Shani txhais tau tias Saturn hla mus rau hauv lub cim zodiac Pisces (Meena).

Zaum kawg Saturn hla dhau Pisces yog xyoo 1995. Txij li thaum nws siv sijhawm li 30 xyoo rau Saturn rov qab mus rau tib lub cim, 1995 + 30 = 2025. Yog li ntawd, xyoo 2025, Saturn yuav hla mus rau Pisces dua ib zaug. Tom qab xyoo 2025, qhov kev hla tom ntej yuav yog 2025 + 30, uas yog 2054–2055.

Cov Panchasakha Mahapurushas tau piav qhia meej hauv lawv phau ntawv Malika tias kev rhuav tshem cov neeg phem, kev tiv thaiv cov neeg ntseeg, thiab kev tsim Dharma los ntawm Vajtswv Kalki yuav tshwm sim feem ntau thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani.

Yuav kom nkag siab qhov no zoo dua, cia peb saib qee cov nqe lus uas tau hais hauv Malika thiab lawv cov lus piav qhia.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Prachi Kalpa’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀମଣ୍ଡଳରେ ଚମକ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ନ ଜାଣିବେ ହୀନ ଜନ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀରେ ସଂହାର ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ର ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mahīmaṇḍaḷare chamaka paḍiba,
na jāṇibe hīna jana.
Mlechcha māruṇīre saṃhāra hoiba,
mīna shani ra pramāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, yuav muaj kev quaj qw thoob ntiaj teb; yuav muaj kev kub ntxhov txhua qhov chaw. Tsis muaj leej twg tsuas yog cov neeg ntseeg thiaj yuav nkag siab txog cov no. Hauv cov xwm txheej no, ntau tus neeg phem thiab cov neeg txhaum yuav raug rhuav tshem. Tus Mahapurush tau hais tias cov xwm txheej no yuav tshwm sim thaum lub sijhawm 2.5 xyoo ntawm Meena-Shani Yoga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦା ପ୍ରମାଣ, ଗୁପ୍ତରେ ଲାଗିବ ସେ ମାୟା ରଣ।
ଥୋକେ ଯିବେ କାମଚଣ୍ଡୀ ମୁଖରେ, ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଗୁପ୍ତରେ।
ତରକ ତେରୁଆ ଯେତେ ଭକତ, ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦାରେ ନାଶ।
ଛିନ୍ନମସ୍ତା ଦେବୀ ଖର୍ପର ପାତି, କାଠମାଳିଆଙ୍କୁ ଖାଇବ ବାଛି।
ଘୋର ଅନ୍ଧକାର ସେବେଳେ ହେବ, ସବୁ ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଜ୍ଞାନ ହଜିଣ ଯିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ନ ମିଳିବ ଶସ୍ୟ ନିଅଣ୍ଟି, ମୀନ ଶନିଆସି ଦେବଟି ଘାଣ୍ଟି।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ଚୁଲି ସେହିଦିନ ଫିଟିବ, ହାଡ଼ିଆ କମାର ପରଣୁ ଥିବ।
ସେଦିନ ଯେ ଗୁରୁବାରଟି ଜାଣ, କାଳିଆ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଅଟେ ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Jobarā Kāḷiābodā pramāṇa,
guptare lāgiba se māyā raṇa.
Thoke jibe Kāmachaṇḍī mukhare, Mlechcha māruṇī je heba guptare.
Taraka teruā jete bhakata, Jobarā Kāḷiābodāre nāsha.
Chhinnamastā debī kharpara pāti, kāṭhamāḷiāṅku khāiba bāchi.
Ghora andhakāra sebeḷe heba, sabu buddhi jñāna hajiṇa jiba.
Anna na miḷiba shasya niaṇṭi, mīna shaniāsi debaṭi ghāṇṭi.
Lakṣe chuli sehidina phiṭiba, hāḍiā kamāra paraṇu thiba.
Sedina je gurubāraṭi jāṇa, Kāḷiā parīkṣā aṭe pramāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau tias thaum Saturn nyob hauv Pisces, thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, kev sim siab rau txhua tus neeg siab phem thiab cov neeg tsis ntseeg yuav tshwm sim hauv Jobra thiab Kaliaboda ntawm Cuttack. Cov Niam Vajtswv (Goddesses) yuav rhuav tshem tag nrho cov neeg tsis ntseeg ntawd. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, yuav muaj kev tsaus ntuj rau xya hnub thiab xya hmo. Txhua tus neeg txoj kev txawj ntse yuav tsum tsis ua haujlwm. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, cov neeg txhaum yuav tsis muaj peev xwm nrhiav tau zaub mov noj.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଅଙ୍କ,
ଭାରତଶ୍ୱରୀ ହେବ ଉତପାତ।
ହେତୁ ରଖିଥାଅ ବ୍ରଜକୁମର,
ସେକାଳେ ତେଜିବି ମୁଁ କଳେବର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna shani heba Mukunda aṅka,
Bhāratashwarī heba utapāta.
Hetu rakhithāa Brajakumara,
sekāḷe tejibi muñ kaḷebara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Saturn nyob hauv Pisces, yuav muaj kev kub ntxhov hauv Is Nrias teb. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, tus Tswv Tshaj Plaws yuav tso nws tus tib neeg daim duab ib txwm tseg thiab hloov mus ua tus Kalki daim duab uas muaj hwj chim loj heev.

Hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika, tus Mahapurush tau sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନିର ସଂଯୋଗ ହେବାର ସମୟ,
ପୃଥବୀରେ ହେବ ପୁଣି ଘୋର ଯେ ପ୍ରଳୟ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna shanira saṃyoga hebāra samaya,
pṛthabīre heba puṇi ghora je pralaya.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Meena-Shani sib koom ua ke, yuav muaj kev rhuav tshem loj heev (Pralaya) dua ib zaug hauv ntiaj teb.

Achyutananda Das ji sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ୋଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧୃବ।
ଅଢ଼ୋଇ ବରଷ ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ ଛବିଶ ଅଙ୍କ କୁଅନା,
ଶ୍ମଶାନ ପରାୟ ଏ ସାରା ସଂସାର ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ସିନା।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna shani āsi deuthiba ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhōi heba,
dasha aṇā jiba chha' aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.
aḍhāi baraṣa ghora utpāta chhabbisha aṅka kuanā,
shmashāna parāya e sārā saṃsāra uchhanna hoiba sinā.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Saturn hla dhau Pisces, Kev Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb Thib Peb yuav pib hauv ntiaj teb, suav nrog Is Nrias teb. Rau ob xyoos ib nrab, kev rhuav tshem txaus ntshai yuav pom thoob ntiaj teb. Tag nrho lub ntiaj teb yuav raug rhuav tshem thiab hloov mus ua ib qhov chaw hlawv neeg tuag. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, ntau tus neeg yuav tuag.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Bhabishyata Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ସାବଧାନ ହୁଅ ତୁହି,
ଠା ଠିକଣା କିଛି ରହିବ ନାହିଁ।
ହେ ଭକ୍ତଗଣ! ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସିଗଲେ ଧାଇଁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Sābadhāna hua tuhi,
ṭhā ṭhikaṇā kichhi rahiba nāhiñ.
He bhaktagaṇa! Mīna shani āsigale dhāiñ.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Au tib neeg, ceev faj. Meena-Shani Yoga tab tom los txog. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, ntau yam kev puas tsuaj yuav tshwm sim, thiab kev rhuav tshem yuav cuam tshuam rau yuav luag txhua tus; tsis muaj leej twg yuav dim. Tib neeg txoj kev txawj ntse yuav tsis ua haujlwm. Kev kub ntxhov yuav muaj nyob txhua qhov chaw.

Yug hauv xyoo pua 19, tus kws sau paj huam dawb huv Bhima Bhoi tau sau txog qhov no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Padmakalpa’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଖିବ ଜଳ ଅବିଳମ୍ବେ,
ମୀନ ଶନିର ମଧ୍ୟଭାଗେ।
ମୋଗଲ ପଠାଣର ଧାଡ଼ି,
ମଧ୍ୟ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଥିବ ମାଡ଼ି।
ସିଂହ ମଙ୍ଗଳ ଗ୍ରହ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି,
କିଛି ମେଦିନୀ ଯିବ ଫାଟି।
ଗିରି କନ୍ଦର ଗୁମ୍ଫାମାନ,
ମୋ ଭକ୍ତେ ରହିବେଟି ପୁଣ।
ଆସିବେ ଚଣ୍ଡୀକାଇଗଣ,
ଭକ୍ଷିବେ ନଗ୍ରରେ ପଶିଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shukhiba jaḷa abiḷambe,
mīna shanira madhyabhāge.
Mogala paṭhāṇara dhāḍi,
madhya Oḍishā thiba māḍi.
Siṃha maṅgaḷa graha dṛṣṭi,
kichhi medinī jiba phāṭi.
Giri kandara gumphāmāna,
mo bhakte rahibeṭi puṇa.
Āsibe chaṇḍīkāigaṇa,
bhakṣibe nagrare pashiṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tom qab nruab nrab ntawm Meena-Shani Yoga, cov dej ntws thiab kwj deg yuav pib qhuav sai sai. Cov tub rog ntawm cov teb chaws yeeb ncuab yuav nkag mus rau Odisha thiab sib ntaus. Nyob rau nruab nrab ntawm tib lub Meena-Shani Yoga ntawd, thaum Mars nyob hauv Leo, av qeeg kuj yuav tshwm sim. Tus Tswv yuav khaws Nws cov neeg ntseeg kom nyab xeeb nrog kev saib xyuas hauv cov qhov tsua ntawm roob. Chandi thiab Yoginis yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab cov neeg txhaum.

Tus kws sau paj huam Narada Das kuj tau hais txog cov kev kub ntxhov uas tshwm sim thaum Meena-Shani Yoga hauv nws phau ntawv Malika. Nws sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀଏ ନାଶ ଯିବ ରଙ୍କରେ।
ବତିଶ ତେତିଶ ପୁଣି ଚଉତିରିଶ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ।
ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ ଉଲ୍ଲୋଳ,
ଯୋଗିନୀ ହେବେ ପ୍ରବଳ।
ପଡ଼ି ହାଡ଼ କଙ୍କାଳ,
ଗଡ଼ିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ ମାଳ।
କମ୍ପିବ ଭୈରବ ଡାକରେ,
ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare,
prāṇīe nāsha jiba raṅkare.
Batisha tetisha puṇi chautirisha,
mīna shani bhoga heba aḍhāi baraṣa.
Pṛthabī heba ulloḷa,
yoginī hebe prabaḷa.
Paḍi hāḍa kaṅkāḷa,
gaḍiba muṇḍa māḷa.
Kampiba bhairaba ḍākare,
mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Meena-Shani Yoga yuav kav tag nrho ob xyoos ib nrab thiab thaum lub sijhawm no, ntau tus neeg yuav tuag. Yuav muaj kev quaj qw thoob ntiaj teb. Yoginis yuav ua tus leela ntawm kev rhuav tshem. Thaum lub sijhawm no, cov cev tuag yuav nteg thoob plaws thaj av. Lub ntiaj teb yuav tshee hnyo nrog Bhairavi lub suab nrov.

Hais txog kev sib tham ntawm Vajtswv Krishna thiab Niam Rukmini thaum Dvapara Yuga, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Rukmini Gita’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ମେଳ ଗୋ ହୋଇବ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ସେବେଳେ ସମର ହେବ ଭାରତ ମଣ୍ଡଳେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna shani meḷa go hoiba jetebeḷe,
sebeḷe samara heba Bhārata maṇḍaḷe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Saturn hla mus rau hauv Pisces, kev sib ntaus sib tua hnyav yuav tshwm sim hauv Is Nrias teb.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau dua tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ପଡ଼ିଲେଟି ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ମନେ ରଖିଥିବୁ ରାମ ତୁହି ବ୍ରଜସୁତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna shani paḍileṭi ghora utpāta,
mane rakhithibu Rāma tuhi Brajasuta.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Meena-Shani Yoga tshwm sim, kev kub ntxhov txaus ntshai yuav pom thoob ntiaj teb no.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ତେରଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ ରାଜନ,
ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଗଗନ।
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ କଷଣ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ଚଉଦ୍ୱାରେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ।
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନିକ ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଚଳ,
ନିକଟରେ ଗୋଳ ଚହଳ।
ତପ ସଙ୍ଗେ ତୋପ ପରୀକ୍ଷା,
ସତ୍ୟ ଅନନ୍ତଟି ଦେଖା।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ହୋଇବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Teradina pakṣa hoiba,
mīna shani yoga paḍiba.
Aḍhāi baraṣa rājana,
dibasare tārā gagana.
Bhaktaṅku kaṣaṇa paḍiba,
Chaudwāre yuddha hoiba.
Baijñānika yantra achaḷa,
nikaṭare goḷa chahaḷa.
Tapa saṅge topa parīkṣā,
satya Anantaṭi dekhā.
Ananta yugaṭi hoiba,
Achyutī kāmanā pūriba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, thaum lub sijhawm tsim Dharma, ib lub sijhawm (Paksha) ntawm kaum peb hnub yuav tshwm sim. Yuav muaj Meena-Shani Yoga uas yuav kav ob xyoos ib nrab. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, hnub qub yuav pom thaum nruab hnub. Hauv lub sijhawm ntawd, cov neeg ntseeg yuav ntsib ntau yam kev nyuaj siab. Kev sib ntaus sib tua yuav tshwm sim hauv Choudwar ntawm Cuttack; cov khoom siv tshawb fawb thiab hluav taws xob yuav tsis ua haujlwm. Kev sib ntaus sib tua hnyav yuav tshwm sim. Cov foob pob hluav taws niaj hnub thiab phom loj ntawm cov teb chaws yeeb ncuab yuav raug tiv thaiv los ntawm lub hwj chim ntawm kev txhiv (Tapa) ntawm Is Nrias teb cov neeg dawb huv thiab cov neeg txawj ntse. Vajtswv Kalki yuav tshwm sim; cov neeg ntseeg yuav tau txais kev pom (Darshan) ntawm Tus Tswv. Lub Hnub Nyoog Nyob Mus Ib Txhis (Ananta Yuga) yuav los txog. Achyutananda txoj kev xav yuav ua tiav.

Nws tau sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଆକାଶୁ ଖସିବ ମୀନ ଶନିର ରାଜତ୍ୱ,
ବାଡ଼ି ବେମାରିରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dibasare tārā ākāśu khasiba mīna shanira rājatva,
bāḍi bemārire aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, hnub qub yuav poob los saum ntuj thaum nruab hnub; ntau tus neeg yuav tuag vim muaj kab mob thiab kev sib kis loj. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, Meena-Shani Yoga yuav tseem tab tom tshwm sim.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି କରିବ ରାଜତ୍ୱ ମାନବ ମାରୁଣୀ ହେବ,
ପରାଧୀନ ହେବ ଏ ଭାରତବର୍ଷ ଶାସନ କଳ ଲାଗିବ।
ଅରାଜକ ହେବ ଏହି ଜମ୍ବୁଦ୍ବୀପ ଭକ୍ତ ହୋଇବେ ଛାନିଆଁ,
ଅନହୁତି ଅଗ୍ନି ପ୍ରଜ୍ୱଳିତ ହେବ ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ସାରା ଦୁନିଆ।
ଛିନିଛତ୍ର ହେବ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳ ଥୟ ନ ପଡ଼ିବେ କେହି,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କୁହଇ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna shani āsi kariba rājatwa mānaba māruṇī heba,
parādhīna heba e Bhāratabarṣa shāsana kaḷa lāgiba.
Arājaka heba ehi Jambudwīpa bhakta hoibe chhāniāñ,
anahuti agni prajwaḷita heba uchhanna sārā duniā.
Chhinichhatra heba Oḍishā maṇḍaḷa thaya na paḍibe kehi,
Chakaḍā maḍāṇa prakāsha kariṇa dīna Achyuta kuhai.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Saturn nkag mus rau Pisces, lub sijhawm kev puas tsuaj rau tib neeg yuav pib. Txoj kev tswj hwm kev ywj pheej yuav vau, thiab Is Nrias teb yuav poob nws txoj kev ywj pheej—tej zaum yuav raug lwm tus tswj hwm. Kev kub ntxhov yuav tshwm sim rau txoj kev tswj hwm, thiab lub teb chaws yuav poob rau hauv kev tsis muaj kev cai. Cov neeg ntseeg siab yuav ntshai heev thaum pom cov xwm txheej no. Hluav taws kub ntawm kev puas tsuaj yuav tsis yog hlawv Odisha thiab Is Nrias teb xwb, tiam sis thoob plaws ntiaj teb.

Achyutananda ji sau hauv nws phau ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକୁ ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଗମନ ହେଲେ ଏମନ୍ତ ଭିଆଣ,
ଭାରା ନିବାରଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଦେବରାୟ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେ ଉହ୍ଲେଇବେ ଜାଣ।
କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna rāshiku Shanishchara gamana hele emanta bhiāṇa,
bhārā nibāraṇe prabhu debarāya martye uhleibe jāṇa.
Kalkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣatrīmānaṅku nikṣatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, txhawm rau txhiv ntiaj teb kom dim ntawm kev txhaum, Tus Tswv Mahavishnu yuav los yug ua neeg nyob hauv ntiaj teb. Thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani Yoga, Nws yuav hloov ua Kalki, Tus Tswv Siab Tshaj Plaws yuav mus ncig thoob teb chaws thiab rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab cov neeg txhaum, Nws yuav cawm Niam Ntiaj Teb.

Mahapurush sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଭାବ ବିନୋଦିଆ ମୂର୍ତ୍ତିଙ୍କୁ ଭାବ,
ଭାବରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ ଚୁମ୍ବିବ।
ବାଇଆ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଗୀର,
ବାରହାତ ଖଣ୍ଡା ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବିରାଟ ଗାଦୀ ସଂହାର।
ଭକତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ,
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହୋଇ ପୃଥବୀ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ ବିଶେଷ।
ମାୟାରେ ବୁଲେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଣାକାର ହୃଦରେ ଘୋଷ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ଦୃଶ୍ୟ।
ଯୋବରା ଘାଟର ଲୀଳା,
ଜଗିଛି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ କରି ହେଳା,
କଟିବ ପାପୀଙ୍କ ଗଳା।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
bhāba binodiā mūrttiṅku bhāba,
bhābare Achyutī dāsa chumbiba.
Bāiā Achyutī gīra,
bārahāta khaṇḍā heba bāhara,
birāṭa gādī saṃhāra.
Bhakata Achyutī dāsa,
bhūmikampa hoi pṛthabī ushwāsa,
bhakta rahibe bisheṣa.
Māyāre bule Achyuta,
mantra aṇākāra hṛdare ghoṣa,
mīna shani heba dṛshya.
Jobarā ghāṭara līḷā,
jagichhi Achyuta na kari heḷā,
kaṭiba pāpīṅka gaḷā.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav tshwm sim. Kev ua tsov rog hnyav yuav tshwm sim hauv Virat Gadi lossis Choudwar ntawm Cuttack, thiab rab ntaj kaum ob tes yuav tua cov neeg txhaum. Av qeeg loj yuav tshwm sim uas yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg phem. Kev sim siab rau txhua tus neeg ua txuj yuav tshwm sim ntawm Jobra Ghat ntawm Cuttack, thiab Niam Vajtswv yuav haus lawv cov ntshav. Thaum lub sijhawm no, cov neeg ntseeg siab yuav raug tiv thaiv. Mahapurush tau sau tias cov xwm txheej no yuav tshwm sim hauv Meena-Shani Yoga.

Achyutananda Das ji sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Tattvabodhini’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକି ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଯିବ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ନଷ୍ଟ ଭ୍ରଷ୍ଟ କରିଦେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna rāshiki Shanishchara jiba,
Oḍishā naṣṭa bhraṣṭa karideba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tom qab Saturn nkag mus rau Pisces, lub xeev Odisha yuav raug puas tsuaj.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das rov sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ଦେଶକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହେବ,
ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ସେଠାବରେ ହେବ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ମନେ ଭାବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna shani yoga deshaku paḍiba tera dina pakṣa heba,
gupata māruṇī seṭhābare heba Rāmachandra mane bhāba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Meena-Shani Yoga yuav tshwm sim, thiab yuav muaj kaum peb hnub. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, tib neeg yuav tuag zais cia, thiab Dharma yuav raug tsim tsa.

Ntxiv mus hauv ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, nws sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଚଳିଯିବ କଳି ଗୋଳ ଉପୁଜିବ ବାବୁ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହୋଇଥିବ ଗୁପତ ରଖିବୁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chaḷi jiba kaḷi goḷa upujiba bābu,
mīna shani hoithiba gupata rakhibu.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani Yoga, Kaliyuga yuav xaus thiab Dharma yuav raug tsim tsa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das rov sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଛାରଖାର ହେବ ଏ ସୁନା ସଂସାର ଭଇରବୀ ଦେବ ଡାକ,
ଅକାଳ ମରଣ ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ମୀନ ଶନିଯୋଗ ହେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chārakhāra heba e sunā saṃsāra bhairabī deba ḍāka,
akāḷa maraṇa gupata māruṇī mīna shaniyoga heba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, lub ntiaj teb no yuav raug "puas tsuaj" lossis rhuav tshem tag nrho. Niam Bhairavi yuav qw nrov heev; ntau tus neeg txhaum yuav tuag ua ntej lub sijhawm, thiab ntau tus yuav tuag zais cia. Tag nrho cov kev puas tsuaj no yuav tshwm sim thaum Meena-Shani Yoga, txhais tau tias thaum Saturn nyob hauv lub hnub qub Pisces.

Los ntawm cov nqe Malika uas tau hais los saum no, nws yog qhov tseeb tias thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani Yoga, Tus Tswv Kalki txoj kev ua phem yuav muaj zog tshaj plaws. Thaum lub sijhawm no, thoob plaws ntiaj teb yuav pom muaj kev puas tsuaj loj heev thiab tsis tau muaj dua li. Tsuas yog kev quaj qw, kev ua tsov rog, kev kub ntxhov, kev ntxhov siab, thiab kev puas tsuaj ntuj tsim yuav pom thoob plaws. Thaum lub sijhawm no, cov neeg phem thiab cov neeg tsis ncaj ncees yuav raug rhuav tshem tawm ntawm thoob plaws ntiaj teb.

5.2 Hauv Meena-Shani twg Tus Tswv Kalki txoj Leela yuav tshwm sim?

Tom qab paub los ntawm cov nqe lus saum toj no tias Kalki txoj kev ua phem yuav tshwm sim thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani, cov lus nug loj tshaj plaws yog: cov xwm txheej no yuav tshwm sim thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani twg?

Meena-Shani Yoga tshwm sim ib zaug txhua 30 xyoo. Yog li ntawd, nws yog qhov tsim nyog yuav tsum paub tias Meena-Shani twg, raws li Malika, yuav pom cov xwm txheej ntawm kev tsim tsa Dharma.

Thaum saib kom zoo, peb pom tias Saturn nyob hauv Pisces xyoo 1995; tam sim no, xyoo 2025, Saturn tau rov nkag mus rau Pisces dua; tom qab ntawd xyoo 2054, thiab kwv yees li 30 xyoo tom qab ntawd, xyoo 2084, Saturn yuav rov hla mus rau Pisces dua.

Meena-Shani twg?

Nws tau sau meej meej hauv Bhavishya Malika tias thaum cov xwm txheej no tshwm sim nyob rau hauv ob xyoos ib nrab ntawm Meena-Shani, lub npe ntawm Vaj Ntxwv Puri, Odisha, thaum lub sijhawm ntawd yuav yog Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chandra Kalpa’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଥିବ ସେହି ବେଳର,
ପୃଥବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ଘନ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତର ଏ ପ୍ରଳୟ ରାଶି,
ଭୂଭାରାକୁ ରାମ ଯିବ ରେ ଗ୍ରାସୀ।
ସେକାଳେ ମୀନେ ଶନି ଥିବ ଚଲି,
ନାଶିବ କାଳ ହେବ ଜଳ ଖେଳି।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ଗୁରୁ ସ୍ମରିଣ,
ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳ୍ପରେ ମୁଁ କଲି ବଖାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dibyasiṃha thiba sehi beḷara,
pṛthabī hoijiba ghana andhāra.
pañchabhūtara e pralaya rāśi,
bhūbhārāku rāma jiba re grāsī.
sekāḷe mīne śani thiba chali,
nāśiba kāḷa heba jaḷa kheḷi.
Achyuta kahai guru smariṇa,
chandra kalpare muñ kali bakhāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Saturn nyob hauv Pisces, thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, kev tsaus ntuj nti yuav npog ntiaj teb rau 7 hnub thiab 7 hmo. Kev puas tsuaj los ntawm tsib yam (Ntiaj Teb, Dej, Hluav Taws, Cua, thiab Ether) yuav rhuav tshem thoob plaws ntiaj teb. Mahapurush tau sau tias thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, lub npe ntawm Vaj Ntxwv Puri yuav yog ‘Divyasingha’.

Tam sim no muaj qee yam tseem ceeb uas yuav tsum nco ntsoov ntawm no. Vaj Ntxwv Puri tam sim no, Shri Divyasingha Deva (IV), tam sim no muaj kwv yees li 72 xyoo. Hauv Meena-Shani tom ntej—uas yog, 30 xyoo tom ntej no—nws yuav muaj 100+ xyoo thiab yuav luag tsis muaj peev xwm tias nws tseem yuav nyob hauv lub zwm txwv ntawm Puri thaum lub sijhawm ntawd!

Tam sim no qee tus neeg, uas hais tias lub sijhawm cov xwm txheej yuav yog Meena-Shani tom ntej (uas yog, xyoo 2054–2057), qhia tias tej zaum cov xwm txheej no yuav tshwm sim thaum muaj lwm tus vaj ntxwv hu ua Divyasingha! Tab sis kuv yuav qhia rau koj tias qhov no tsis muaj peev xwm. Vaj Ntxwv Puri tam sim no yog tus Vaj Ntxwv thib 19, thiab raws li Malika, tus Vaj Ntxwv thib 19 yuav yog tus Vaj Ntxwv kawg ntawm Kaliyuga. Qhov ntawd yog, raws li Malika, tom qab Vaj Ntxwv Shri Divyasingha Deva tam sim no, yuav tsis muaj Vaj Ntxwv Puri ntxiv lawm.

Kev sib tham saum toj no ua pov thawj tias tag nrho cov xwm txheej puas tsuaj yuav tshwm sim thaum Meena-Shani Yoga ntawm xyoo 2025–2028, thaum Vaj Ntxwv Shri Divyasingha Deva IV tseem tswj hwm.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das rov sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ବହୁତ ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ପାପର ଭାରା,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ହୋଇବେ ଧରା।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରେ ପାଣି ପଶିବ,
ସିଂହ ଦ୍ୱାରେ ରକ୍ତଧାର ବହିବ।
ଟିକରା ଗୋହିରୀ ହୋଇବ ରଡ଼ି,
ଧାନ ଥିବ ଯିବ କଣ୍ଠା ଅଗାଡ଼ି।
ସେବେଳକୁ ଏକା ଅକ୍ଷର ସାର,
ସପତ ଦିନ ଯେ ହେବ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ଗାଲୁଆ ମାଲୁଆ ଭକତ ଛନ୍ତି,
ବଗୁଲିଆ କର୍ମେ ପେଟ ପୋଷନ୍ତି।
ଏମାନେ ଯେ ଦେବୀ ଖପରରେ ଯିବେ,
ଭକତମାନେ ଆନନ୍ଦରେ ରହିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bahuta anyāya pāpara bhārā,
Dibyasiṃha deba hoibe dharā.
Baḍa deuḷare pāṇi pashiba,
siṃhadvāre raktadhāra bahiba.
ṭikarā gohirī hoiba raḍi,
dhāna thiba jiba kaṇṭhā agāḍi.
sebeḷaku ekā akṣara sāra,
sapata dina je heba andhāra.
gāluā māluā bhakata chanti,
baguliā karme peṭa poṣanti.
emāne je debī khaparare jibe,
bhaktamāne ānandare rahibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, yuav muaj kev tsis ncaj ncees ntau thiab kev txhaum hnyav heev. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, dej hiav txwv yuav nkag mus rau hauv Jagannath Temple. Kev ua tsov rog yuav tshwm sim hauv lub tuam tsev. Ntshav yuav ntws los ntawm Lion's Gate (Singhadwara). Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, kev tsaus ntuj yuav npog thoob plaws ntiaj teb rau xya hnub thiab xya hmo. Tag nrho cov neeg ntseeg siab uas ua txuj, uas khwv noj khwv haus los ntawm kev ua phem, yuav raug rhuav tshem los ntawm Niam Vajtswv. Cov neeg ntseeg siab yuav siv lawv lub sijhawm nyob hauv kev zoo siab. Thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, Vaj Ntxwv Puri yuav yog Shri Divyasingha Deva, uas yuav raug ntes lossis raug nyiag los ntawm cov neeg tsim txom.

Nws yog qhov tseeb heev los ntawm cov nqe lus saum toj no tias thaum cov xwm txheej ntawm kev tsim tsa Dharma tshwm sim, thiab thaum muaj kev tsaus ntuj rau xya hnub thiab xya hmo hauv ntiaj teb, Vaj Ntxwv Puri thaum lub sijhawm ntawd yuav yog Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Lwm tus Mahapurush los ntawm Odisha yug hauv xyoo pua 20 tau sau hauv ib phau ntawv tias tom qab xyoo 2033, lub teeb ntawm lub sijhawm tshiab (Satyayuga) yuav tshwm sim; txhais tau tias, raws li Mahapurush, tag nrho cov Leela ntawm kev hloov pauv ntawm lub sijhawm yuav tiav ua ntej xyoo 2033. Peb yuav nthuav tawm cov nqe lus cuam tshuam txog qhov no rau koj hauv Tshooj 12. Lub ntsiab lus yog tias tag nrho cov xwm txheej puas tsuaj yuav tshwm sim ua ntej xyoo 2033.

Mahapurush Bhima Bhoi (tus neeg ntseeg siab-kws sau paj huam dig muag yug hauv Sambalpur, Odisha hauv xyoo pua 19) tau sau txog qhov no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Padma Kalpa’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ନିଶ୍ଚୟ କଳଙ୍କି ହୋଇବ, ଯେ ଦିନ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ।
ପଞ୍ଚଗ୍ରହ ଯେ ଯୁକ୍ତ ହେବେ, ଆଗାମୀ ଅଢ଼ାଇର ଯୋଗେ।
ତଦନ୍ତେ ଷଡ଼ ଗ୍ରହ ଆସି, ଶନି କୁମ୍ଭରୁ ଯିବେ ଖସି।
ପ୍ରବେଶ ମୀନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ, ଟାକିଥାଅ ହେ ସୁଜ୍ଞ ନରେ।
ହୋଇବ ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ ସମର, ଦମନ ହୋଇବ ବାହାର।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ନାଶିବ ଅବହେଳେ, ଜଗତେ ରଖି ବାହୁବଳେ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛଙ୍କୁ ଦମନ ନାଶିବ, ଦୁର୍ଗା ଖପର ପକାଇବ।
ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ କଳି ସେ ଦମନ, ଶୁଣ ହେ ସାଧୁ ସୁଜ୍ଞଜନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Nishchaya Kaḷaṅki hoiba, je dina mīna shani bhoga.
Pañchagraha je yukta hebe, āgāmī aḍhāira joge.
Tadante ṣaḍa graha āsi, shani kumbharu jibe khasi.
Prabesha mīna saṅgatare, ṭākithāa he sujña nare.
Hoiba asaṃkhya samara, damana hoiba bāhāra.
Biṣṇu nāshiba abaheḷe, jagate rakhi bāhubaḷe.
Mlechchaṅku damana nāshiba, Durgā khapara pakāiba.
Asaṃkhya kaḷi se damana, shuṇa he sādhu sujñajana.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Saturn nkag mus rau Pisces, Tus Tswv Kalki txoj kev ua phem yuav tshwm sim. Lub sijhawm no yuav kav ob xyoos ib nrab. Thaum lub sijhawm no, kev ua tsov rog tsis suav yuav tshwm sim ntawm ntau lub teb chaws. Tus Tswv Kalki yuav tiv thaiv cov neeg ntseeg siab, thiab Niam Durga yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab cov neeg txhaum.

Hauv cov nqe lus saum toj no, Mahapurush tau hais meej tias Meena-Shani twg Kalki txoj kev ua phem yuav tshwm sim. Piav qhia txog tus kheej ntawm Meena-Shani ntawd, nws hais tias thaum hnub Saturn nkag mus rau Pisces, 5 lub hnub qub yuav twb nyob hauv Pisces lawm. Qhov ntawd yog, hnub ntawd, tag nrho 6 lub hnub qub yuav nyob hauv Pisces—5 lub hnub qub twb nyob ntawd lawm thiab ib lub Saturn, tag nrho yog 6 lub hnub qub.

Yog tias peb saib Meena-Shani xyoo 2025, peb pom tias Mahapurush cov lus hais yog qhov tseeb heev. Thaum Lub Peb Hlis 29, 2025, hnub Saturn hla mus rau Pisces, tsib lub hnub qub (Rahu, Venus, Mercury, Sun, thiab Moon) twb nyob hauv Pisces lawm.

Tam sim no, yog tias peb saib Meena-Shani xyoo 2054, peb pom tias thaum Lub Tsib Hlis 14, 2054, hnub Saturn hla mus rau Pisces, tsuas yog 2 lub hnub qub (Venus thiab Mars) yuav nyob hauv Pisces ua ntej, thiab nrog Saturn, nws ua rau muaj 3 lub hnub qub. Whereas raws li Saint Bhima Bhoi, yuav tsum muaj tag nrho 6 lub hnub qub nyob hauv Pisces, tsis yog 3.

Yog tias peb saib Meena-Shani Yoga xyoo 2084, peb pom tias thaum Lub Peb Hlis 20, 2084, hnub Saturn hla mus rau Pisces, tsuas yog 2 lub hnub qub (Mercury thiab Sun) yuav nyob hauv Pisces ua ntej. Txawm suav nrog Saturn, nws yog 3 lub hnub qub. Qhov no txhais tau tias kev hla ntawm 6 lub hnub qub tsis tshwm sim ntawm no ib yam nkaus.

Nws yog qhov tseeb tias Mahapurush tsis yog hais txog Meena-Shani Yoga xyoo 2054 lossis 2084. Lub Meena-Shani uas nws hais txog hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Padma Kalpa’ yog Meena-Shani xyoo 2025.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das kuj tau hais hauv nws phau ntawv Malika tias leej twg yuav yog tus Mahant (Tus Thawj Coj) ntawm nws lub Padmaban Peeth nyob hauv Nemalo, Cuttack, thaum cov kev ua phem no tshwm sim. Nws sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳରେ,
ନବ୍ୟକ୍ତ କଥା କହିଲି ରାମ ହେତୁ ଚିତ୍ତରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta kaḷi śeṣa beḷare,
Nabyakta kathā kahili rāma hetu chittare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Au tus thwj tim Ramadas, thaum Kaliyuga xaus, thaum lub sijhawm ntawd ib tug neeg hu ua Gaganānanda yuav yog tus Thawj Coj ntawm kuv lub Peeth.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau rau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ ଗଗନେ ଉଡ଼ିବ ଧୂଳି,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶ ଯେ ଚମକି ପଡ଼ିବ ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ହାଲହୁଳି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta hoiba gagane uḍiba dhūḷi,
Oḍishā desha je chamaki paḍiba paḍijiba hālahuḷi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov kev ua Vajtswv yuav tshwm sim thaum Mahant Gaganānanda lub sijhawm. Thaum ntawd, yuav muaj kev quaj ntsuag hauv lub xeev Odisha.

5.3 Mahant Gaganānanda Goswami

Nws yog ib qho tseem ceeb uas nyob rau tiam 14 ntawm Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, nws tus xeeb ntxwv thiab tam sim no muaj 85–90 xyoo Shri Gaganānanda Goswamiji yog tus Thawj Coj ntawm Mahapurush lub chaw Vajtswv dawb huv, Padmaban, Nemalo. Qhov no kuj ua rau nws pom tseeb tias tus Mahapurush tab tom hais txog lub sijhawm tam sim no.

Los ntawm plaub lub ntsiab lus saum toj no, nws pom tseeb tias raws li Malika, tus Meena-Shani uas Kalki cov kev ua Vajtswv rhuav tshem yuav tshwm sim yuav tuaj ua ntej xyoo 2033, thiab thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani ntawd, Shri Divyasingha Deva yuav yog Vajntxwv ntawm Puri thiab Shri Gaganānanda yuav yog tus Mahant ntawm Padmaban Peeth, Nemalo. Tsis yog li ntawd xwb, tiam sis hnub Saturn hloov mus rau Pisces, ib lub Shad-graha (6-lub ntiaj teb) Yoga yuav tsim nyob rau hauv Pisces.

Nws pom tseeb tias tus Mahapurush tab tom taw qhia rau tus Meena-Shani uas yuav tuaj txog ntawm 2025–2028 vim tias tag nrho plaub lub ntsiab lus saum toj no tsuas yog siv rau tus Meena-Shani Yoga no thiab tsis muaj lwm tus.

Tsis tas yuav hais tias raws li Malika, cov xwm txheej loj heev uas Vajtswv Kalki yuav ua yuav tshwm sim ntawm 2025–2028.

Nco ntsoov: Hnub no qee tus neeg tab tom hais tias tus Meena-Shani uas tau hais hauv Malika tsis yog tus Meena-Shani tam sim no, tiam sis yog tus tom ntej uas yuav tuaj txog xyoo 2054! Nws yuav tsim nyog rau txhua tus ntseeg Vajtswv uas muaj kev ntseeg hauv Malika kom tsis txhob tsis meej pem los ntawm qhov no, vim tias cov lus zoo li no yog qhov tsis raug tag nrho.

6 Vaj Ntxwv ntawm Puri thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga

Hauv tshooj dhau los, peb tau pom tias tag nrho cov xwm txheej tseem ceeb ntawm kev tsim Dharma yuav tshwm sim thaum Vajntxwv ntawm Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, kav. Hauv tshooj no, peb yuav nkag siab qhov no los ntawm qee cov nqe lus Malika ntxiv.

Ib tug ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Jagannath Das, tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kali Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ପୁରୁଷୋତ୍ତମ ଦେବ ରାଜା ଠାରୁ,
ଉନ୍ନବିଂଶ ରାଜା ହେବ ସେଠାରୁ।
ସେଠାରୁ ପୁଣ ରାଜା ନାହିଁ ଆଉ,
ଅକୁଳି ହୋଇବେ କୁଳକୁ ବହୁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Puruṣottama Deba rājā ṭhāru,
unnabiṃsha rājā heba seṭhāru.
Seṭhāru puṇa rājā nāhiṃ āu,
akuḷi hoibe kuḷaku bahu.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, pib txij Vajntxwv Purushottama Deva tom qab Vajntxwv Indradyumna, tag nrho 19 tus vajntxwv yuav los ua Vajntxwv ntawm Jagannath Puri nyob rau lub sijhawm sib txawv. Tus Mahapurush tau sau tias tus Vajntxwv 19 yuav tsis muaj tub thiab nws yuav yog tus vajntxwv kawg; txhais tau tias, tsis muaj lwm tus neeg yuav los ua vajntxwv tom qab nws.

Yog tias peb saib qhov xwm txheej tam sim no, peb pom tias tus Vajntxwv ntawm Puri tam sim no yog tus Vajntxwv 19 thiab kuj tsis muaj tub. Tus Mahapurush qhov qhia meej yog tias tag nrho Kalki cov kev ua Vajtswv ntawm kev tsim Dharma yuav tshwm sim thaum tus vajntxwv tam sim no kav.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Adi Samhita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନାମେ ରାୟେ,
ଏ ଯୁଗ ପ୍ରାନ୍ତେ ସେ ଉଦୟେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shri Dibyasiṃha nāme rāye,
e juga prānte se udaye.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, Shri Divyasingha yuav yog Vajntxwv ntawm Puri.

Hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika, nws tau sau:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଅଙ୍କେ ବାବୁ ସରବ ଦେଖିବୁ,
ଛାଡ଼ି ଚକା ଗଲୁ ବୋଲି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣିବୁ,
ନର ବାଳୁତ ରୂପରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମିବୁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dibyasiṃha aṅke bābu saraba dekhibu,
chhāḍi chakā galu boli nishchaya jānibu,
nara bāḷuta rūpare āmbhe janamibu.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, thaum Vajntxwv Shri Divyasingha Deva kav hauv Puri, thaum ntawd tag nrho Vajtswv Kalki cov kev ua Vajtswv yuav tshwm sim. Thaum ntawd, Vajtswv yuav tawm ntawm Nws lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath thiab yug los ua tib neeg lub cev ua tus Kalki Avatar.

Tom qab ntawd hauv phau ntawv Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, Achyutanandaji sau:

Odia Sloka:

ବୃଦ୍ଧ ବାଳକ ରାଜା ହୋଇଥିବ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସଣ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବ।
ବାଳବୃଦ୍ଧ ଅଙ୍କେ ବତାସ ହେବ,
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ।
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୀନମାନ ବହୁ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bruddha bāḷaka rājā hoithiba,
gupatare saṇa sāla chāliba.
bāḷabruddha aṅke batāsha heba,
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba.
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba,
pṛthibī hīnamāna bahu hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, ib tug “Baalak-Briddha” yuav yog Vajntxwv ntawm Puri; qhov ntawd yog, ib tug neeg yuav yog tus vajntxwv uas tau los ua vajntxwv thaum yau thiab nyob hauv txoj haujlwm ntawd mus txog thaum laus. Thaum nws kav, cov xwm txheej xws li dej nyab thiab cua daj cua dub yuav tshwm sim.

Nws paub tias ntawm cov vajntxwv uas tau ua vajntxwv txog tam sim no, tsis muaj leej twg tsuas yog Shri Divyasingha Deva tau ua vajntxwv txij thaum yau mus txog thaum laus. Tus Vajntxwv tam sim no Shri Divyasingha Deva tau los ua vajntxwv rau Lub Xya Hli 7, 1970, thaum muaj hnub nyoog tsuas yog 17 xyoo. Thaum nyob hauv txoj haujlwm no, nws tau laus lawm thiab tam sim no muaj hnub nyoog txog 72 xyoo. Los ntawm nqe lus no, hauv cov lus cim, tus Mahapurush tau qhia tias Divyasingha Deva yuav yog tus vajntxwv thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga.

Achyutananda Dasji tau sau ntxiv hauv nws ‘Kalikalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ଅଙ୍କରେ ଜାଣ,
ଧୂମକେତୁ ହେବ ପୂର୍ବଭାଗେଣ।
ଉଲ୍‌କାପାତ ହେବ ହାଲହୁଳି,
ଶ୍ୱାନ ଶୃଗାଳ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ବୋବାଳି।
ମାତିବେ କାଳୀ ଯେ କରାଳୀଗଣ,
ଲୋକେ ମରିବେ ହୋଇ ରଣଭଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dibyasiṃha Deba aṅkare jāṇa,
dhūmaketu heba pūrbabhāgeṇa.
ulkāpāta heba hālahuḷi,
shwāna shṛgāḷa chhāḍibe bobāḷi.
mātibe kāḷī je karāḷīgaṇa,
loke maribe hoi raṇabhaṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, thaum Shri Divyasingha Deva yog Vajntxwv ntawm Puri, thaum lub sijhawm ntawd yuav muaj ib lub hnub qub pom nyob rau sab hnub tuaj. Yuav muaj hnub qub poob thoob plaws ntiaj teb. Cov tsiaj qus noj nqaij xws li dev thiab hma yuav tawm hauv hav zoov thiab mus rau thaj chaw nyob. Niam Kali thiab nws cov phooj ywg Yoginis yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg txhaum hauv ntiaj teb, ua rau muaj neeg coob tuag.

Tus Mahapurush tau qhia luv luv los ntawm nqe lus saum toj no tias cov xwm txheej ntawm kev tsim Dharma yuav tshwm sim thaum tus Vajntxwv Puri hu ua Shri Divyasingha Deva kav thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga.

Tus Mahapurush tau hais rau nws tus thwjtim Ramadas hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika:

Odia Sloka:

କେଉଁ କାଳ ବୋଲି ବୋଇଲୁ ଶୁଣ ରାମ ରତନ,
ଥିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ରାଜନ କୋଳେ କନ୍ୟା ରତନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Keuñ kāḷa boli boilu śuṇa Rāma ratana,
thiba Dibyasiṃha rājana koḷe kanyā ratana.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, Au tus thwjtim Ramadas, mloog seb kuv tab tom hais txog lub sijhawm twg—thaum cov xwm txheej no tshwm sim, Vajntxwv Divyasingha yuav kav, uas yuav tsis muaj tub; tsuas muaj ntxhais xwb.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv ib phau ntawv Malika:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ କରିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପତି,
ତାଙ୍କ କୁଳେ ପୁତ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭଣେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bāra bāra beḷaku kariba Dibyasiṃha nṛpati,
tāṅka kuḷe putra nohiba bhaṇe dīna Achyutī.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, yuav muaj Vajntxwv Divyasingha uas yuav tsis muaj tub.

Qhov kev qhia ua ntej no ntawm tus Mahapurush kuj haum rau tus Vajntxwv Puri tam sim no, Shri Divyasingha Deva, zoo kawg nkaus. Tus Vajntxwv tam sim no tsis muaj tub; nws tsuas muaj plaub tug ntxhais xwb.

Hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika, ‘Kaliyuga Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau cov lus uas Vajtswv Krishna tau hais rau Arjuna hauv Dwaparayuga, uas Vajtswv Krishna tau hais tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shrikshetra rājana mora na sebi payara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, Vajntxwv ntawm Puri yuav tsis ua kuv (hauv daim ntawv Jagannath) txoj haujlwm txhua hnub.

Achyutananda Dasji tom qab ntawd sau:

Odia Sloka:

ନ ଥିବ ରାଜନ ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ମଉଡ଼ ମଣି,
ରାଜ୍ୟ ରାଜକୁଳ କନ୍ୟାକୁ ନ କରିବ ବର୍ଣ୍ଣି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Na thiba rājana Shrīkṣetre kṣetra mauḍa maṇi,
rājya rājakuḷa kanyāku na kariba barṇṇi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, Vajntxwv ntawm Puri yuav tsis nyob hauv Shrikshetra lossis Puri, tiam sis yuav nyob rau lwm qhov chaw. Nws yuav tsis yuav ib tug ntxhais huab tais ntawm Odisha tsev neeg huab tais tiam sis yuav yuav ib tug ntxhais huab tais los ntawm lwm lub xeev.

Ib tug neeg dawb huv loj ntawm Odisha yug nyob rau xyoo pua 18, Mahapurush Arakshita Das, tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Dwadasa Chandrika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଆସିବେ ଫେରି,
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରକୁ ନ ଯାଇ ରହିବେ ଜାଣ ସେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି।
ଭେଦ ମୁଁ କାହୁଁ ଜାଣିଥାନ୍ତି ଆଦିମାତା କହିଲେ,
ଭଣେ ଅରକ୍ଷିତ ଅଧମ ତହୁଁ ଶୁଣିଲି ଭଲେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati nishchaya āsibe pheri,
kṣhetraku na jāi rahibe jāṇa se Khaṇḍagiri.
bheda muñ kāhuñ jāṇithānti Ādimātā kahile,
bhaṇe Arakṣhita adhama tahuñ śuṇili bhale.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus Mahapurush sau tias thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, Divyasingha Deva yuav los ua Vajntxwv ntawm Puri thiab nws yuav tsis nyob hauv Shrikshetra lossis Puri, tiam sis yuav nyob hauv Khandagiri cheeb tsam lossis Bhubaneswar. Tus Mahapurush sau ntxiv tias nws tus kheej yuav ua li cas thiaj paub cov lus zais ntawm cov teeb meem no; Niam Qub Qab (Adi Mata) tau qhia nws cov lus no.

Cia kuv qhia rau cov nyeem tias tag nrho cov vajntxwv ua ntej tus Vajntxwv tam sim no ib txwm nyob hauv Puri, Odisha, thiab ua Vajtswv Jagannath txoj haujlwm txhua hnub, tiam sis tus Vajntxwv tam sim no Shri Divyasingha Deva tsuas ua Vajtswv Jagannath txoj haujlwm rau qee lub sijhawm tshwj xeeb xwb. Nws tsis nyob hauv Puri tiam sis nyob hauv Shahid Nagar, Bhubaneswar. Tsis tas li ntawd, Shri Divyasingha Deva tsis tau yuav ib tug ntxhais huab tais los ntawm Odisha tiam sis tau yuav tus ntxhais ntawm Lieutenant Colonel Anant Singh Charak ntawm Jammu.

Plaub qhov kev qhia ua ntej saum toj no haum rau tus Vajntxwv Puri tam sim no, Shri Divyasingha Deva, zoo kawg nkaus. Qhov no kuj yog ib qho pov thawj tias Kaliyuga yuav xaus thaum Vajntxwv Shri Divyasingha Deva kav.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Māgha Saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājattwa karibe ehi Ekāmra Nagara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, thaum Divyasingha Deva yog Vajntxwv ntawm Puri, ces Ananta Keshari, piv txwv li, Vajtswv Kalki, yuav kav. Nws yuav yog hnub Magha Saptami; Bhubaneswar (Ekambra Nagar) yuav yog qhov chaw.

Nqe lus no kuj ua pov thawj tias Vajntxwv ntawm Puri thaum Kalki cov kev ua Vajtswv yuav tsis yog lwm tus tsuas yog Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Mohakalpa’, Achyutanandaji tau qhia ib qho cim ntxiv ua kom pom tseeb tias Shri Divyasingha Deva yuav yog tus vajntxwv kawg ntawm Puri. Tus Mahapurush sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଧନୁଶର ଧରି ଆଗେ ସେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ଯେ ଜାଣ ବୈଷ୍ଣବ ଧର୍ମ ଛାଡିବେ।
କ୍ଷତ୍ରି ବୃତ୍ତି ଧରି ପଦାତିଙ୍କ ସହ କରିବେ ବହୁ ସମର,
ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କ ଦେଖି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଡିବେ ପଦ୍ମପାଦର।
ନବଲକ୍ଷ ଧର୍ମ-ହୀନ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ତହିଁ ତୁରିତେ ହେବେ ଛେଦନ,
ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଘାତେ ତହିଁ ମେଦିନୀ ଯେ କମ୍ପମାନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଠାରୁ ଆଦି ଓଡିଶା ରାଜ୍ୟରେ ଯେତେକ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଥିବେ,
ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେଇ ସମସ୍ତେ ତହିଁ ମରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ର ପୁଛିବେ,
ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ହତ୍ୟା ବୋଲି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ କହି ବଧ୍ୟ ନ କରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପରୀକ୍ଷା କରିବେ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ଜାଣ,
ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣଟି ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେବ ନାଶିବ ଯେ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ ଚକ୍ର ଶୂନ୍ୟ ଗଗନେ ରଖିବେ,
ଚଉଷଠି ଅକ୍ଷର ପ୍ରମାଣ ନ ପାଇ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଥାଇ ଛେଦିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Lalāṭa Kesharī dhanushara dhari āge se juddha karibe,
Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati je jāṇa baiṣṇaba dharma chhāḍibe.
Kṣatri bṛutti dhari padātiṅka saha karibe bahu samara,
Prabhuṅka dekhi Dibyasiṃha Deba paḍibe padmapādara.
Nabalakṣa dharma-hīna byakti tahiṃ turite hebe chhedana,
Lalāṭa Kesharī juddha ghāte tahiṃ medinī je kampamāna.
Kṣetra ṭhāru ādi Oḍishā rājyare jeteka brāhmaṇa thibe,
Gāyatrī mantraku parīkṣā na dei samaste tahiṃ maribe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba brāhmaṇamānaṅku Gāyatrī mantra puchhibe,
Brāhmaṇa hatyā boli brāhmaṇamānaṅku kahi badhya na karibe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba parīkṣā karibe Gāyatrī mantraku jāṇa,
Jeuṃ brāhmaṇaṭi parīkṣā na deba nāshiba je Sudarshana.
Ananta Kesharī Sudarshana chakra shūnya gagane rakhibe,
Chauṣaṭhi akṣara pramāṇa na pāi shūnyare thāi chhedibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, Vajntxwv ntawm Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, yuav tso nws txoj Vaishnava Dharma tseg thiab coj txoj haujlwm Kshatriya los tawm tsam ntau yam kev ua tsov rog. Thaum pom Vajtswv Kalki, nws yuav poob rau Vajtswv ko taw, tau txais Vajtswv cov koob hmoov, thiab rhuav tshem cuaj lab tus neeg tsis ntseeg. Hauv cheeb tsam Jagannath, Puri, tag nrho cov Brahmins uas muaj yuav raug sim nrog Gayatri Mantra. Vim ntshai txoj kev txhaum ntawm kev tua Brahmin, Divyasingha Deva yuav tsis tua cov Brahmins uas ua tsis tau qhov kev sim. Tus Mahapurush sau tias tag nrho lawv yuav raug rhuav tshem los ntawm Vajtswv tus Sudarshana Chakra nws tus kheej. Vajtswv yuav muab Nws tus Sudarshana Chakra tso rau saum ntuj dawb paug thiab txiav caj dab cov Brahmins ntawd nrog tib tus chakra uas tsis nyeem Gayatri Mantra.

Ntawm no, tus Mahapurush muab pov thawj tias Vajntxwv ntawm Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, yuav ntsib Vajtswv Kalki thiab pab tsim Dharma. Nws yog qhov tseeb tias Vajtswv Kalki txoj kev ua si rhuav tshem yuav tshwm sim thaum Shri Divyasingha Deva tseem kav.

Tom qab ntawd tus Mahapurush sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kalpa Tika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିୟ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ କରିଥିବେ ସେ ବନ୍ଧନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ନୃପତି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଯେ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଯେ ରଖିଥିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଗୋପନ।
ବନ୍ଧନର ଦୁଇ ଦିନରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି,
ରାତ୍ରିକାଳେ ରାଜା ପାଶେ ବିଜେ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ରାଜାର ବନ୍ଧନ ଫେଇ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବାସୁ ଦେବେ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ତଳ ପୁରେ ଗୋପନେ ରଖିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dibyasiṃha Deba se je Kṣhatriya rājana,
tāhānku Phiringī karithibe se bandhana.
Kṣhetrara nṛupati Dibyasiṃha je rājana,
tāhānku je rakhithibe Shrī Hari gopana.
Bandhanara dui dinare Prabhu je Shrī Hari,
rātrikāḷe rājā pāśe bije je Shrī Hari.
rājāra bandhana phei Prabhu bāśu debe,
Khaṇḍagiri taḷa pure gopane rakhibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, thaum Vajntxwv ntawm Puri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, raug tub rog txawv teb chaws ntes, ces ob hnub tom qab, Vajtswv Kalki yuav mus txog tus vajntxwv thaum hmo ntuj, tsom nws dim ntawm kev raug kaw, thiab zais nws rau ntawm ko taw roob Khandagiri.

Tsis tas li ntawd, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Padmakalpa Tika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ମୋ ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ,
ସେହିବେଳେ ସରକାର ଖାଦ୍ୟ ପକାଇବେ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଆଦିକରି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ananta gumphā madhyare mo bhakta rahibe,
sehibeḷe sarakāra khādya pakāibe.
Ananta Keshari swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha Lalāṭa Keshari ādikari.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, thaum muaj kev puas tsuaj loj heev los ntawm dej, Vajtswv Kalki cov neeg pe hawm uas vam khom Nws yuav nyob nyab xeeb hauv lub qhov tsua Ananta uas nyob ntawm roob Khandagiri. Thaum ntawd, tsoom fwv yuav muab zaub mov thiab khoom pab rau cov neeg raug kev txom nyem. Tus Mahapurush sau tias thaum ntawd, Ananta Madhava, uas yog Vajtswv Kalki, yuav nyob hauv Khandagiri, Odisha. Vajntxwv Divyasingha Deva kuj yuav nrog Nws nyob.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau dua ib zaug ntxiv hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kalpa Tika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାବରେ ସେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଦେଖିବେ,
ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହକୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Anaṃta kesharī swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha deba se je lalāṭakesharī.
Khaṇḍagiri ṭhābare se prabhuṃku dekhibe,
Lalāṭakesharī Dibyasiṃhaku chihnibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, thaum Vajtswv Ananta Keshari, uas yog Vajtswv Kalki, nyob hauv Khandagiri, ces thaum ntawd Divyasingha Deva kuj yuav nyob hauv Khandagiri. Hauv Khandagiri, Divyasingha Deva yuav tau pom (Darshan) Vajtswv.

Los ntawm peb nqe lus saum toj no ib yam nkaus, nws paub tias thaum Vajtswv Kalki txoj kev ua si dawb huv, Vajntxwv ntawm Puri yuav yog Divyasingha Deva.

6.1 Vajtswv Kalki thiab Baladeva ntsib Divyasingha Deva thiab ntau tus Vajntxwv

Phau Bhavishya Malika muaj ntau yam dab neeg zais cia thiab tej lus faj lem. Ntawm cov lus faj lem no yog qhov kev kwv yees tias ntau tus vajntxwv yuav ntsib Vajtswv.

Tus Mahapurush sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika tias yav tom ntej, yuav muaj ib lub sijhawm thaum cov tub rog txawv teb chaws yuav tuaj nyob hauv Chhatia, Jajpur mus txog Nilagiri lossis Nilachal, Puri. Txawm tias cov yeeb ncuab cov tub rog loj heev yuav nkag mus rau hauv Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath. Pom tag nrho cov no hauv kev nyuaj siab heev, Vajtswv, uas tab tom ua kev txhiv txom, yuav nco txog Sahadeva, Nakula, Ashwatthama, thiab Shalya. Sai li Vajtswv nco txog lawv, plaub tus tub rog siab loj no, uas yog cov kws tshaj lij hauv kev tua hneev, txoj kev xav thiab kev txhiv txom yuav tawg, thiab tag nrho plaub tus yuav mus txog Chhatia thiab poob rau Vajtswv ko taw, thov Nws cov lus txib. Vajtswv yuav txib plaub tus ntawd kom rhuav tshem cov tub rog Mughal loj heev thiab tiv thaiv cov neeg pe hawm. Raws li Vajtswv cov lus txib, plaub tus ntawd yuav rhuav tshem cov tub rog Mughal thiab tom qab ntawd rov qab mus rau Vajtswv. Thaum ntawd, ntau tus neeg pe hawm yuav nrog Vajtswv nyob hauv Chhatia, thiab rau 27 hnub, Chhatia yuav dhau los ua qhov chaw sib ntsib ntawm cov neeg pe hawm. Lub sijhawm no, cov vajntxwv ntawm Russia, Nepal, Manipur, Ali, Kujang, Kanika, thiab lwm yam, yuav ntsib Vajtswv, tau txais Nws Darshan, thiab muab lawv tus kheej rau Nws ko taw. Thaum ntawd, Vajtswv yuav coj tag nrho lawv mus txog Sealdah thov Vajtswv Baladeva kom rhuav tshem cov tub rog txawv teb chaws uas tseem tshuav. Ua li no, tag nrho cov vajntxwv nrog Vajtswv yuav mus rau Sealdah thiab ntsib Baladeva, qhov chaw uas Arjuna thiab Bhima twb nyob ntawd lawm.

Ntawm no tus Mahapurush sau tias lub sijhawm no, ntawm cov vajntxwv uas yuav ntsib Vajtswv Ananta Keshari thiab Vajtswv Baladeva, Vajntxwv ntawm Puri, Shrikshetra, kuj yuav nyob ntawd. Qhia tias nws yuav yog leej twg, tus Mahapurush sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika tias:

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଯେ ରାଜା ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ତାହାଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗତେ ସ୍ଥିତି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kṣhetre je rājā Dibyasiṃha Deba tāhāṅka saṅgate sthiti.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, ntawm cov vajntxwv uas tau txais Vajtswv Darshan yuav yog Shrikshetra Puri tus Vajntxwv, Divyasingha Deva.

Los ntawm cov kev sib tham saum toj no, nws kuj pom tseeb tias Vajntxwv ntawm Puri thaum Kalki txoj kev ua si dawb huv yuav yog Shri Divyasingha Deva tiag tiag.

Los ntawm 17 nqe lus saum toj no, nws tau pov thawj meej tias thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, thaum Vajtswv Kalki yug los thiab thaum Nws txoj kev ua si rhuav tshem tshwm sim thoob plaws ntiaj teb, thaum kev rhuav tshem cov neeg phem, kev cawm dim ntawm cov neeg pe hawm, thiab kev tsim Dharma tshwm sim, thaum ntawd ib tug vajntxwv hu ua Divyasingha Deva yuav zaum ntawm lub zwm txwv ntawm Jagannath Puri, thiab nws kuj yuav yog tus vajntxwv kawg uas yuav nyob hauv txoj haujlwm no.

Shri Divyasingha Deva yuav yog tus vajntxwv kawg—qhov no tau sau los ntawm Mahapurush Jagannath Das, ib tug ntawm cov Panchasakhas, hauv nws phau ntawv Malika, uas peb tau hais hauv thawj nqe lus ntawm tshooj no.

Tab sis qhov tseem ceeb yog tias tsis yog Jagannath Das xwb, tab sis lwm tus Mahapurush ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Shishu Ananta Das, kuj tau sau txog qhov no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Patta Madana’. Tus Mahapurush sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଆଉ ଶାସନ ନ ଥିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dibyasiṃha nṛupa rājattwa karibe āu śāsana na thiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Txhais tau tias, Vajntxwv Divyasingha Deva yuav kav thiab tom qab nws, yuav tsis muaj lwm tus vajntxwv kav ntxiv lawm. Divyasingha Deva yuav yog tus vajntxwv kawg. Hauv lwm lo lus, Kaliyuga yuav xaus thaum nws lub sijhawm, thiab Vajtswv Kalki txoj kev ua si rhuav tshem yuav tshwm sim.

6.2 Shri Divyasingha Deva

Qhov tseem ceeb uas yuav tsum nco ntsoov ntawm no yog tias tus Mahapurush tsis tau tsuas yog hais tias Vajntxwv ntawm Puri lub npe thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga yuav yog Divyasingha Deva xwb; tab sis, nws tau hais ntau yam txog nws, xws li nws yuav yog tus vajntxwv thib 19, nws yuav yog tus Divyasingha Deva thib plaub, nws yuav tsis muaj tub, nws yuav muaj tsuas yog ntxhais xwb, nws yuav tsis nyob hauv Puri tab sis nyob hauv Bhubaneswar, nws yuav tsis muaj peev xwm ua Vajtswv Jagannath cov kev pabcuam txhua hnub, nws yuav pib kav thaum tseem yau thiab yuav kav mus txog thaum laus, thiab lwm yam.

Tag nrho cov no haum rau tus Vajntxwv Shri Divyasingha Deva tam sim no zoo kawg nkaus.

7 Yam Cim Qhia Txog Qhov Kawg Ntawm Kaliyuga ntawm Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das thiab lwm tus Panchasakhas tau sau ntau qhov chaw hauv lawv phau ntawv Malika tias cov neeg pe hawm yuav tau txais ntau yam cim los ntawm Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath los ntawm qhov uas lawv yuav paub tias Kali Yuga tau xaus lawm thiab Vajtswv Kalki tau los yug. Cov cim no yuav pom tsis yog los ntawm Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath xwb tab sis kuj los ntawm lwm lub tsev teev ntuj thiab tuam tsev hauv Is Nrias teb, tab sis feem ntau lawv yuav pom los ntawm Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath uas nyob hauv Puri.

Odia Sloka:

ବାରମ୍ବାର ଶ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ଦିରରେ ପୁଣି,
ନାନା ଅଘଟଣ ସତ୍ୟ ମୋ ବାଣୀ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bārambāra shrīmandirare puṇi,
nānā aghaṭana satya mo bāṇī.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika tias thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, ntau yam kev phem (cim phem) yuav pom rov qab los hauv Shrimandir (Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath) uas nyob hauv Puri. Nws hais ntxiv tias kuv cov lus no yuav muaj tseeb tiag.

7.1 Ib Zaj Dab Neeg Txog Cov Cim Phem ntawm Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath

Niaj hnub no peb pom tias dhau 15-20 xyoo dhau los, ntau yam cim phem tau tshwm sim ntawm Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath, xws li ib lub pob zeb loj poob los ntawm lub tuam tsev "Amla Bedha" nyob rau xyoo 1990; cov pob zeb poob rau hauv av los ntawm lub tuam tsev cov qauv sab saud, suav nrog lub sanctum sanctorum, ntau zaus nyob rau xyoo tsis ntev los no; cov noog phem (noog noj nqaij tuag) zaum ntawm lub tuam tsev tus ncej chij thiab Aruna Stambha nyob rau pem hauv ntej ntawm Singhadwara (Rooj Vag Tsov); ntau tus dav dawb hau ya ncig saum lub tuam tsev; lub npog npog pob zeb (Ratnachandua) uas muab tso rau saum cov vajtswv raug hluav taws kub; tus 'Patitapabana Bana' (chij) uas nqa saum lub tuam tsev raug hluav taws kub; tus Neelachakra hnyav 2.2 tons, ua los ntawm Ashtadhatu (yim hlau), raug cua daj cua dub ntau ua rau khoov; tus 'Patitapabana Bana' raug cua daj cua dub ntau ua rau ya mus poob rau hauv hiav txwv thaum muaj cua daj cua dub loj; ib ceg ntawm tsob Kalpavata loj (tsob ntoo banyan) uas nyob hauv lub tuam tsev tawg; tus Patitapabana Bana raug hluav taws kub vim xob laim; kwv yees li 40 lub qhov cub av raug cov neeg phem rhuav tshem hauv lub tuam tsev chav ua noj dawb huv; pom muaj qhov tawg hauv lub tuam tsev Aruna Stambha, tus nqaj sab hauv Natamandapa, lub tsev nyob rau pem hauv ntej ntawm lub sanctum sanctorum (Jagamohana), thiab ze Singhadwara; lub pob zeb 'Padma Pakhuda' (paj ntoo lotus) ploj ntawm Ratnasinghasana (lub zwm txwv pob zeb); xob laim ntaus rau lub tuam tsev chav ua noj; kev tub sab tshwm sim hauv lub tuam tsev thaum hmo ntuj; cov nas ntau ntxiv hauv lub tuam tsev vim cov neeg pe hawm tsawg dua thaum muaj tus kab mob Corona, ua rau muaj kev cuam tshuam thiab rhuav tshem lub tuam tsev cov kev cai dab qhuas; pom muaj ntshav txaws ntau qhov chaw suav nrog lub tuam tsev Ratnasinghasana, tom qab ntawd yog kev da dej loj (Mahasnana) ntawm Chaturdha Murti (plaub tus vajtswv); saum ntuj tig liab tag nrho ib ncig ntawm lub tuam tsev; kev ploj tam sim ntawd ntawm cov puav uas yav tas los pom ntau heev hauv lub tuam tsev; kev siv xov tooj txawb txawm tias lawv raug txwv hauv lub tuam tsev; kev kaw video dag hauv lub tuam tsev siv cov koob yees duab zais hauv tsom iav; kev nkag mus ntawm cov neeg tsis yog Hindu thiab tsis yog Sanatani rau hauv lub tuam tsev txawm tias raug txwv; kev tua tus kheej lossis kev tuag vim lwm yam tshwm sim hauv lub tuam tsev; kev tuag ntawm Baisi Pahacha, uas yog 22 kauj ruam ntawm lub tuam tsev; pom muaj qhov tawg hauv Gumuta (dome) ntawm lub tuam tsev Hasti Dwara (Rooj Vag Qaum Teb), thiab lwm yam.

Pom muaj tej lus faj lem sib cais txog qee yam ntawm cov xwm txheej no hauv ntau phau ntawv Malika, qhov chaw uas tau hais meej tias cov xwm txheej no yuav tshwm sim tsuas yog thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga thiab yuav qhia cov neeg pe hawm txog Vajtswv Kalki txoj kev los rau hauv ntiaj teb. Yog li ntawd, cov neeg uas qhia lawv txoj kev tsis ntseeg thiab tsis ntseeg niaj hnub no txog qhov kawg ntawm Kali Yuga thiab Vajtswv Kalki txoj kev los yug yuav tsum rov xav txog lawv cov kev xav.




Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau 'Jaiphula Malika' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁଁ ପଥର,
ଖସୁଥିବ ପୁଣି ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ତୁହି ଦେଖିବୁ ବେନି ନେତ୍ରର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Baḍa deuluṃ pathara,
khasuthiba puṇi tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
tuhi dekhibu beni netrara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus Mahapurush sau tias lub sijhawm ntawd, cov xwm txheej ntawm cov pob zeb poob rov qab los hauv Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath uas nyob hauv Puri yuav tshwm sim, uas cov neeg pe hawm yuav pom meej nrog lawv lub qhov muag.

Yog peb xav txog qhov tseeb ntawm zaj lus faj lem no, peb yuav pom tias tom qab ib lub pob zeb hnyav 5-6 tons poob los ntawm lub tuam tsev "Amla Bedha" rau Lub Rau Hli 14, 1990, cov xwm txheej zoo li no tau tshwm sim ntau zaus. Rau Lub Yim Hli 13, 1992, ob lub pob zeb loj heev tau poob ncaj qha rau hauv lub sanctum sanctorum ntawm lub tuam tsev. Ib yam li ntawd, rau Lub Kaum Hli 26, 1998, ib lub pob zeb hnyav 15 kg tau dov los ntawm lub tuam tsev loj thiab poob ze lub tuam tsev Bimala nyob rau hauv thaj tsam ntawm lub tuam tsev loj. Rau Lub Kaum Ib Hlis 1, 2011, ib lub pob zeb hnyav txog 1 ton tau poob los ntawm ze Dadhinauti ntawm lub rooj vag sab hnub poob ntawm Lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath.

Kuj nyob rau xyoo 2015, thaum Lub Ob Hlis 2, ib lub pob zeb hnyav 30-40 kg tau poob rau hauv av los ntawm Jalakrida Mandapa uas tau tsim nyob ze Bimala Temple nyob rau sab hnub poob ntawm Jagannath Temple. Hais txog lub sijhawm tsis ntev los no, thaum Lub Yim Hli 3, 2022, ib daim plaster hnyav li 1.5 kg tau poob los ntawm lub ru tsev ntawm lub sanctum sanctorum ntawm lub tuam tsev uas tau tsim nyob rau xyoo pua kaum ob no. Thaum Lub Kaum Hli 23, 2022, ib lub pob zeb tau poob los ntawm sab qab teb-sab hnub tuaj ntawm lub tuam tsev nyob ze Majana Mandapa thiab Charcha Ghara, thiab tsis pub dhau ib lub lim tiam tom qab ntawd, thaum Lub Kaum Hli 28, 2022, ib lub pob zeb hnyav 2-3 kg tau poob los ntawm txheej thib peb saum tus pej thuam ntawm ib tug Sakhi nyob ze qhov chaw sib koom ntawm Natamandapa thiab Jagamohana. Txawm nyob rau xyoo 2024, thaum Lub Peb Hlis 9, ib lub pob zeb hnyav li plaub kilograms tau poob los ntawm lub ru tsev ntawm Baraha Temple uas nyob rau pem hauv ntej ntawm Bimala Temple hauv Jagannath Temple thaj chaw.

Yog li no, qhov kev qhia ua ntej uas Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau hais tseg ntau pua xyoo dhau los txog cov pob zeb poob "tharaku thara" (rov qab los) los ntawm Jagannath Temple kuj tau muaj tseeb nyob rau ob peb xyoos dhau los no.

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ସେହିଦିନଠାରୁ ସଖୀ ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ପଥର ଖସିବ ଯୁଗ ଶେଷ ହେବାପାଇଁ,
ଜାଣି ମୁଁ ଗରୁଡ଼ ଏ ଶକୁନମାନ ଗୁପତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ଏକ ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ତେଇଶି ବରଷେ କଳି ଦେଶ ଛାଡ଼ି ଯିବ।
ଚୁଳରୁ ପଥର ଯେବେ ଖସିବ ସୁତ,
ଖସିଲେ ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ହେବ ଏ କଳି ହତ।
ମୋର ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ତୋ ଦେଉଳ ଚୁଳେ,
ଖସିବ ପଥର ଖଣ୍ଡ ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ତଳେ।
ବତାସରେ ହେ। ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବକ୍ର ହେବ ବାରେ ହେ।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ଅରୁଣ ଖମ୍ବର।
ବତାସର ହେ। ବକ୍ର ହେବ ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ମୋର ହେ।
ତରକିବେ ତେରଜଣ ଜାଣିଥା ନିକର,
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁ ପଥର ଖସି ବାରମ୍ବାର।
ଗୃଧ୍ରପକ୍ଷୀ ମଥାପରେ ବସିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ,
ସେହିଦିନୁ ହତଶିରୀ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Baḍa deularu mora pathara khasiba,
sehidinaṭhāru sakhī bipada paḍiba.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru pathara khasiba yuga sheṣha hebāpāiṃ,
jāṇi mu Garuḍa e shakunamāna gupate deli chetei.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru eka pathara khasiba,
teishi baraṣhe kali desha chāḍi jiba.
Chularu pathara jebe khasiba suta,
khasile aaṃlā beḍhāru heba e kali hata.
Mora baḍa deularu khasiba pathara,
Shrīkṣhetra rājana mora na sebi payara.
Basiba je giddha pakṣhī to deula chule,
khasiba pathara khaṃḍa paḍiba je tale.
Batāsare he. Nīlachakra bakra heba bāre he.
Baḍa deularu mora khasiba pathara,
basiba je giddha pakṣhī Aruṇa khambara.
Batāsara he. Bakra heba Nīlachakra mora he.
Tarakibe terajaṇa jāṇithā nikara,
baḍa deulu pathara khasi bārambāra.
Giddhapakṣhī mathāpare basiṇa je jiba,
sehidinu hatashirī pṛithwī hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau tham txog ntau yam cim phem txog qhov kawg ntawm Kali Yuga uas tau txais los ntawm Jagannath Temple hauv Puri hauv ntau phau ntawv Malika xws li 'Rukmini Gita', 'Garuda Samvada', 'Hari Arjuna Chautisha', 'Kaliyuga Gita', 'Jai Birachita Chautisha', thiab 'Chakada Madana'. Hauv qhov no, nws sau tias thaum ib lub pob zeb poob los ntawm "Amla Bedha" ntawm Jagannath Temple, cov neeg pe hawm yuav tsum nkag siab tias qhov kawg ntawm Kali Yuga yog ze heev thiab ntau yam kev puas tsuaj thiab kev txom nyem yuav tshwm sim rau lub ntiaj teb sai sai no. Nws hais tias qhov poob ntawm lub pob zeb no yuav yog ib qho cim tseem ceeb thiab zais cia ntawm qhov kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, thiab 23 xyoos tom qab nws poob, Kali Yuga yuav xaus. Nws tseem hais ntxiv tias thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, ib tug noog phem yuav zaum saum Jagannath Temple thiab Aruna Stambha uas nyob rau pem hauv ntej ntawm Singhadwara ntawm lub tuam tsev. Tsis tas li ntawd, lub Neelachakra hnyav 2.2-ton uas ua los ntawm Ashtadhatu nyob rau saum lub tuam tsev yuav khoov los ntawm cua daj cua dub.

Cov nyeem yuav xav tsis thoob thaum paub tias tag nrho cov kev qhia ua ntej uas tau sau tseg ntau pua xyoo dhau los no tau tshwm sim tiag tiag hauv Jagannath Temple. Thaum muaj nag hnyav, ua ntej tshaj plaws, thaum Lub Rau Hli 14, 1990, ib lub pob zeb hnyav 5-6 tons tau poob los ntawm "Amla Bedha" ntawm lub tuam tsev. Tom qab ntawd, kuj pom ib tug noog phem zaum saum Aruna Stambha.

Nws yog ib qho tseem ceeb uas tsis tau pom ib tug noog ya saum Jagannath Puri temple, thiab cov kws tshawb fawb kuj tsis tuaj yeem tshawb xyuas tias vim li cas qhov no thiaj tshwm sim. Tab sis nyob rau lub sijhawm kawg ntawm Kali Yuga no, ua pov thawj tias cov lus qhia ua ntej ntawm Malika muaj tseeb, cov noog phem xws li dav dawb hau/dav dub tau pom zaum saum Patitapabana Bana (tus ncej chij) saum lub tuam tsev ntau zaus. Nyob rau ob peb xyoos dhau los no, qhov no tau tshwm sim thaum Lub Yim Hli 11, 2018, Lub Kaum Ob Hlis 26, 2018, thiab Lub Xya Hli 23, 2020, thaum pom cov noog phem zaum saum tus ncej chij ntawm lub tuam tsev. Thaum Lub Kaum Ob Hlis 7, 2021, pom ntau tus noog zoo li no ya ncig ua pab pawg saum Jagannath Temple. Ib qho xwm txheej zoo sib xws tau pom dua ib zaug thaum Lub Yim Hli 17, 2022. Qhov no tsis tau nres ntawm no; nyob rau lub Tsib Hlis, Lub Rau Hli, thiab Lub Cuaj Hli ntawm xyoo 2023, cov neeg nyob hauv Puri tau pom dua qhov xwm txheej tsis zoo no thaum pom cov noog zoo sib xws zaum saum tus ncej chij uas tau teeb tsa saum lub tuam tsev.

Tsis ntev los no nyob rau xyoo 2025 thiab, pom ib tug noog zaum saum Aruna Stambha thaum Lub Cuaj Hli 4, thaum pom cov dav dawb hau zaum saum lub tuam tsev thaum Lub Cuaj Hli 2, Lub Cuaj Hli 16, thiab Lub Kaum Hli 14. Thaum Lub Kaum Ob Hlis 9, pom ntau tus noog ya ncig Shrimandir.

Ntxiv rau tag nrho cov no, cua daj cua dub hnyav heev, 'Fani', uas tau tsoo Puri ntug dej hiav txwv thaum Lub Tsib Hlis 3, 2019, tau khoov Neelachakra saum lub tuam tsev, yog li ua tiav lwm qhov kev qhia ua ntej uas tau sau tseg ntau pua xyoo dhau los hauv qhov no.

Nws yog ib qho tseem ceeb uas cov lus qhia ua ntej no uas tau sau tseg nyob rau xyoo pua 16 tsis yog tsuas yog cov cim qhia txog qhov kawg ntawm Kali Yuga xwb, tab sis kuj yog cov cim qhia los ntawm Panchasakhas txog qhov tuaj txog ntawm Kalki Avatar, vim tias Kalki Avatar yuav tuaj txog tsuas yog thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, raws li tag nrho cov vaj lug kub tau hais tseg suav nrog Bhavishya Malika.





Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।
ହର ଉଦ୍ୟାନରେ ମାୟା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛକୁ ଗ୍ରାସୁଥିବେ,
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବାନା ଝଡ଼ିବ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଲୋଡ଼ିଯିବେ।
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ପରୁ ପତାକା ଛିଡ଼ିଣ ପଡ଼ିବ ସମୁଦ୍ର କୂଳେ,
କଳକୀ ରୂପକୁ ଚିନ୍ତା ପୁଣ ହେବ ଭାବିବେ କ୍ଷୀରାଧି କୂଳେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Nīlachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṃgā adhara,
Jājanagre janma hoibe Biṣhṇu Sharmā gṛihara.
Hara udyānare māyā prakāshiba mlechchaku grāsuthibe,
Nīlachakra bānā jhaḍiba nishchaya bhakataṃku loḍijibe.
Nīlachakra paru patākā chiḍiṇa paḍiba samudra kūle,
Kalki rūpaku chiṃtā puṇa heba bhābibe kṣhīrādhi kūle.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau hais txog lwm qhov cim qhia txog qhov kawg ntawm Kali Yuga thiab qhov yug los ntawm Vaj Ntxuj Kalki hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' thiab 'Kalikalpa Gita'. Nws sau tias yuav muaj ib lub sijhawm thaum Patitapabana Bana uas tau txuas saum Neelachakra ntawm Shrimandir yuav ya tawm ntawm nws qhov chaw. Tus Mahapurush kuj tau sau tias tus chij yuav ya tawm thiab poob rau hauv hiav txwv.

Qhov kev qhia ua ntej no uas Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau hais tseg kuj tau muaj tseeb. Ib hnub ua ntej cua daj cua dub hnyav heev 'Fani' tsoo Puri ntug dej hiav txwv, thaum Lub Tsib Hlis 2, 2019, vim muaj cua daj cua dub, Patitapabana Bana tau ya tawm thiab poob rau hauv hiav txwv. Qhov xwm txheej zoo sib xws tau tshwm sim dua ib zaug thaum Lub Tsib Hlis 18, 2020, ob hnub ua ntej Cyclone 'Amphan' tsoo Odisha ntug dej hiav txwv.



Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡ଼ି ଆମ୍ଭେ ଯିବୁ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ଲାଗିବ ରତ୍ନଚାନ୍ଦୁଆ ଅଗ୍ନି ସେତେବେଳେ।
ନିଶାକାଳେ ହେ। ନୀଳାଚଳୁ ଚୋରିଯିବ ହେଲେ ହେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Nīlāchala chāḍi āmbhe jibu jetebele,
lāgiba ratnachāṃduā agni setebele.
Nishākāle he. Nīlāchalu chorijiba hele he.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hais txog Vaj Ntxuj cov lus, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Jai Birachita Chautisha' tias thaum Vaj Ntxuj Jagannath tawm ntawm Nilachala, uas yog, Shrimandir, los yug los ua tib neeg lub cev, thaum lub sijhawm ntawd lub npog pob zeb uas tau tso saum Vaj Ntxuj peb leeg hauv Jagannath Temple yuav kub hnyiab.

Nws yog ib qho tseem ceeb uas qhov xwm txheej ntawm Ratnachandua kub hnyiab tau tshwm sim nyob rau xyoo 2004-05, thiab yog li no qhov kev qhia ua ntej no ntawm tus Mahapurush kuj tau muaj tseeb.

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍ପବଟ ଡ଼ାଳ ଛେଦନ ଭୋଇବ ଦେଖିବୁ ତୁ ନୟନରେ,
ଶିବକଳ୍ପ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ତୁରେ।
ଏକ ଦକ୍ଷିଣରେ ନବ ଯେ ଥିବ,
କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଛେଦନ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kalpabaṭa ḍāla chedana hoiba dekhibu tu nayanare,
Shibakalpa bāṇī anyathā nohiba shuṇa Rāmadāsa ture.
Eka dakṣhiṇare naba je thiba,
kalpabaṭa mora chedana hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' 600 xyoo dhau los tias yuav muaj ib lub sijhawm thaum ceg ntoo Kalpavata uas nyob hauv Jagannath Temple thaj chaw yuav tawg. Nws tseem tau sau hauv lwm phau ntawv tias thaum muaj 'cuaj' nyob ib sab ntawm 'ib', uas yog, tus lej 19, tsuas yog thaum ntawd qhov xwm txheej no thiaj yuav tshwm sim.

Raws nraim li tus Mahapurush tau hais tseg, qhov kev qhia ua ntej no tau muaj tseeb nyob rau xyoo 2019 thaum cua daj cua dub hnyav heev 'Fani', uas tau tsoo Puri ntug dej hiav txwv thaum Lub Tsib Hlis 3, tau ua rau Kalpavata loj heev, laus heev uas nyob hauv lub tuam tsev thaj chaw puas tsuaj.

Odia Sloka:

ରକ୍ତଛିଟା ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ରତ୍ନସିଂହାସନେ,
ଏହି ଘଟଣା ଘଟିବ ମନେ ରଖ ଦିନେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Raktachīṃṭā paḍiba je ratnasiṃhāsane,
ehi ghaṭaṇā ghaṭiba mane rakha dine.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Ntawm ntau yam cim qhia uas tus Mahapurush tau hais tseg tias yuav tau txais los ntawm Jagannath Temple hauv Puri thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, ib qho yog pom cov ntshav txaws rau ntawm Ratnasinghasana ntawm cov pej thuam dawb huv.

Qhov kev qhia ua ntej no uas tus Mahapurush tau sau tseg kuj tau muaj tseeb. Nyob rau ob peb xyoos dhau los no, cov ntshav txaws tau poob rau ntau qhov chaw hauv lub tuam tsev, suav nrog Ratnasinghasana, tom qab ntawd, raws li lub tuam tsev kev cai, Chaturdha Murti tau raug ntxuav kom huv los ntawm Mahasnana (kev da dej loj) kev cai. Piv txwv li, thaum Lub Peb Hlis 6, 2021, pom ntshav nyob ze 'Bhitar Katha' uas nyob deb me ntsis ntawm lub sanctum sanctorum ntawm lub tuam tsev. Thaum Lub Plaub Hlis 18, 2021, vim muaj kev sib cav sib ceg ntawm ob tug tub ceev xwm uas tau raug xa mus saib xyuas lub tuam tsev, cov ntshav txaws tau poob rau hauv lub tuam tsev thaj chaw. Thaum Lub Peb Hlis 7, 2022, thaum lub 'Abakasha Niti' (kev cai), pom cov ntshav txaws nyob ze 'Bhitar Katha' sab hauv lub tuam tsev Jagamohana (lub tsev nyob rau pem hauv ntej ntawm lub sanctum sanctorum). Tom qab ntawd thaum Lub Plaub Hlis 11, 2022, pom cov ntshav txaws nyob ze Dhukudi Dwara ntawm lub tuam tsev. Thaum Lub Kaum Ib Hlis 3, 2022, tom qab 'Mangala Alati', 'Mailama', thiab 'Tadapa Lagi' kev cai, pom cov ntshav txaws nyob ze 'Bhitar Katha'. Qhov xwm txheej zoo li no tau pom dua nyob rau lub lim tiam thib peb ntawm Lub Ib Hlis hauv xyoo 2023. Tom qab Vaj Ntxuj Jagannath 'Abakasha' thiab 'Besa Lagi' kev cai xaus, pom cov ntshav qhua hauv Shrimandir, vim li ntawd qhov khoom pub (Bhoga) rau Gopalaballabha tsis tuaj yeem ua tiav. Tom qab ntawd thaum Lub Rau Hli 4, 2023, tom qab kev da dej dawb huv (Devasnana) xaus, pom cov ntshav txaws rau ntawm Snana Mandapa. Txawm tsis ntev los no, thaum Lub Kaum Hli 13, 2023, vim muaj kev sib cav sib ceg hnyav ntawm ob tug neeg ua hauj lwm txog kev faib Prasad tam sim ntawd tom qab 'Madhyahna Dhupa' hauv Shrimandir, cov ntshav txaws tau poob rau hauv lub tuam tsev sanctum sanctorum. Nyob rau xyoo 2024 thiab, thaum sawv ntxov ntxov Lub Plaub Hlis 23, tom qab Singhadwara qhib, ib tug poj niam los ntawm ib tsev neeg neeg ua hauj lwm tau tso nws txhais tes rau ntawm lub qhov rooj tuam tsev thaum nws tus ntiv tes raug tsoo hauv ntawd. Yog li ntawd, ntshav tau tawm los ntawm nws tus ntiv tes nyob ze Dhukudi Dwara ze rau sab hauv Bedha. Hauv qhov xwm txheej zoo li no, tus Tswv Ntuj Mahasnana tau ua tiav txawm ua ntej Mangala Alati. Zoo sib xws, cov xwm txheej ntshav tawm hauv lub tuam tsev thaj chaw tau txuas ntxiv nyob rau xyoo 2025 thiab.

7.2 Vaj Ntxuj Balabhadra tus pej thuam poob thaum Rath Yatra 2024.

Thaum lub Rath Yatra 11-hnub hauv Shri Jagannath Puri, 'Adapa Mandapa Bije' kev cai tau pib thaum 7 PM yav tsaus ntuj Lub Xya Hli 9, 2024. Thaum lub sijhawm no, thaum coj Vaj Ntxuj Balabhadra tus pej thuam los ntawm lub tsheb kauj vab mus rau Adapa Mandapa ntawm Gundicha Temple, Nws tau poob ntsej muag rau Charamala, vim li ntawd 12 tug neeg ua hauj lwm kuj raug mob. Qhov xwm txheej tsis zoo li no tau tshwm sim thawj zaug hauv keeb kwm. Qhov poob ntawm Vaj Ntxuj Balabhadra li no yog ib qho cim qhia txog kev puas tsuaj uas yuav tshwm sim, uas tau hais tseg raws li hauv 'Niladri' phau ntawv txog Shrikhetra cov cai thiab txoj cai, raws li Vaj Ntxuj Brahma thiab Vaj Ntxuj Vishnu tau qhia.

Odia Sloka:

ମୁରବୈରିଣ ଏତସ୍ୟ ଜଗଦୀଶସ୍ୟ ଭୂପତେହଃ।
ଭଦ୍ରାୟାଶ୍ଚ ବଳସ୍ୟାପି ପ୍ରମାଦାତ୍ ପତନଂ ଯଦି।।
ମହାଭୟଂ ନୃପସ୍ୟାପି ରାଜ୍ୟସ୍ୟାପି ଭବେଦ୍ଧ୍ରୁବମ୍।
ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷଂ ମରଣଂ ଚୈବ ଜାୟତେ ଚ ପୁନଃ ପୁନଃ।।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Murabairiṇa etasya jagadīshasya bhūpatehaḥ.
Bhadrāyāshcha balasyāpi pramādāt patanaṃ yadi.
Mahābhayaṃ nṛipasyāpi rājyasyāpi bhabeddhrubam.
Durbhikṣhaṃ maraṇaṃ chaiba jāyate cha punaḥ punaḥ.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, "Au Vaj Ntxuj, yog tias Jagannath, Balabhadra, lossis Subhadra poob yam tsis tau npaj tseg, ces kev puas tsuaj loj yuav tshwm sim rau vaj ntxuj thiab lub xeev. Kev tshaib plab yuav tshwm sim, thiab kab mob yuav rov tshwm sim dua thiab dua."

Hluav taws kub hauv lub sanctum sanctorum ntawm Mahakal Temple, Ujjain nyob rau xyoo 2024

Thaum sawv ntxov hnub Monday, Lub Peb Hlis 25, 2024, thaum lub koob tsheej Holi, hluav taws kub tau tawg hauv lub sanctum sanctorum ntawm Mahakaleshwar Temple hauv Ujjain vim muaj kev pov 'gulal' (hmoov xim) thaum Bhasma Aarti. Txog 14 tus neeg, suav nrog tus pov thawj loj thiab lwm tus neeg ua hauj lwm, raug mob hluav taws hauv qhov xwm txheej no.

7.3 Cov cim phem tseem ceeb uas tau txais los ntawm Jagannath Temple nyob rau xyoo 2025

Cov cim phem uas tau txais los ntawm Jagannath Temple tau txuas ntxiv nyob rau xyoo 2025 thiab. Los ntawm ntau yam cim, Vaj Ntxuj Jagannath tau qhia Nws cov neeg pe hawm txog kev puas tsuaj uas yuav tshwm sim, qee qhov cim tseem ceeb uas peb tab tom nthuav tawm rau koj.

(a) Akshaya Danda tawg thaum Lub Ob Hlis 14

Ib yam li txhua hnub, thaum tav su Lub Ob Hlis 14, 2025 (Hnub Friday), thaum tus Chunara neeg ua hauj lwm mus txog Neelachakra saum lub tuam tsev los hloov Patitapabana Bana lossis tus chij tseem ceeb, nws pom Akshaya Danda (tus ncej xyoob uas tuav Patitapabana Bana) tawg. Tom qab ntawd, cov neeg ua hauj lwm tau khi tus chij rau Neelachakra nws tus kheej.

Qhov tawg ntawm Akshaya Danda li no tau ua rau ntau tus neeg pe hawm lub siab mob, thiab lawv tau qhia kev tu siab heev txog qhov no. Nrog rau qhov no, ntau tus neeg pe hawm tau hu qhov no ua ib qho cim qhia txog kev puas tsuaj uas yuav tshwm sim sai sai no.

(b) Pom ntshav txaws hauv lub tuam tsev thaum Lub Ob Hlis 16; Mahasnana ntawm cov vaj ntxuj tau ua tiav

Thaum Lub Ob Hlis 16 (Hnub Sunday), tom qab ua tiav cov kev cai thaum sawv ntxov xws li 'Dwara Phita Niti' thiab 'Mangala Alati', sai li sai tau thaum pib pom Tus Tswv Jagannath, tsis ntev tom qab ntawd, cov neeg ua haujlwm pom cov ntshav qhua nyob ze thaj tsam 'Bhitar Katha'. Tam sim ntawd tom qab qhov xwm txheej, tau npaj rau 'Bada Mahasnana Niti' ntawm cov vajtswv, thiab kev pom kuj tau nres ib ntus.

(c) Ib tug dav ya nrog Patitapabana Bana thaum Lub Plaub Hlis 13

Thaum Lub Plaub Hlis 13, 2025, cov neeg pe hawm Tus Tswv Jagannath nyob ntawm Puri Shrimandir tau pom ib qho cim tsis tau pom dua. Txog li 5 teev tsaus ntuj, muaj ib tug dav pom ya ncig lub tuam tsev nqa Patitapabana Bana hauv nws lub qhov ncauj. Raws li cov ntaub ntawv, tus dav ya ncig lub tuam tsev li 4-5 zaug thiab tom qab ntawd ya mus rau hiav txwv hla lub rooj vag sab hnub poob.

Daim vis dis aus ntawm qhov xwm txheej no tau nrov heev hauv social media. Ntau tus neeg pe hawm tau qhia lawv txoj kev tu siab thiab kev txhawj xeeb txog qhov no. Ntau tus neeg pe hawm tau txuas qhov no nrog cov kev puas tsuaj yuav los, thiab ntau tus tau hu nws tias yog ib qho cim tsis zoo tsis tau pom dua los ntawm Tus Tswv Jagannath.

(d) Pom cov ntshav qhua hauv lub tuam tsev thaum Lub Plaub Hlis 21 thiab Mahasnana ntawm cov vajtswv tau ua

Thaum Lub Plaub Hlis 21 (Hnub Monday) thiab, pom cov ntshav qhua nyob ze Bhitar Katha hauv Shrimandir, tom qab ntawd Mahasnana ntawm Tus Tswv cov mlom tau ua raws li kev lig kev cai. Lub caij no, kev pom Tus Tswv Qib Siab rau cov neeg pe hawm tau nres li 1 teev. Raws li cov ntaub ntawv, pom cov ntshav qhua hauv lub tuam tsev vim yog iav tawg nkaug tus tub ceev xwm ko taw.

(e) Ib tug dav zaum saum Patitapabana Bana thaum Lub Cuaj Hli 2

Ib tug dav zaum saum Patitapabana Bana saum Shrimandir tau pom ntau zaus nyob rau ob peb xyoos dhau los. Txhua zaus pom qhov xwm txheej zoo li no, nws tau qhia tawm yuav luag txhua lub xov xwm hauv zos hauv Odisha. Thaum Lub Cuaj Hli 2, tib yam nkaus tau tshwm sim dua ib zaug thaum xub pom ib tug dav ya nyob ze Neelachakra thiab tom qab ib ntus pom zaum saum Patitapabana Bana uas txuas nrog saum Neelachakra.

(f) Ib tug dav zaum saum Aruna Stambha dua thaum Lub Cuaj Hli 4

Tsis yog tsuas yog Jagannath Temple xwb, Bhavishya Malika kuj piav txog kev zaum ntawm ib tug dav los yog lwm tus noog phem rau ntawm Aruna Stambha nyob rau pem hauv ntej ntawm Singhadwara ntawm lub tuam tsev tias yog ib qho cim tsis zoo. Thaum Lub Cuaj Hli 4, uas yog, tsuas yog ob hnub tom qab pom tus dav saum Shrimandir thaum Lub Cuaj Hli 2, kuj pom ib tug dav zaum saum Aruna Stambha. Cov neeg kuj pom qhov no tias yog ib qho cim tsis zoo. Los ntawm qhov xwm txheej no, ntau tus tau piav txog Malika tej lus faj lem sau 600 xyoo dhau los tias yog qhov tseeb.

(g) Ib tug dav zaum saum lub tuam tsev dua thaum Lub Cuaj Hli 16

Tsuas yog 15 hnub tom qab Lub Cuaj Hli 2, thaum sawv ntxov ntxov Lub Cuaj Hli 16, pom ib tug dav zaum dua saum Patitapabana Bana ntawm lub tuam tsev.

(h) Pom dav zaum saum Patitapabana Bana ntawm Shrimandir dua thaum Lub Kaum Hli 14

Pom ib tug dav zaum saum lub tuam tsev ob zaug hauv lub Cuaj Hli. Qhov kev tshwm sim no tsis tau nres ntawm no; cov neeg nyob hauv Puri tau pom qhov xwm txheej no rov tshwm sim dua thaum Lub Kaum Hli 14. Ntau tus neeg pe hawm tau piav txog kev rov zaum ntawm tus dav saum lub tuam tsev tias yog ib qho cim qhia txog kev puas tsuaj yuav los.

(i) Ntau tus dav ya ncig saum Shrimandir thaum Lub Kaum Ob Hlis 9

Ib yam li thaum Lub Kaum Ob Hlis 7, 2021, thiab Lub Yim Hli 17, 2022, pom ib qho cim tsis zoo heev thaum Lub Kaum Ob Hlis 9, 2025, thiab, thaum pom ntau tus dav ya ncig saum Jagannath Temple. Qhov xwm txheej no tau nyiam cov neeg pe hawm tsis yog hauv Puri lossis Odisha xwb, tab sis kuj yog cov neeg nyob sab nraud Odisha. Ntau tus neeg pe hawm tau hu qhov xwm txheej no tias yog ib qho cim qhia txog ib qho xwm txheej loj yuav tshwm sim sai sai.

7.4 Cov cim tsis zoo tau txais los ntawm lwm lub tsev teev ntuj thiab tuam tsev tseem ceeb hauv xyoo 2025

Hluav taws kub tawg hauv Mahakaleshwar Temple thaj tsam hauv Ujjain

Thaum Lub Tsib Hlis 5, 2025, thaum 12 teev tav su, muaj hluav taws kub tawg hauv chav tswj hwm kev tswj hwm kev paug pa nyob ze Shankh Dwara hauv lub ntiaj teb nto moo Baba Mahakaleshwar Temple thaj tsam hauv Ujjain. Tom qab qhov xwm txheej no, kev ntshai tau tshwm sim hauv lub tuam tsev. Tom qab hluav taws kub tawg, kev nkag ntawm cov neeg pe hawm mus rau hauv lub tuam tsev tau nres ib ntus.

Hluav taws kub tawg hauv Peetha thaj tsam ntawm Mahapurush Achyutananda Das

Thaum hnub Devasnana Purnima, Lub Rau Hli 11, 2025, hluav taws kub tawg hauv Nemal Padmabana Peetha thaj tsam ntawm Mahapurush Achyutananda Das nyob hauv Nischintakoili, Cuttack kuj tau pom tias yog ib qho cim tsis zoo heev los ntawm Malika cov neeg pe hawm. Ib qho kev ntshai tau tshwm sim ntawm cov neeg coob coob ntawm ntau txhiab tus neeg pe hawm thaum lub teeb-sawv ntsug nyob hauv thaj tsam dheev kub hnyiab, thiab tsis ntev tom qab ntawd, cov nplaim taws kub hnyiab tau pib tawg.

Tsoom fwv txiav Nemal banyan ntoo

Thaum pib Lub Yim Hli, feem ntau ntawm cov cav ntoo banyan qub qub nyob rau pem hauv ntej ntawm lub tuam tsev nyob ntawm Sadhana Peetha ntawm tus sau thawj ntawm Malika cov ntawv, tus vajtswv Shri Achyutananda Das, tau raug txiav. Allegedly, tsoom fwv tau ua li ntawd rau kev tsim kho ntawm Achyutananda Das Peetha. Muaj kev tawm tsam dav dav los ntawm cov neeg hauv zos thiab cov pov thawj, thiab thaum lawv tawm tsam, tsuas yog ob peb ceg ntoo banyan tau tshuav tsis tau txiav. Nws yuav tsum nco ntsoov tias Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau cov ntawv Malika thaum zaum hauv qab 'Nemal Banyan Tree' hauv kev xav. Kev txiav Nemal banyan ntoo tau pom tias yog ib qho cim tsis zoo heev los ntawm Malika cov neeg pe hawm.

Tus chij kub uas tau ntsia rau saum ncov ntawm Mahakaleshwar Temple poob qis

Thaum Lub Rau Hli 1, 2025, pom ib qho cim tsis zoo dua los ntawm lub ntiaj teb nto moo Mahakaleshwar Temple nyob hauv Ujjain. Qhov tseeb, vim muaj cua daj cua dub, tus chij kub uas tau ntsia rau saum ncov ntawm Mahakaleshwar Temple dheev poob qis. Tus pov thawj hauv tuam tsev tau piav txog qhov xwm txheej no tias yog ib qho teeb meem txhawj xeeb los ntawm kev ntseeg.

Lub npog ntsej muag ntawm Tus Tswv Shiva ua rau Shivalinga hauv Mahakaleshwar Temple tawg thiab poob qis

Ib qho xwm txheej tshwj xeeb tau tshwm sim thaum Lub Yim Hli 18 ntawm Mahakal Temple nyob hauv Ujjain. Ntawm no, ib yam li txhua hnub thaum 8:00 teev tsaus ntuj thaum kho kom zoo nkauj (Shringara) ntawm Shivalinga, tau ua ib lub npog ntsej muag ntawm Tus Tswv Shiva nrog Bhang (cannabis paste). Tau siv Bhang ntau heev thaum lub sijhawm no. Tam sim ntawd tom qab no, Aarti tab tom yuav pib thaum Bhang Shringara tawg thiab poob qis.
