Lub qhov ntswg, daim di ncauj, thiab ib lub qhov muag tawg los ntawm Tus Tswv Shiva lub npog ntsej muag thiab poob qis.

Ib qho pom tsis txawv txav pom hauv Maa Samaleswari Temple

Los ntawm lub tuam tsev nto moo ntawm Maa Samaleswari nyob hauv Lakhanpur, Jharsuguda, Odisha, thaum hmo ntuj Hnub Thursday, Lub Yim Hli 21, 2025, cov neeg pe hawm tau txais ib qho cim qhia tias Tus Vajtswv Poj Niam tawm ntawm Nws tus mlom thiab tawm los. Thaum tus pov thawj qhib lub qhov rooj tuam tsev thaum sawv ntxov ntxov rau 'Mangala Alati', pom cov hneev taw mus sab nraud, pleev xim nrog Alta (xim liab), uas yog Tus Vajtswv Poj Niam, thiab nrog rau qhov no, Nws rab ntaj (Khanda) thiab rab riam peb ceg (Trishul), uas tau txuas nrog Tus Vajtswv Poj Niam tus mlom, tau pom poob rau sab.

Raws li 'Bhavishya Malika', nyob rau theem kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, ua ntej kev tsim tsa Dharma, txhua tus Vajtswv Poj Niam yuav tawm ntawm lawv qhov chaw thiab tawm los koom nrog kev rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab cov neeg txhaum. Qhov xwm txheej no yog pov thawj tias lub sijhawm ntawd tau los txog thiab tej lus faj lem ntawd tau muaj tseeb.

Hluav taws kub hnyiab hauv tej mlom dawb huv hauv lub Tuam Tsev Brahmagiri Jagannath

Thaum Lub Yim Hli Ntuj Tim 28, 2025, tej mlom ntawm Vajtswv Jagannath, tus nus hlob Balabhadra, thiab niam Subhadra raug hluav taws kub hnyiab hauv lub Tuam Tsev Brahmagiri Jagannath uas nyob hauv lub zos Kandagoda, hauv cheeb tsam Puri, Odisha. Qhov tseeb, tom qab ua kev pe hawm yav tsaus ntuj tas, cov pov thawj tau mus tsev. Thaum 8:30 PM, vim muaj pa taws tawm hauv lub tuam tsev, thaum lawv rhuav lub qhov rooj tuam tsev, lawv pom tias tej mlom sab hauv raug hluav taws kub hnyiab. Tom qab sab laj nrog Muktimandapa, ib tug Shaligrama tau raug pe hawm txij hnub ntawd mus. Tom qab sau Daru (ntoo dawb huv) rau hauv Nabakalebara uas yuav los txog, tej mlom tshiab tau raug tso rau hauv.

Cim qhia txog kev puas tsuaj uas yuav los txog tau txais los ntawm lub Tuam Tsev Lingaraj, tus ncej chij ntawm lub tuam tsev tawg

Thaum Hnub Monday, Lub Cuaj Hli Ntuj Tim 15, 2025, vim muaj nag hlob thiab cua daj cua dub, tus ncej chij ntawm lub Tuam Tsev Lingaraj uas tau tsim nyob rau xyoo pua 11, nyob hauv Bhubaneswar, tau tawg. Thaum sawv ntxov Hnub Tuesday, tom qab cov neeg ua hauj lwm pom, ib daim chij tau raug tsa rau ntawm tus ncej tshiab.

Daim chij nyob saum lub ncov ntawm lub Tuam Tsev Lingaraj uas nyob hauv Bhubaneswar tau tawg vim nag hlob thiab cua daj cua dub. Lub chaw dawb huv tshaj plaws raug xob laim; qhov tsaus ntuj npog lub tuam tsev.

Thaum muaj nag hlob thiab cua daj cua dub hnyav rau Hnub Wednesday, Lub Kaum Hli Ntuj Tim 8, 2025, lub Tuam Tsev Lingaraj uas nyob hauv Odisha lub peev, Bhubaneswar, tau raug xob laim. Qhov no kuj yog ib qho cim qhia txog ib qho xwm txheej tsis zoo. Daim chij ntawm lub tuam tsev tau tawg vim cua daj cua dub, thaum lub koob yees duab CCTV thiab lub teeb pom kev ntawm lub tuam tsev tau raug puas tsuaj los ntawm xob laim. Tau tshaj tawm tias qee tus neeg pe hawm thiab cov neeg ua hauj lwm uas nyob hauv lub tuam tsev kuj raug hluav taws xob.

(j) Thawj zaug hauv nws keeb kwm 500 xyoo, Bal Bhog tsis tau muab hauv lub Tuam Tsev Banke Bihari hauv Vrindavan

Thakur Banke Bihari, uas tau tshwm sim txog tsib puas xyoo dhau los, tau muab Bhog (khoom noj fij) rau plaub zaug hauv ib hnub. Ua ntej teem chaw rau kev pom thaum sawv ntxov (Darshan), Bal Bhog raug muab. Ua ntej lub tuam tsev kaw thaum tav su, Raj Bhog raug muab, thiab thaum qhov rooj qhib thaum yav tsaus ntuj, Utthapan Bhog raug muab. Thaum qhov rooj kaw, Shayan Bhog raug muab. Daim ntawv cog lus los npaj Bhog yog muab rau tus kws ua khoom qab zib (Halwai). Tus kws ua khoom qab zib npaj Bhog thiab muab rau cov neeg ua hauj lwm, thiab lawv muab Bhog.

Tab sis thaum Lub Kaum Ob Hlis Ntuj Tim 15 thaum 8:45 AM, qhov rooj tuam tsev qhib, tab sis tus kws ua khoom qab zib tsis tuaj nrog Bal Bhog. Thaum hu mus, pom tias tus kws ua khoom qab zib tsis tuaj vim tsis tau them nqi txhua hli. Thawj zaug hauv nws keeb kwm 500 xyoo, qhov no tau tshwm sim thaum Bal Bhog tsis tuaj yeem muab rau Banke Bihari Ji rau hnub ntawd.

7.5 Kev npaj Rath Yatra thaum lub sij hawm tsis tsim nyog

Raws li peb txhua tus paub, Rath Yatra tau raug npaj txhua xyoo rau Dwitiya Tithi ntawm Shukla Paksha ntawm lub hli Ashadha. Qhov kev lig kev cai no tau raug ua raws li txhua lub sij hawm hauv lwm lub tuam tsev Jagannath hauv Is Nrias teb thiab txawv teb chaws, suav nrog lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath hauv Puri. Tab sis nyob rau ob peb xyoos dhau los, muaj ntau zaus uas ISKCON tau pom tias ua txhaum qhov kev lig kev cai no.

Hauv qhov teeb meem no, tom qab tus Thawj Coj ntawm Shree Jagannath Temple Administration thiab tus Thawj Tswj Hwm ntawm Shree Jagannath Temple Managing Committee, Gajapati Maharaj Divyasingha Deva, tau tawm tsam, thiab tau sau ntawv tsis txaus siab mus rau ISKCON lub hauv paus chaw hauj lwm nyob hauv Mayapur, qee qhov kev txhim kho tau tshwm sim tiag tiag, tab sis ISKCON tseem pom tias tab tom npaj Rath Yatras tsis raws sij hawm hauv Is Nrias teb thiab tshwj xeeb tshaj yog txawv teb chaws.

Hauv qhov no, ISKCON nyuam qhuav npaj Rath Yatra hauv Taiwan rau Lub Kaum Ib Hlis Ntuj Tim 23, 2025, uas tau raug thuam los ntawm cov zej zog Odia thoob ntiaj teb thiab ntau tus neeg pe hawm Jagannath ntawm Odisha. Cov neeg pe hawm tsis tsuas yog suav tias nws yog kev ua txhaum cai ntawm cov cai lig kev cai xwb tab sis kuj suav tias nws yog kev tsis quav ntsej txog Vajtswv Jagannath tus kheej txoj lus txib.


7.6 Qhov yuam kev hauv daim ntawv qhia hnub Askiv xyoo 2026 luam tawm los ntawm Shrimandir Administration

Tsis ntev los no, ib qho yuam kev loj tau tshwm sim hauv daim ntawv qhia hnub Askiv xyoo 2026 uas tau tshaj tawm los ntawm Jagannath Temple Administration. Qhov tseeb, ib daim duab ntawm Ratnabedi tau luam tawm hauv daim ntawv qhia hnub uas qhov kev txiav txim ntawm Vajtswv Peb Leeg tsis raug. Los ntawm tus neeg saib pom, hauv daim duab ntawm daim ntawv qhia hnub, Vajtswv Jagannath tau raug tso rau sab laug kawg, tom qab ntawd Vajtswv poj niam Subhadra, thiab tom qab ntawd tus nus hlob Balabhadra, thaum nyob rau hauv qhov kev txiav txim raug, Vajtswv Balabhadra yuav tsum nyob rau sab laug kawg, tom qab ntawd Vajtswv poj niam Subhadra, thiab tom qab ntawd Shri Jagannath. Qhov ntawd yog, qhov yuam kev yog tias hauv daim ntawv qhia hnub, daim duab ntawm tus nus hlob Balabhadra tau luam tawm nyob rau hauv qhov chaw ntawm Vajtswv Jagannath, thiab Vajtswv Jagannath daim duab nyob rau hauv qhov chaw ntawm Vajtswv Balabhadra. Ntxiv rau qhov no, Chakraraj Sudarshan tsis tau muab chaw rau hauv daim duab ntawm Ratnasinghasana hauv daim ntawv qhia hnub. Tsis tas li ntawd, ib daim duab ntawm Rath Yatra kuj tau luam tawm hauv daim ntawv qhia hnub, uas qhia txog Niam Subhadra lub tsheb kauj vab ua ntej, tom qab ntawd Vajtswv Supreme Jagannath lub tsheb kauj vab, thiab thaum kawg Vajtswv Balabhadra lub tsheb kauj vab.

Vim cov yuam kev no, muaj kev tsis txaus siab thiab kev npau taws hauv cov neeg pe hawm lub siab. Kuj muaj kev npau taws ntawm cov neeg ua hauj lwm ntawm lub Tuam Tsev Jagannath txog qhov xwm txheej no. Raws li qee tus neeg pe hawm, qhov no yog ib qho cim qhia los ntawm Vajtswv Jagannath tus kheej txog kev phem uas yuav los txog thiab kev hloov pauv hauv ntiaj teb kev txiav txim hauv xyoo tshiab.

8 Tus Tswv Kalki Yug Los – Thaum twg thiab Qhov twg?

Raws li Bhavishya Malika, Vajtswv Kalki twb yug los lawm. Tsis yog Vajtswv Kalki xwb, tab sis tag nrho cov tub rog siab loj ntawm Mahabharata lub sij hawm xws li Yudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babruvahana, Ekalavya, Ghatotkacha, Duryodhana, Shakuni, thiab lwm yam, tau rov yug los rau hauv ntiaj teb no. Raws li Malika, lub sij hawm no tag nrho cov tub rog no yuav sib ntaus ua ke ntawm Dharma sab tawm tsam Adharma.

Txawm li cas los xij, peb yuav tsis tham txog lwm tus tub rog ntawm no thiab yuav txwv peb cov lus tsuas yog rau Parahbrahma Narayana Mahavishnu Vajtswv Kalki Ram.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯୋଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Nīḷachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṅgā adhara,
yojanagre janama hoibe Biṣṇusharmā gṛuhara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Patitapabana Bana (chij) ntawm Puri Jagannath Temple ya tawm ntawm nws qhov chaw thiab mus rau lwm qhov, tib lub sij hawm ntawd, Mahaprabhu Kalki yuav yug los hauv Jajanagar lossis Jajpur ntawm Odisha hauv tsev ntawm ib tug Brahmin uas hu nkauj qhuas Vajtswv Vishnu.

Kuv yuav tsum qhia kom meej rau cov nyeem tias qhov xwm txheej no tau pom ntau zaus los ntawm Jagannath Temple, piv txwv li thaum xyoo 1990-91, thaum xyoo 2005, tom qab ntawd thaum Cyclone Fani thiab Cyclone Amphan hauv xyoo 2019 thiab 2020 raws li, thiab lwm yam.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika tias Mahaprabhu Kalki yug los yuav tshwm sim tsuas yog tom qab qhov xwm txheej no. Txij li qhov xwm txheej no tau tshwm sim, nws tuaj yeem hais tau tias Vajtswv Kalki twb yug los lawm. Tab sis txij li qhov xwm txheej no tau tshwm sim hauv ntau xyoo sib txawv, nws tsis zoo li yuav ua tau kom paub txog lub xyoo tshwj xeeb ntawm Mahaprabhu yug los ntawm qhov xwm txheej no ib leeg.

Txhawm rau paub tias Mahaprabhu yug los xyoo twg, peb yuav tsum tau txais kev pab los ntawm qee cov nqe lus ntawm Malika.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।
କେଉଁ ଦିନ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ କହେ ରାମଦାସ ଯହିଁ,
ବିଶାଖାପାଟଣା ନୃପ ନାଶ ହେଲେ ମୁହିଁ ବିକାଶ ହୁଅଇ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Māgha saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājatwa karibe ehi Ekāmra nagara.
Keuñ dina tumbhe prakāsha hoiba kahe Rāmadāsa yahiñ,
Viśākhāpāṭaṇā nṛpa nāśa hele muhiñ bikāsha huai.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Los ntawm cov nqe lus saum toj no, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das qhia nws tus thwjtim Ramadas tias thaum ib tug huab tais hu ua Shri Dibyasingha Deb tab tom kav hauv Puri, lub sij hawm ntawd Ananta Keshari, uas yog, Mahaprabhu Kalki, yuav kav hauv Ekamra Nagar lossis Bhubaneswar. Nws yuav yog hnub xya (Saptami Tithi) ntawm lub hli Magha. Tus Mahapurush hais ntxiv tias lub sij hawm ntawd, kuv kuj yuav nyob nrog Vajtswv thiab loj hlob. Qhov no yuav tshwm sim thaum tus Vaj Ntxwv ntawm Visakhapatnam (uas yog, Tus Thawj Fwm Tsav Tebchaws ntawm Andhra Pradesh) tuag.

Yog tias peb xav txog kom zoo, peb yuav pom tias los ntawm qhov cim, tus Mahapurush tab tom tham txog kev tuag ntawm Tus Thawj Fwm Tsav Tebchaws ntawm Andhra Pradesh, Y.S. Rajasekhara Reddy, uas tau tshwm sim hauv kev sib tsoo tsheb helicopter hauv xyoo 2009. Los ntawm cov nqe lus saum toj no, tus Mahapurush tsis qhia txog lub xyoo Vajtswv yug los, tab sis nws yeej hais tias nyob ib ncig ntawm xyoo 2009, Kalki tej Leela yuav txuas ntxiv.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws ‘Jaiphula Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଚୈତନ୍ୟ,
ଜନ୍ମିଛନ୍ତି ଆସି ଏହି ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସତଚାଳିଶକୁ ତୁହି ଘେନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Sehi Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Chaitanya,
janmichhanti āsi ehi martyeṇa lo jāiphula,
satachāḷishaku tuhi ghena.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Vajtswv tib yam uas tau los ua ntej ua Shri Krishna thiab Shri Chaitanya, yuav los rau hauv ntiaj teb no hauv daim ntawv Kalki ntawm tus lej 47.

Nws paub zoo tias tus lej 47 no yog tus Vaj Ntxwv ntawm Puri, Shri Dibyasingha Deb, uas poob rau ntawm xyoo 2005 thiab 2008.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kalpatika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଦ୍ରି ଛାଡି ପ୍ରଭୁ ପୀତବାସ,
ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟରେ ଜନ୍ମ ଲଭିବେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।
ଗୁଣରେ ଭେଦି ଭେଦ ଅଙ୍କେ ଜାଣ,
ଦେଲେ ଯେତେ ସାଲ ହେଲା ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ଏହି ସାଲରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିବେ,
ଅଙ୍କ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଯେ କହିଲୁ ଏବେ।
ବିଂଶ ରେବ ଅଙ୍କ ଯେବଣ ଦିନ,
ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଉଦେ ଗଗନ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ଲକ୍ଷଣ ଦେଖିବୁ ଯେବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇଥିବେ ତେବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Nīḷādri chhāḍi prabhu pītabāsa,
martyare janma labhibe abashya.
guṇare bhedi bheda aṅke jāṇa,
dele jete sāla helā pramāṇa.
ehi sālare prabhu janamibe,
aṅka pramāṇa je kahilu ebe.
biṃsha reba aṅka jebaṇa dina,
indu pare bindu ude gagana.
emanta lakṣaṇa dekhibu jebe,
prabhu janma hoithibe tebe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tus Mahaprabhu yeej yuav los yug rau hauv ntiaj teb tom qab tawm ntawm nws lub chaw nyob Jagannath. Thaum hais txog lub xyoo Mahaprabhu yug los, tus Mahapurush hais tias thaum koj ntxiv nees nkaum (20) rau tus lej ntawm Revati Nakshatra (27), ces tus lej uas tawm los yuav tsum nkag siab tias yog lub xyoo Vajtswv yug los. Qhov ntawd yog, 20+27 = 47.

Nws paub zoo tias tus lej no yog lub xyoo kav (Anka) ntawm tus Vaj Ntxwv ntawm Puri, Dibyasingha Deb, uas qhia txog ib lub xyoo ntawm 2005 thiab 2008.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau dua hauv ib phau ntawv Malika ntawm nws –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଥାମାନ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଘଟିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ବାନା ଧରି ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଧରିଣ କଳକୀ ବେଶ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ehi kathāmāna nishchaya ghaṭiba Dibyasiṃha pañchachāḷisha,
shūnya bānā dhari āsibe Shrī Hari dhariṇa Kaḷakī besha.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum nws yog xyoo 45th ntawm Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb ntawm Puri, ces Vajtswv Shri Hari yuav los yug ua Kalki thiab nqes los rau hauv ntiaj teb.

Nws tsim nyog sau cia tias xyoo 45th ntawm Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb ntawm Puri qhia txog ib xyoo nyob nruab nrab ntawm 2005 thiab 2008.

Achyutananda Das Ji tau sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତି ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ହୋଇବ ସଂସାର,
କାହାରି ନ ଥିବ କୁଳ ବିଚାର।
ବହୁତ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସଂସାରେ ହେବ,
ନିର୍ଭୟ ହୋଇ ଜଣେ କେ ନଥିବ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶେ,
ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କଳକୀ ବେଶେ।
ନାଶିବେ ଯାବତ୍ ଅବନୀଭାର,
ଏଇଠୁ ହୋଇବ ପୃଥବୀ ସଂହାର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ati anyāya hoiba saṃsāra,
kāhāri na thiba kuḷa bichāra.
bahuta utpāta saṃsāre heba,
nirbhaya hoi jaṇe ke nathiba.
Dibyasiṃha deba pañchachāliśe,
āsibe Śrī Hari Kaḷakī beśe.
nāśibe jābat abanībhāra,
eithu hoiba pṛuthabī saṃhāra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, yuav muaj kev tsis ncaj ncees ntau hauv ntiaj teb. Tsis muaj leej twg yuav saib xyuas tsev neeg lossis ua raws li Dharma. Yuav muaj kev kub ntxhov txhua qhov chaw; tsis muaj leej twg yuav siv lawv lub neej nyob kaj siab lug thiab zoo siab. Thaum ntawd, nyob rau xyoo 45th ntawm tus vaj ntxwv hu ua Dibyasingha Deb (nruab nrab ntawm 2005-2008), Vajtswv Shri Hari yuav nqes los rau hauv ntiaj teb no hauv daim ntawv ntawm Kalki. Nws yuav tshem tawm lub nra ntawm kev txhaum ntawm lub ntiaj teb, rhuav tshem cov neeg phem, thiab rhuav tshem cov 'Mlechhas'.

Cov Mahapurushas tau hais txog Vajtswv Kalki yug nyob rau ntau qhov chaw hauv ntau phau ntawv.

Hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika ntawm nws, ‘kalikalpa Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau –

Odia Sloka:

ବିରଜା ପୀଠ ବୈତରଣୀ ନଦୀ,
ଦଶାଶନୀ ଘାଟ ଉଦୟ ଭେଦି।
ସପତ ମାତୃକା ଟେକିଅଛି ଶିର,
ତାଙ୍କ ଅଧରୁ ବହିବ ରୁଧିର।
ସେହି ସଂକେତକୁ ଜାଣିଥାଅ ରାମ,
କଳଙ୍କି ଉଦୟ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Birajā pīṭha Baitaraṇī nadī,
Dashāshanī ghāṭa udaya bhedi.
Sapata mātṛkā ṭekiachhi shira,
tāṅka adharu bahiba rudhira.
Sehi saṅketaku jāṇithāa Rāma,
Kaḷaṅki udaya nishchaya jāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tus Dej Baitarani ntws ze ntawm Lub Tuam Tsev Biraja nyob hauv Jajpur cheeb tsam ntawm Odisha. Ze ntawm Dashashwamedha Ghat ntawm tus Dej Baitarani ntawd yog lub tuam tsev Saptamatruka, qhov chaw uas Xya Leej Niam Vajtswv—xws li Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, thiab Narasimhi—nyob.

Tus Mahapurush sau tias thaum ntshav ntws tawm ntawm daim di ncauj, piv txwv li, lub qhov ncauj, ntawm cov Niam Saptamatruka, nws yuav yog ib qho cim uas cov ntseeg yuav tsum nkag siab tias Vajtswv Kalki tau yug los lossis nws txoj kev yug los ze heev.

Nws yog qhov paub zoo tias qhov xwm txheej no twb tau tshwm sim hauv Lub Tuam Tsev Saptamatruka lawm, ua pov thawj tias Mahapurush Achyutananda Das txoj kev qhia yog qhov tseeb.

Raws li cov pov thawj ntawm Lub Tuam Tsev Biraja, Jajpur, thiab Lub Tuam Tsev Saptamatruka, thiab cov neeg hauv zos nyob ntawd, cov ntshav ntws tawm ntawm lub qhov ncauj ntawm cov Niam Saptamatruka twb tau tshwm sim nyob rau xyoo 2005 lawm.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେ ଦିନ ବିରଜା ଆସ୍ଥାନ ଛୋଡ଼ିବ ଭୀମ ସିଂହନାଦ ହେବ,
ଗର୍ଭସ୍ଥିତ ଶିଶୁଭୂମିରେ ପଡ଼ିବେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ଯିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Je dina Birajā āsthāna choḍiba bhīma siṃhanāda heba,
garbhasthita śiśubhūmire paḍibe Bhubaneśwaraku jiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Ntawm no, tus Mahapurush tab tom piav txog lub sijhawm Mahaprabhu yug los hauv lus cim. Nws sau tias hnub uas Maa Biraja tawm ntawm nws lub rooj zaum, thaum ntawd ib tug menyuam (Vajtswv Kalki) yuav nkag mus rau hauv nws niam lub plab, uas tom qab ntawd yuav mus rau Bhubaneswar.

Nws yog qhov paub zoo tias Maa Biraja tau tawm ntawm nws lub rooj zaum nyob rau xyoo 2004. Nyob rau lub Cuaj Hlis 2004, qee tus neeg nom tswv tau koom tes thiab nyiag tus mlom dawb huv ntawm Maa Biraja (uas tau teeb tsa hauv Lub Tuam Tsev Biraja, Jajpur). Tom qab li 6 lub hlis, nyob rau lub Peb Hlis 2005, tus Niam mlom dawb huv tau rov qab mus rau lub tuam tsev.

Los ntawm rau nqe lus saum toj no, nws yog qhov meej heev tias Mahaprabhu Kalki tau yug los lawm, thiab nws lub sijhawm yog nyob ib ncig ntawm xyoo 2005-2008. Yog li ntawd, cia txhua tus nyeem ntawv nkag siab meej tias tus Kalki avatar tam sim no nyob hauv ntiaj teb.

8.1 Tus Mahaprabhu tau yug nyob qhov twg?

Raws li cov Puranas, Vajtswv Kalki lub chaw yug yog lub zos Sambhal. Tus Bhavishya Malika kuj hais tias Vajtswv Kalki yuav yug hauv lub zos Sambhal. Txhua tus pom zoo rau qhov no.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ କଷଣ ଦେଖି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଭଗବାନ,
କଳିରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇଣ।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ପିତାଙ୍କର ନାମଟି ଅଟଇ,
ମାତା ନାମ ସୁମତି ଯେ କହିଲୁ ବୁଝାଇ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhaktaṅka kaṣaṇa dekhi prabhu bhagabāna,
Kaḷire Sambaḷa nagre janama hoiṇa.
Biṣṇusharmā pitāṅkara nāmaṭi aṭai,
mātā nāma Sumati je kahilu bujhāi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, pom cov ntseeg txoj kev nyuaj siab thaum lub sijhawm Kali era, Vajtswv Kalki yuav yug hauv Sambal Nagar. Tus Mahaprabhu txiv lub npe yuav yog Vishnusharma thiab nws niam lub npe yuav yog Sumati.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau ntxiv hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ହେବେ ଜନମ,
ଛତିଆ ବଟରେ ଲାଗିବ ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି ସଂଗ୍ରାମ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Sambaḷa nagare prabhu se gupte hebe janama,
Chhatiā baṭare lāgiba dibārātri saṅgrāma.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Mahaprabhu Kalki yuav yug zais cia hauv Sambal Nagar. Kev sib ntaus sib tua lossis kev ua tsov rog nruab hnub nruab hmo yuav tshwm sim ntawm Chhatia Bata.

Achyutananda Das Ji tau sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାରୁ ମହରଗ ରାଜକର ଆସି ଘରେ ଘରେ ପଶିଯିବ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ କଳ୍କୀ ଉଦୟ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Māru maharaga rājakara āsi ghare ghare paśijiba,
bāraṇa beḷare Sambaḷanagre Kaḷkī udaya hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kev tuag, kev tshaib plab, thiab se nkag mus rau txhua lub tsev thiab kev kub ntxhov kis thoob plaws txhua qhov chaw, Vajtswv Kalki yuav tshwm sim hauv Sambal Nagar thaum lub sijhawm kawg ntawm Kaliyuga.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau ntxiv hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କହନ୍ତି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଭକତ ମୋ ଗଳା ମାଳ,
ଭକତ ନିମନ୍ତେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ ହେବି କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kahanti Achyuta śuṇa Rāmadāsa bhakata mo gaḷā māḷa,
bhakata nimante Sambaḷanagre hebi Kaḷkī abatāra.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳକୀର ସୀମା କାଳ ପୂରିଗଲେ କଳ୍କୀ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଯଶା ପୁରେ,
ସମ୍ବଳ ଗ୍ରାମରେ ଜାତ ହୋଇଥିବେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାର କାଳରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷakīra sīmā kāḷa pūrigale Kaḷkī Biṣṇujashā pure,
Sambaḷa grāmare jāta hoithibe mlechha saṃhāra kāḷare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Thaum Kaliyuga lub sijhawm txwv tiav, Kalki yuav yug hauv Vishnuyasha lub tsev hauv lub zos Sambal rau kev rhuav tshem cov Mlechhas."

Txawm li cas los xij, qhov chaw 'Sambal' nyob yog ib qho teeb meem uas ntau tus neeg tseem tsis pom zoo txog niaj hnub no. Hauv tsab xov xwm no, peb yuav daws qhov no.

8.2 Tus Bhavishya Malika hais li cas txog qhov teeb meem no?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Jaiphula Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ବେଳ କାଳ ଜାଣି,
ଓଡିଶାରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନ୍ମିବେ ପୁଣି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କେହି ତାଙ୍କ ମାୟାକୁ ନ ଚିହ୍ନି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Sehi beḷa kāḷa jāṇi,
Oḍishāre prabhu janmibe puṇi lo jāiphula,
kehi tāṅka māyāku na chinhi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum lub sijhawm ntawd thaum kev txhaum nyob rau theem siab tshaj plaws, Vajtswv Kalki yuav yug hauv lub xeev Odisha. Tus Vajtswv txoj kev dag yuav muaj zog heev uas tsis muaj leej twg yuav paub nws.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau dua hauv ib phau ntawv Malika ntawm nws –

Odia Sloka:

ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁରେ କଳ୍କୀର ଜନମ,
ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ରାଜହଂସ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଜନମି ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ଏହା କହିଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ୍ୟ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Utkala bhuiñre Kaḷkīra janama,
grāma nāma Rājahansa.
Kaḷkī janami līḷā prakāśiba,
ehā kahili prakāshya.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Kalki txoj kev yug yuav nyob rau thaj av Utkala (Odisha), hauv ib lub zos hu ua Rajahansa. Tom qab yug los, Kalki yuav qhia nws txoj kev ua si dawb huv."

Tam sim no, hais txog qhov chaw hauv Odisha uas Vajtswv Kalki yuav yug, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das nws tus kheej tau sau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika, ‘Bhabishya Jatipataka Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ନଦୀ ତୀର,
ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁବିପ୍ର ଦ୍ୱିଜବର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Oḍiśā deśare gupta Gaṅgā nadī tīra,
Sambaḷa nagre Biṣṇubipra dwijabara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tus Mahaprabhu lub chaw yug yuav nyob ze ntawm ntug dej Gupta Ganga (Dej Baitarani) hauv Sambal Nagar, Odisha. Nyob rau qhov chaw ntawd, hauv ib tug Brahmin siab zoo uas hu nkauj qhuas Vajtswv Vishnu lub tsev, Mahaprabhu Kalki yuav yug.

Los ntawm ob nqe lus saum toj no, nws yog qhov meej heev tias raws li tus Bhavishya Malika, Vajtswv Kalki yuav yug hauv Sambal, uas nyob hauv lub xeev Odisha ntawm Is Nrias teb.

8.3 Sambal nyob qhov twg hauv Odisha?

Ib tug ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Jagannath Das, tau sau hauv phau ntawv Malika ‘Bhabishyat Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରରେ,
ଦୁଇ ଜଣ ଅଛନ୍ତି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଘରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Jājapura Sambaḷa nagrare,
dui jaṇa achhanti brāhmaṇa ghare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hauv nqe lus saum toj no, tus Mahapurush sau – “Jajapura Sambala nagrare”. Qhov ntawd yog, “Hauv Sambal Nagar ntawm Jajpur”. Nws yog qhov meej tias Sambal Nagar nyob hauv Jajpur.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷୀର ନଦୀ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଯେ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ଖ୍ୟାତ ଯେ କମଳପୁର ଗୁପ୍ତ ଯେ ସମ୍ବଳ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kṣhīra nadī dakṣhiṇa je Birajā maṇḍala,
khyāta je Kamalapura gupta je Sambaḷa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, sab qab teb ntawm tus dej Khira thiab sab qaum teb ntawm tus Mahanadi yog thaj chaw tuam tsev Biraja. Ze ntawm thaj chaw Biraja no yog ib lub zos hu ua Kamalapur. Qhov no yog qhov uas zais cia hu ua lub zos Sambal.

Los ntawm nqe lus no, tus Mahapurush tau qhia tias Sambal nyob hauv thaj chaw Biraja lossis Jajpur cheeb tsam ntawm Odisha.

Cia peb kawm seb Sambal nyob qhov twg hauv Odisha los ntawm qee cov nqe lus ntxiv.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Gurubhakti Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘର,
ଜନମିଣ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଚକ୍ରଧର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Biṣṇusharmā ghara,
janamiṇa tapasyā karibe Chakradhara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Chakradhara (Vajtswv Vishnu) yuav yug los hauv tsev ntawm ib tug neeg pehawm Vajtswv nyob hauv Sambal Nagar, Gaya, thiab tom qab ntawd yuav ua kev txhiv txoj kev txhaum.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das rov sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଜାତ,
ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Biṣṇusharmā gruhe prabhu hoibe jāta,
Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Harihara kṣhetra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Vajtswv yuav yug los hauv tsev ntawm Vishnusharma, hauv cheeb tsam Harihara ntawm Sambal Nagar, Gaya."

Raws li ob nqe lus saum toj no, Vajtswv yuav yug los hauv Sambal Nagar ntawm Gaya. Tam sim no, Vajtswv Krishna nws tus kheej tau qhia rau nws kaum ob tus Gopas tias Gaya yuav yog qhov twg thiab nws nyob qhov twg hauv Odisha, uas Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau hais hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Biraja Mahatmya’. Tus Mahapurush sau –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାର ସୁତ ନିହାର ବଚନ ଏ ଅଟେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଠାର,
ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥେ ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଗ୍ରାମଟି ସମ୍ବଳ ପୁର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Śuṇa bāra suta nihāra bachana e aṭe Achyuta ṭhāra,
Nābhigayā tīrthe Harihara kṣhetra grāmaṭi Sambaḷa pura.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Mloog, O kaum ob tug tub, qhov no yog Achyuta cov lus tseeb: lub zos Sambalpur nyob hauv cheeb tsam Harihara ntawm qhov chaw mus pehawm Nabhigaya."

Qhov ntawd yog, cheeb tsam Harihara nyob hauv qhov chaw mus pehawm Nabhigaya, uas hu ua Sambal.

Hauv cov lus sib tham saum toj no, peb tau pom ntau qhov chaw tias tus Mahapurush tau piav qhia Mahaprabhu qhov chaw yug los yog cheeb tsam Biraja lossis Gaya (Nabhigaya) qhov chaw mus pehawm. Tam sim no peb yuav paub meej tias cov cheeb tsam no nyob qhov twg, cov lus dab neeg cuam tshuam nrog lawv yog dab tsi, thiab lawv qhov tseem ceeb yog dab tsi.

8.3.1 Cheeb Tsam Biraja

Lub 'Tuam Tsev Biraja' yog ib lub tuam tsev loj nyob ntawm ntug Dej Baitarani hauv Jajpur, Odisha, qhov chaw uas Adi Shakti raug pehawm hauv daim ntawv 'Biraja'. Qhov tseeb, thaum ub, Brahma Ji nws tus kheej tau ua Yajna (kev fij) ntawm no ntawm Brahmakunda, tom qab ntawd Niam Parvati tau tawm los ntawm hluav taws fij thiab qhia Brahma Ji kom tsim nws los ntawm lub npe 'Biraja'. Cheeb tsam no kuj hu ua cheeb tsam Biraja raws li lub npe ntawm Vajtswv poj niam Biraja.

Nws yog ntawm qhov chaw no uas Vajtswv poj niam Sati lub plab (Nabhi) poob, ua rau nws yog ib qho ntawm 51 Shakti Peethas. Qhov no yog tib lub tuam tsev uas Vajtswv poj niam raug pehawm hauv daim ntawv Mahishasura Mardini nrog ob txhais caj npab.

Qhov chaw no kuj muaj npe nrov ua Nabhigaya rau Pinda Daan (kev fij rau cov poj koob yawm txwv). Qhov tseeb, nws yog tib lub Shakti Peetha uas Pinda Daan raug ua. Nyob ze Brahmakunda, muaj ib lub qhov dej tob hu ua Nabhikunda, uas cov neeg siv nws cov dej los ua Pinda Daan rau lawv cov poj koob yawm txwv. Qhov chaw no kuj tau hais hauv Skanda Purana.

8.3.2 Nabhigaya

Nws tau hais hauv Puranas tias hauv Satya Yuga, muaj ib tug dab hu ua Gayasura. Txawm hais tias nws yug los hauv ib tsev neeg dab, nws lub siab tau txhawb los ntawm Dharma thiab kev txhiv txoj kev txhaum. Nws yog tus ntseeg Vajtswv heev, muaj zog heev, thiab muaj lub cev loj heev. Nws ib txwm ua haujlwm rau lub ntiaj teb kev noj qab nyob zoo thiab lwm tus kev pab. Nws tau hais hauv Puranas tias tus dab tau ua kev txhiv txoj kev txhaum hnyav, uas Vajtswv Vishnu tau txaus siab, thiab Vajtswv tau hais kom nws thov ib qho koob hmoov. Gayasura thov ib qho koob hmoov tshwj xeeb. Nws hais tias, “Thov kom cov neeg mus txog ntuj ceeb tsheej tsuas yog los ntawm kuv txoj kev kov.” Vajtswv Vishnu tau muab qhov koob hmoov no pom nws txoj kev txhiv txoj kev txhaum hnyav. Tab sis vim li ntawd, Yamaloka (lub ntiaj teb ntawm cov tuag) pib ua neeg tsis muaj neeg nyob, vim tias leej twg Gayasura kov tau mus ncaj qha rau ntuj ceeb tsheej. Qhov no tau dhau los ua ib qho teeb meem loj rau cov vajtswv thiab Yamraj. Tag nrho cov vajtswv tau mus cuag Trinity (Brahma, Vishnu, Mahesh) thiab thov kom nrhiav kev daws teeb meem no. Cov vajtswv tau thov Trinity kom nres Gayasura. Trinity tau txiav txim siab nres Gayasura, uas tau ua rau muaj teeb meem hauv kev tsim txoj cai txawm tias nws yog tus ncaj ncees. Lawv peb leeg tau hloov ua peb tus Brahmins, mus cuag Gayasura, thiab hais tias lawv xav ua Yajna tsis tu ncua rau xya hnub thiab rau qhov no, lawv xav tau ib qho chaw loj thiab dawb huv. Lawv tau hais kom Gayasura muab nws lub cev rau Yajna, tab sis nws yuav tsum tsis txhob txav rau xya hnub no. Gayasura pom zoo rau qhov no. Gayasura tau nthuav nws lub taub hau hauv Gaya (Bihar) qhov chaw Vajtswv Vishnu zaum, muab nws lub plab hauv Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha) qhov chaw Brahma Ji zaum, thiab muab nws ob txhais taw hauv Pithapuram (Andhra Pradesh) qhov chaw Vajtswv Shiva zaum, thiab yog li ntawd Yajna pib. Trinity tau ua Yajna tsis tu ncua rau xya hnub.

Gayasura tau suav hnub los ntawm kev hnov ​​tus qaib qw thaum sawv ntxov. Paub qhov no, hnub kawg, Vajtswv Shiva tau hloov ua tus qaib thiab qw ua ntej hmo ntuj yuav tag. Hnov tus qaib lub suab, Gayasura xav tias nws yog sawv ntxov thiab txav nws lub cev xav tias xya hnub tau tag. Trinity tam sim ntawd tshwm sim rau nws thiab hais tias Gayasura tau ua txhaum nws cov lus cog tseg kom nws lub cev nyob twj ywm rau xya hnub. Lawv hais tias kev rau txim rau kev ua txhaum cov lus cog tseg yog kev tuag. Tus ncaj ncees Gayasura tau txais qhov no yam tsis muaj kev yws. Trinity tau txaus siab rau nws txoj kev ntseeg Vajtswv thiab hais kom nws thov ib qho koob hmoov. Tom qab ntawd Gayasura thov lawv kom nws lub npe raug nco txog nrog Trinity. Nws thov lawv kom kho kom zoo nkauj nws lub cev nrog cov khaub ncaws ntawm Brahmins. Nws kuj thov koob hmoov tias yog leej twg cov poj koob yawm txwv tau txais kev fij (Pinda Daan thiab Tarpan) ntawm peb qhov chaw no, lawv yuav tsum tsis txhob rov yug dua tshiab.

Txais Gayasura qhov kev xav, Vajtswv Vishnu tau hloov ua Gadadhar hauv Gaya, Brahma Ji raug pehawm hauv daim ntawv Girija Devi hauv Nabhigaya, thiab Vajtswv Shiva raug pehawm hauv Pithapuram ua Kukkuteshwar (Vajtswv uas tau hloov ua tus qaib). Hauv txoj kev no, peb qhov chaw no tau muaj npe nrov rau Pinda Daan thiab Tarpan:

  • Shirogaya (Gaya, Bihar)
  • Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha)
  • Padagaya (Pithapuram, Andhra Pradesh)

Cov pov thawj tias qhov chaw mus pehawm Nabhigaya nyob hauv Jajpur kuj pom muaj hauv cov ntawv Malika ntawm Panchasakha Mahapurushas.

Hauv phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁରଠାରେ ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥ ଏକଥା ଜାଣ ସତ୍ୱର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Jājapurathāre Nābhigayā tīrtha ekathā jāṇa satwara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Paub qhov no nrog kev paub tseeb, qhov chaw mus pehawm Nabhigaya nyob hauv Jajpur."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, thaum kwv yees nws qhov kev yug los thib kaum txog 600 xyoo dhau los, tau sau hauv phau ntawv Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶମ ଜନମ ମୋହର ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଯାଜପୁର ନାଭିଗୟା ହରିହର ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Daśama janama mohara hoiba prakāśa,
Jājapura Nābhigayā Harihara khyāta.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Kuv qhov kev yug los thib kaum yuav tshwm sim hauv cheeb tsam Harihara nto moo ntawm Nabhigaya, Jajpur."

Los ntawm nqe lus no ib yam nkaus, nws yog qhov tseeb tias qhov chaw mus pehawm Nabhigaya nyob hauv Jajpur.

Tus Mahapurush sau txog tib lub ntsiab lus no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ପଞ୍ଚକୋଶ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ନାଭିଗୟା ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘରେ ହେବ ଖେଳ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Jājapura pañchakośa Birajā maṇḍala,
Nābhigayā Biṣṇusharmā ghare heba kheḷa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cheeb tsam Biraja nyob hauv tsib kosha ncig ntawm Jajpur. Vajtswv Kalki yuav yug los hauv tsev ntawm ib tus neeg pehawm Vishnu nyob hauv tib cheeb tsam Biraja ntawd, Nabhigaya.

Yog li ntawd, los ntawm cov lus sib tham saum toj no, nws yog qhov tseeb tias Sambal Nagar, Gaya qhov chaw mus pehawm, thiab cheeb tsam Biraja qhov chaw uas Panchasakhas tau kwv yees Vajtswv Kalki yug los, yog qhov tseeb nyob hauv Jajpur koog tsev kawm ntawv ntawm Odisha.

Txhawm rau kom nkag siab ntau ntxiv txog Vajtswv Kalki qhov chaw yug los, peb yuav coj qee cov nqe lus ntxiv uas tau hais hauv Malika.

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଉଶ୍ୱାସୀ ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷkī abatāre Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśi bhaktaṅku uśwāsī dharmaku tumbhe pāḷiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Kalki Avatar yuav yug los hauv lub xeev Odisha. Mahaprabhu Kalki yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg phem, cawm cov neeg pehawm Vajtswv, thiab rov tsim Dharma rau hauv ntiaj teb.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, hais Vajtswv cov lus, sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଦେଖିବ ଜାଣ,
ଆମ୍ଭ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ ଯାଜପୁରେଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Indu pare bindu dekhiba jāṇa,
āmbha ābirbhāba Jājapureṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum pom ib lub cim los yog ib lub hnub qub saum lub hli hauv ntuj, paub tias tus Tswv Tshaj Plaws yuav tshwm sim hauv Jajpur.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Jai Birachita Chautisha’, sau thaum rov hais Vajtswv cov lus tiag tiag –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେତେବେଳେ ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡିକରି ଯିବୁ,
ଯାଜପୁର ବିପ୍ର ଘରେ ଯାଇଁ ଜନମିବୁ।
ଜଗିଥିବୁ ହେ। ଯେ ଯେତେ କହିଲେ ନ ଶୁଣିବୁ ହେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Jetebeḷe Nīḷāchaḷa chhāḍikari jibu,
Jājapura bipra ghare jāiñ janamibu.
Jagithibu he. Je jete kahile na śuṇibu he.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Thaum koj tawm hauv Nilachala (Puri), koj yuav mus yug hauv ib tug Brahmin lub tsev hauv Jajpur. Ceev faj; tsis txhob mloog leej twg hais dab tsi."

Hauv phau ntawv Malika ‘Tatwabodhini’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହେବେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଭାବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Jājanagre prabhu janama hebe,
janama hoibe brāhmaṇa bhābe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Vajtswv yuav yug hauv Jajanagar; Nws yuav yug los ua ib tug Brahmin."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Bigyanakalpa’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ମହାପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ,
ଗରୁଡକୁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନି ଲୀଳା ଯେ କରିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Jājanagre Mahāprabhu janama hoibe,
Garuḍaku saṅge gheni līḷā je karibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Mahaprabhu yuav yug hauv Jajanagar; nqa Garuda nrog, nws yuav ua nws txoj kev ua si dawb ceev."

Los ntawm cov nqe lus saum toj no, tsis muaj kev tsis ntseeg ntxiv lawm tias Mahaprabhu Kalki yug los yuav tshwm sim hauv Sambal Nagar, Nabhigaya chaw mus pe hawm, Biraja cheeb tsam, Jajpur koog tsev kawm ntawv, Odisha.

Yog peb saib phau Bhagavata Purana thiab Mahabharata uas tau sau ua ntej Bhavishya Malika, lawv kuj qhia mus rau Jajpur, Odisha txog Tus Tswv Kalki qhov chaw yug.

Thaum ub, Maharishi Vedavyas tau hais tias Tus Tswv Kalki qhov chaw yug yog 'Sambhal zos' hauv Shrimad Bhagavata Mahapurana. Nws tau sau –

Sanskrit Sloka:

संभलग्राममुख्यस्य ब्राह्मणस्य महात्मनः ।
भवने विष्णुयशसः कल्किः प्रादुर्भविष्यति ।।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Śambhalagrāmamukhyasya brāhmaṇasya mahātmanaḥ |
bhavane viṣṇuyaśasaḥ kalkiḥ prādurbhaviṣyati ||

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
"Hauv ib tug Brahmin uas muaj siab zoo, tus thawj coj ntawm Sambhal zos, hu ua Vishnuyasha, Tus Tswv Kalki yuav tshwm sim."

Tom qab ntawd, hauv Mahabharata 'Vanaparva', Maharishi Vyas nws tus kheej tau hloov kho qhov no me ntsis thiab hu Tus Tswv Kalki qhov chaw yug ua 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama'. Nws sau –

Sanskrit Sloka:

कल्कि विष्णुयशा नाम द्विजः कालप्रचोदितः ।
उत्पत्स्यते महावीर्यो महाबुद्धिपराक्रमः ।।
सम्भूतः सम्भलग्रामे ब्राह्मणावसथे शुभे ।
मनसा तस्य सर्वाणि वाहनान्यायुधानि च ।।
उपस्थास्यन्ति योधाश्च शस्त्राणि कवचानि च ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kalki Vishnuyashā nāma dwijaḥ kālaprachoditaḥ |
utpatsyate mahāvīryo mahābuddhiparākramaḥ ||
sambhūtaḥ sambhalagrāme brāhmaṇāvasathe shubhe |
manasā tasya sarvāṇi vāhanānyāyudhāni cha ||
upasthāsyanti yodhāshcha shastrāṇi kavachāni cha |

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Los ntawm cov nqe lus saum toj no, Maharishi Vyas tau hais tias Tus Tswv Kalki yuav yug hauv 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama', txhais tau tias yog ib lub zos Sambhal 'tau tsim' lossis 'tau teeb tsa', ib lub zos Sambhal uas yuav tau tsim los ua ib pawg neeg Brahmin rau kev ua Yajnas thiab pe hawm. Nws sau tias Tus Tswv Kalki yuav yug hauv ib tug Brahmin zoo uas nyob hauv lub zos Sambhal no thiab qhuas Tus Tswv Vishnu lub hwj chim.

Tam sim no, yog peb tshawb fawb thiab tshawb nrhiav hauv keeb kwm, peb yuav pom tias cov neeg Brahmin uas tau tsim los rau Yajna thiab pe hawm nyob hauv Jajpur koog tsev kawm ntawv ntawm Odisha.

Qhov tseeb, lub npe Jajpur tau muab rau xyoo pua 10th tom qab lub npe ntawm Somavamsi Vaj Ntxwv Jajati (Yayati). Vaj Ntxwv Jajati tau tsim lub nroog no thiab ua Abhinav Jajatinagar nws lub peev. Tom qab ntawd, hloov nws lub npe, nws tau ua Jajanagar ua ntej thiab tom qab ntawd Jajpur. Vaj Ntxwv Jajati Keshari tau coj txog 10,000 tus Brahmin los ntawm Kannauj hauv Uttar Pradesh thiab teeb tsa lawv hauv Jajpur, Odisha, los ua kaum Ashwamedha Yajnas (Dashashwamedha). Txawm niaj hnub no, Dashashwamedha Ghat ntawm ntug dej Baitarani tseem ceeb rau peb txog qhov xwm txheej no. Txawm li cas los xij, Maharishi Vyas tau qhia rau pawg neeg Brahmin no uas tau teeb tsa nyob rau sab hnub tuaj ntawm Biraja lub tuam tsev hauv Jajpur los ntawm kev hu nws 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama' hauv Mahabharata 'Vanaparva'.

9 Khandagiri – Hauv Bhubaneswar los yog lwm qhov chaw?

Txhua tus neeg txaus siab rau Bhavishya Malika tsis muaj kev tsis ntseeg txog qhov chaw Mahaprabhu Kalki yuav yug. Lawv paub zoo heev tias Kalki avatar yuav yug hauv Sambal zos nyob hauv Jajpur, Odisha.

Lawv kuj tsis muaj kev tsis ntseeg tias tom qab yug hauv Jajpur, Tus Tswv yuav nyob hauv ib qho chaw hu ua Khandagiri.

Ib tug ntawm Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das, tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଳିଯୁଗେ କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ହରି,
ଶ୍ରୀ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ehi kaliyuge Kalki abatare Hari,
Shri khaṇḍagirire thibe prabhu bije kari.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, hauv Kaliyuga, Tus Tswv Hari yuav nyob hauv Khandagiri hauv Nws tus Kalki incarnation.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୋପନେ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଯେ ଆସି,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ବସି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Gopane Mukunda je asi,
Khandagirire thibe basi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Tus Tswv yuav nyob zais cia hauv Khandagiri.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das tau sau dua hauv ib phau ntawv Malika, 'Patta Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକତ ସୁମେଳ ହୋଇ ଏକ ଠୁଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଶେ ଯିବେ,
ଅନନ୍ତ ମୂରତି ଦର୍ଶନ ସାରିଣ ସର୍ବେ ସର୍ବଙ୍କୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhakata sumeḷa hoi eka ṭhuḷa Khaṇḍagiri pāshe jibe,
ananta mūrati darshana sāriṇa sarbe sarbaṅku chinhibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, ntawm ib qho chaw hu ua Khandagiri, yuav muaj kev sib sau ua ke ntawm cov neeg pe hawm. Nyob ntawd, cov neeg pe hawm yuav tau pom (darshan) Tus Tswv Kalki thiab yuav paub ib leeg.

Txog rau qhov no, txhua tus pom zoo, tab sis tib neeg txoj kev tsis ntseeg tshwm sim txog qhov chaw Khandagiri no nyob. Niaj hnub no, los ntawm qee cov yeeb yaj kiab thiab PDF, muaj ntau yam kev thov txog Khandagiri. Raws li qee tus neeg, qhov chaw hu ua Khandagiri no, qhov chaw Tus Tswv yuav nyob, yog nyob hauv Jajpur, Odisha, thaum qee tus hais tias Khandagiri nyob hauv Dhenkanal koog tsev kawm ntawv ntawm Odisha.

9.1 Khandagiri nyob qhov twg?

Los ntawm tsab xov xwm no, cia kuv hais tias txhua lub npe uas tau hais saum toj no txog Khandagiri (Jajpur, Dhenkanal), lawv ob leeg yog qhov tsis raug kiag li.

Piav qhia tias Khandagiri nyob qhov twg, ib tug ntawm Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Basanta Manjari’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମହିମା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଭକ୍ତ ତହିଁ ସେଠାରେ ଅଛଇ।
ଗୁପତ ଲୀଳା ହୋଇବ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ,
ତପସ୍ୟାରେ ରହିଥିବେ ଋଷିଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗରେ।
ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ସେକାଳରେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବ,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସେହି ଭସାଇଣ ଦେବ।
ତ୍ରିଭୁବନ ସ୍ୱର କଥା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ବାରଭୁଜା ଦୁର୍ଗାମାତା ଜଗି ରହିଛଇଁ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ବାବୁ ଶୁଣ ମନଦେଇ,
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଜଗିଅଛି ସେଠାବକୁ ତହିଁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Khaṇḍagiri mahimā kahita nuhañi,
lakṣha lakṣha bhakta tahin seṭhāre achhai.
Gupata līḷā hoiba Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre,
tapasyāre rahithibe ruṣhiṅka saṅgare.
Gupta Gaṅgā sekāḷare bāhara hoiba,
Bhubaneśwaraku sehi bhasāiṇa deba.
Tribhubana swara kathā kahita nuhañi,
bārabhuja Durgāmātā jagi rahichhaiṃ.
Āhuri kathāe bābu śuṇa manadei,
Bhūāsuṇī jagiachhi seṭhābaku tahiñ.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Khandagiri lub hwj chim yog qhov piav tsis tau. Muaj ntau lab tus neeg pe hawm nyob ntawd. Leela zais cia yuav tshwm sim hauv Khandagiri. Muaj ntau tus neeg txawj ntse thiab cov hauj sam tab tom ua kev txhiv txoj kev txom nyem nyob ntawd. Ib tus dej zais cia (Gupta Ganga) tab tom ntws nyob ntawd, uas yuav ua rau muaj kev puas tsuaj hauv Bhubaneswar thaum Kalki txoj Leela rhuav tshem. Bhubaneswar lub hwj chim kuj loj heev. Nyob ntawd, Niam Barabhuji thiab Niam Bhuasuni nyob, uas yuav ua txoj hauj lwm rhuav tshem thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga.

Hauv cov shloka saum toj no, Mahapurush tau qhia meej tias Khandagiri nyob hauv Bhubaneswar.

Piav qhia Khandagiri qhov chaw nyob, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das kuj tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ,
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପକୁ ଧ୍ୟାନ କରନ୍ତି।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହିଯାଏ ଗୁପ୍ତର,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ହୁଏ ସତ୍ୱର।
ଘଟଣା ସ୍ଥାନ ଯେ ଅଟଇ ସେହି,
କଳିର ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ସେଠାହିଁ।
ଉଦୟଗିରି ଯେ ଅପୋଡା ସ୍ଥାନ,
ସେଠାବରେ ବହୁ ସାଧୁ ଆଶ୍ରମ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhubaneśwarare lāgiba goḷa, ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sādhu achhanti, Kaḷkī rūpaku dhyāna karanti.
Kṣhīra dhāra bahijāe guptara, gumphāre bhajana hue satwara.
Ghaṭaṇā sthāna je aṭai sehi, Kaḷira bhārata yuddha seṭhāhiñ.
Udayagiri je apoḍā sthāna, seṭhābare bahu sādhu āśrama.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, yuav muaj kev sib ntaus sib tua hauv Bhubaneswar, thiab Ekamra hav zoov nyob ntawd. Tom qab ntawd Mahapurush tau hais tias Khandagiri kuj nyob ntawd, qhov chaw muaj ntau tus neeg txawj ntse thiab cov neeg dawb huv yuav nyob thiab niaj hnub hu Kalki lub npe thiab xav txog Nws daim duab. Hauv cov qhov tsua ntawm Khandagiri, nruab hnub hmo yuav muaj bhajans thiab kirtans. Nyob ntawd, ib tus dej zais cia (Kshira Nadi) kuj yuav ntws. Khandagiri ntawd yuav yog "Qhov Chaw Tshwm Sim", qhov chaw kev sib ntaus sib tua ntawm Mahabharata yuav tshwm sim. Nyob ze yog Udayagiri, qhov chaw cov chaw nyob dawb ceev ntawm ntau tus neeg dawb huv tau nyob.

Los ntawm cov shloka saum toj no, Mahapurush tau qhia meej tias Khandagiri qhov chaw muaj ntau tus neeg dawb huv yuav nyob, qhov chaw kev hu Kalki lub npe zais cia yuav mus ntxiv hauv qhov tsua, thiab qhov chaw kev sib ntaus sib tua ntawm Mahabharata yuav tshwm sim, yog nyob hauv Bhubaneswar, qhov chaw Ekamra hav zoov kuj nyob.

Ntawm no kuv xav kom cov neeg nyeem saib xyuas ib qho tseem ceeb. Hauv Malika cov ntawv, Bhubaneswar feem ntau hu ua Ekamra Kshetra, Ekamra Van, lossis Ekamra Kanan. Qhov tseeb, raws li xyoo pua 13th Ekamra Purana, yav tas los tsuas muaj ib tsob ntoo txiv nkhaus taw nyob ze qhov chaw ntawm tus vajtswv Lingaraj. Yog li ntawd, Lingaraj cheeb tsam kuj hu ua Ekamra Kshetra (thaj chaw uas muaj ib tsob ntoo txiv nkhaus taw).

Qhov no yog vim li cas Mahapurushas tau ntau zaus hu Bhubaneswar Lingaraj Kshetra (thaj chaw ntawm Lingaraj lub tuam tsev) lossis Ekamra Van Kshetra (thaj chaw ntawm ib tsob ntoo txiv nkhaus taw).

Tsis yog qhov no xwb, vim yog Bindu Sarovar qhov chaw nyob hauv Bhubaneswar, Panchasakhas hauv lawv cov ntawv Malika kuj tau hais txog Bhubaneswar ua Bindu Sarovar cheeb tsam.

Mahapurush tau sau qhia meej hauv 'Garuda Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ଏ ସବୁ ମୋ ଗୁପ୍ତ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଆଉ ପୃଥ୍ବୀରେ ଯେତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନ ପୁରାଣେ ଅଛି ବର୍ଣ୍ଣନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhubaneśwara je ekāmra kānana e sabu mo gupta sthāna,
āu pruthwīre jete siddha sthāna purāṇe achhi barṇana.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus Tswv Krishna qhia Nws tus neeg pe hawm, Garuda, tias Ekamra Kanan nyob hauv Bhubaneswar yog ib qho chaw zais cia thiab Siddha chaw (chaw zoo tag nrho).

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Chhayalisha Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଏକାମ୍ରରେ,
ରହିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ତିନିଘରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhubaneśwara Ekāmrare, rahibe bhakta tini ghare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov neeg pe hawm Tus Tswv yuav nyob hauv Bhubaneswar Ekamra cheeb tsam.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Chumbaka Malika' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ସୀମା ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ,
ପଞ୍ଚବିଂଶ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବେ ତଟରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhubaneśwara sīmā Khaṇḍagirire, pañchabiṃśha bhakta thibe taṭare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Khandagiri nyob ntawm ciam teb ntawm Bhubaneswar qhov chaw 25 tus neeg pe hawm yuav koom nrog kev ua si dawb ceev.

Mahapurush sau ntxiv hauv 'Chakada Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଗୋଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାର,
ଭାବି କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ନିଜ ନାମ ସ୍ମର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhubaneśwarare goḷa Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāra,
bhābi kahai Achyutī nija nāma smara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, yuav muaj kev sib ntaus sib tua hauv Khandagiri cheeb tsam hauv Bhubaneswar.

Ib tug ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଠାରେ ଜାଣ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସେ ସରୋବର ପୁଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhubaneśwara ṭhāre jāṇa,
Bindu se sarobara puṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, paub tias Bindu Sarovar nyob hauv Bhubaneswar.

Mahapurush Yasovanta Das sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜ ସ୍ଥଳୀ,
ଦୁଇ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକଜଣ ସ୍ତିରୀ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhubaneśwara Liṅgarāja sthaḷī,
dui puruṣha ekajaṇa stirī.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Bhubaneswar yog Lingaraj lub chaw nyob, txhais tau tias Lingaraj lub tuam tsev nyob hauv Bhubaneswar.

Yog tias peb siv Google pab, peb tseem tuaj yeem paub yooj yim tias txawm yog Khandagiri los yog Ekamra Kanan los yog Bindu Sarovar los yog Lingaraj lub tuam tsev, tag nrho cov no nyob hauv Bhubaneswar tib si.

Khandagiri, uas yuav yog tus Tswv lub chaw nyob, qhov chaw uas ntau tus dawb huv yuav hu Kalki lub npe—los piav qhia tias Khandagiri no nyob hauv Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Kshetra), Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau dua nyob rau hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଦଦେଶେ ଭରତ ପାଦୁକା,
ଛଦ୍ମବେଶୀ ସାଧୁ ପୂଜା କରୁଅଛି ଏକା।
ଗୁପ୍ତଗଙ୍ଗା ବହିଅଛି ଗୁପ୍ତ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଗୁପ୍ତ ବେଶେ ରହିଛନ୍ତି ପୁରି।
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଅଟଇ ଶିବ ଯୋଗ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶୁକପକ୍ଷୀ ପାଇଲା ଯେଉଁଠି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଜ୍ଞାନ।
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଦେବୀ ଅଛି ଜାଣିଥା ସତ୍ୱର,
ସେ ଦେବୀ ଘାତରେ ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ କମ୍ପମାନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Khaṇḍagiri pādadeśe Bharata pādukā, chhadmabeśī sādhu pūjā karuachhi ekā.
Guptagaṅgā bahiachhi gupta Khaṇḍagiri, siddha sādhu gupta beśe rahichhanti puri.
Ekāmra bana aṭai Śiba yoga sthāna, śukapakṣhī pāilā jeuṇṭhi brahmajñāna.
Bhūāsuṇୀ debī achhi jāṇithā satwara, se debī ghātare pruthibī heba kampamāna.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, ntawm Khandagiri roob ko taw yog Bharata Paduka, uas ib tug dawb huv zais ntshis tab tom pe hawm. Nyob rau hauv tib lub Khandagiri ntawd, Gupta Ganga tus dej tab tom ntws zais ntshis. Nyob ntawd (hauv Khandagiri), ntau tus dawb huv uas tau ua tiav tab tom nyob zais ntshis. Ekamra hav zoov nyob ntawd, uas yog tus Tswv Shiva qhov chaw ua Yoga. Nyob ntawd, Shuka noog ib zaug tau txais Brahma-kev paub. Nyob ze yog Vajtswv poj niam Bhuasuni, uas yuav ua txoj haujlwm rhuav tshem hauv ntiaj teb thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga.

Kuv xav qhia rau cov nyeem ntawm no tias nyob rau hauv ib qho ntawm cov nqe lus saum toj no, Mahapurush tau hais tias Khandagiri nyob hauv Ekamra hav zoov cheeb tsam thiab ntau tus dawb huv tab tom ua kev txi nyob rau hauv Khandagiri. Ib tus dej kuj tab tom ntws zais ntshis nyob ntawd. Nyob rau hauv nqe lus tam sim no no, Mahapurush tab tom rov hais tib yam.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau dua nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ଲୀଳାମୟଙ୍କର ସତ୍ୟ ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ,
ଲୀଳା କରୁଥିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମାଧବ ସର୍ବେ ଆନନ୍ଦ ହୋଇଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Līḷā prakāśiba līḷāmayankara satya je Ekāmra bana,
līḷā karuthibe Ananta Mādhaba sarbe ānanda hoiṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Ntawm no Mahapurush rov hais dua tias tus Tswv lub chaw nyob yog Bhubaneswar. Tab sis nws tsis tau hais Bhubaneswar lub npe los yog Khandagiri. Mahapurush tau hais tib qhov chaw ntawd tias yog ‘Ekamra Van’. Mahapurush tau sau tias tus Tswv cov Leela yuav tshwm sim los ntawm Ekamra Van cheeb tsam. Nyob rau hauv tib lub Ekamra Van cheeb tsam ntawd, tus Tswv Ananta Madhava, piv txwv li, Kalki, yuav ua Leela, pom thiab hnov qhov uas cov neeg pe hawm txoj kev zoo siab yuav tsis muaj qhov kawg.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau dua nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv ‘Aagata Bhavishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳା ଧଳା ଅଶ୍ୱ ଉପରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ରହିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନରେ ଯେତେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ଋଷି,
ସକଳେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ନାମକୁ ଘୋଷି।
ସକଳେ ସେବେଳେ ଠୁଳ ଯେ ହେବେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗର,
କପିଳେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଭେଟିଣ ହୋଇବେ ସୂପକାର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷā dhaḷā ashwa upare prabhu hebe bāhāra,
Bhubaneshwarare rahibe prabhu raṅgā adhara.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sthānare jete achhanti ruṣi,
sakaḷe bāhāra hoibe prabhu nāmaku ghoṣi.
Sakaḷe sebeḷe ṭhuḷa je hebe Bindu Sāgara,
Kapileshwarare bheṭiṇa hoibe sūpakāra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tus Tswv yuav tawm mus rhuav tshem ntawm ib tug nees “Kala-Dhala” los yog dub-thiab-dawb. Ua ntej ntawd, tus Tswv yuav nyob hauv Bhubaneswar. Tag nrho cov neeg txawj ntse thiab cov hauj sam uas ua kev txi nyob rau hauv Khandagiri qhov chaw ua tiav yuav tawm los ntawm cov qhov tsua thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, tshaj tawm tus Tswv lub npe, thiab yuav sib sau ua ke ntawm Bindu Sarovar uas nyob hauv Bhubaneswar. Tom qab ntawd, cov kws ua zaub mov yuav ntsib tag nrho lawv ntawm Kapileshwar lub tuam tsev nyob ze.

Nyob rau hauv nqe lus saum toj no kuj, Khandagiri raug hu ua qhov chaw ua tiav uas yuav yog ntau tus neeg txawj ntse thiab cov hauj sam lub chaw nyob. Thiab nws kuj tau hais tias txawm yog Khandagiri los yog Bindu Sarovar los yog Kapileshwar Lub Tuam Tsev, tag nrho cov no nyob hauv Bhubaneswar thiab nyob ze ib leeg.

Ib tug ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, thaum nws tus thwjtim Baranga Das nug nws tias tus Tswv Kalki yuav yug qhov twg thiab Nws yuav nyob qhov twg, piav qhia nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ହେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ କହିବା ସେ ରଙ୍ଗ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଅବତାର ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିରଜା କ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ଜନମ ଲଭିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମିଶ୍ର ଗୃହେଣ।
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ଗୃହକୁ ଛାଡିବେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଯାଇଁ,
ସିଦ୍ଧଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ସିଦ୍ଧଙ୍କ ସଦନ ରହିବେ ସେ ଭାବଗ୍ରାହୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ସୁବର୍ଣ୍ଣଚ୍ୟୁତ ଗିରିରେ,
ସେହି ସ୍ଥାନେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରିଥିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ଜନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ଅନନ୍ତ ବାସୁଦେବ ବିପ୍ରଲିଙ୍ଗ ଭେଟ ସେ ଯହିଁ ଆଗରେ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ବିଧି ସାରିଣ ସିଦ୍ଧ କେଦାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ସାରିବେ ସମସ୍ତ ଭକତ ମିଳି,
ନିଜ କଳା ଭକ୍ତି କଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବେ ଛାଡିବେ କଳିର ମଳି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Shuṇa he bāraṅga kahibā se raṅga prabhu abatāra sthāna,
Shrī Birajā kṣetre janama labhibe Ananta Mishra gruheṇa.
Janama hoibe gruhaku chhāḍibe tapasyā karibe jāiñ,
Siddhagiri sthāna siddhaṅka sadana rahibe se bhābagrāhī.
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna Ekāmra kānana Subarṇachyuta girire,
sehi sthāne prabhu bije karithibe bhakta jana saṅgatare.
Shrī Ananta Bāsudeba bipraliṅga bheṭa se jahiñ āgare,
Bindusāgare snāna bidhi sāriṇa siddha Kedāra madhyare.
Shrī Bindu Sāgare snāna sāribe samasta bhakata miḷi,
nija kaḷā bhakti kaḷā prakāshibe chhāḍibe kaḷira maḷi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tus Tswv yuav yug hauv ib tug neeg pe hawm lub tsev hu ua Ananta Mishra nyob rau hauv Shri Viraja Kshetra (Jajpur). Tom qab yug los, tus Tswv yuav tawm Nws lub tsev thiab mus rau ib qhov chaw ntawm cov dawb huv uas tau ua tiav los ua kev txi. Mahapurush tsis tsuas yog hais tias “qhov chaw ua tiav,” nws kuj tau hais tias qhov chaw ntawd lub npe yuav yog Khandagiri, uas yuav nyob hauv Ekamra Kanan. Nyob rau hauv tib lub Khandagiri cheeb tsam ntawm Ekamra Kanan, tus Tswv yuav nyob nrog Nws cov neeg pe hawm. Mahapurush tau hais tias Shri Ananta Vasudev lub tuam tsev yuav nyob ntawd, thiab Linga, piv txwv li, Lingaraj lub tuam tsev, kuj yuav nyob ntawd. Tus Tswv yuav da dej hauv Bindu Sagar thiab mus xyuas Siddha Kedara (Kedareshwar) lub tuam tsev.

Mahapurush kuj tau hais tias tag nrho cov neeg pe hawm yuav da dej hauv tib lub Bindu Sarovar ntawd, tom qab ntawd lub teeb dawb huv yuav tshwm sim hauv cov neeg pe hawm, thiab lawv tag nrho yuav dim ntawm Kali Yuga cov qias neeg.

Los ntawm nqe lus saum toj no, Sishu Ananta Dasji tau qhia meej tias qhov chaw uas Shri Ananta Vasudev lub tuam tsev nyob, qhov chaw uas Kedareshwar lub tuam tsev nyob, qhov chaw uas Lingaraj nyob, nyob rau hauv tib lub Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Van cheeb tsam) ntawd, Khandagiri nyob. Khandagiri no yog cheeb tsam ntawm cov dawb huv uas tau ua tiav, thiab tus Tswv yuav nyob ntawm no.

Ib tug Achyutananda Dasji tus tshwm sim, Dawb Huv Hadi Das, tau sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦେଇ ଶୁଣ ସୁଜନେ କଳିଯୁଗ ବିଚାର,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ଜନମ ହେବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ଚକ୍ରଧର।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହି ଯାଉଛି ସ୍ଥାନ ଅମରାବତୀ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଗୁପତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି।
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ଲାଗିଛି ରାତ୍ର ଦିନ ମଧ୍ୟରେ,
ଗୁପତେ ଖେଳ ସେ ଖେଳିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେହି ଠାବରେ।
ଝାଡଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ସେ ଠାବ,
ଝଲମଲ ଜ୍ୟୋତି ବାହାରେ ଲୀଳା ଚହଟ ହେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Karṇa dei shuṇa sujane kaḷiyuga bichāra,
Kaḷkī rūpare janama hebe Shrī Chakradhara.
Kṣīra dhāra bahi jāuchhi sthāna Amarābatī,
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna gupate siddha sādhu achhanti.
Gumphāre bhajana lāgichhi rātra dina madhyare,
gupate kheḷa se kheḷibe prabhu sehi ṭhābare.
Jhāḍakhaṇḍagiri gumphā madhyare prabhuṅkara se ṭhāba,
jhalamala jyoti bāhāre līḷā chahaṭa heba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, tus Tswv Maha Vishnu yuav yug hauv ntiaj teb hauv Kalki kev tshwm sim. Nws cov kev ua si dawb huv yuav pib tshwm sim hauv cheeb tsam ntawm Kshira tus dej (Vaitarani), Amaravati, piv txwv li, Jajpur. Tom qab ntawd Nws cov kev ua si yuav tshwm sim hauv Khandagiri qhov chaw uas cov dawb huv uas tau ua tiav yuav koom nrog bhajan-kirtan nruab hnub hmo ntuj hauv cov qhov tsua zais ntshis. Mahapurush tau hais tias tus Tswv yuav nyob thiab ua Nws cov kev ua si hauv tib lub Khandagiri los yog Jhadakhandagiri cheeb tsam ntawd.

Dawb Huv Hadi Das tau sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଘଟଣା ପ୍ରତିମା ପୁରୁଷ ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜଙ୍କ ପୁର,
ଘୋର କଳି ମହାଭାରତ ହେବ ସେହି ଠାବର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ghaṭaṇā pratimā puruṣha Liṅgarājaṅka pura,
ghora kaḷi mahābhārata heba sehi ṭhābara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush sau tias “Qhov Chaw Tshwm Sim” (Ghatana Sthana) yuav yog Lingaraj cheeb tsam los yog Bhubaneswar, thiab qhov kev sib ntaus sib tua Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav tshwm sim nyob ntawd. Nws paub tias peb tau pom ib nqe lus ua ntej qhov chaw uas Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau – “Ghatana sthana je atai sehi, kalira bharata yuddha sethahin”. Achyutananda Dasji tau sau nqe lus no txog Khandagiri nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ thiab tau hais tias Khandagiri yog “Ghatana Sthana,” qhov chaw uas qhov kev sib ntaus sib tua Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav tshwm sim.

Tom qab ntawd, Hadi Das tau sau – Bhubaneswar los yog Lingaraj cheeb tsam yog “Ghatana Sthana” thiab Kali Mahabharata kev ua tsov rog yuav tshwm sim nyob ntawd.

Qhov no txhais tau tias qhov uas Achyutananda Das tab tom hais txog Khandagiri nyob rau hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ yog tib yam uas nws tus tshwm sim, Hadi Das, tab tom hais txog Lingaraj cheeb tsam (Bhubaneswar) nyob rau hauv ‘Kali Chautisha’. Qhov no ua pov thawj tias ob qhov chaw, piv txwv li, Khandagiri thiab Bhubaneswar, tab tom taw qhia tib qhov chaw. Hauv luv luv, Khandagiri nyob hauv Bhubaneswar.

Nco ntsoov – Txog tam sim no nyob rau hauv ntau nqe lus saum toj no, peb tau kawm tias Khandagiri hauv Bhubaneswar yog cheeb tsam ntawm cov dawb huv thiab cov neeg dawb huv. Cov dawb huv ntawd koom nrog bhajan-kirtan ntawm tus Tswv Kalki nruab hnub hmo ntuj hauv Khandagiri cov qhov tsua. Tam sim no peb yuav kawm tias cov neeg txawj ntse thiab cov hauj sam twg yuav ua kev txi rau tus Tswv Kalki hauv Khandagiri ntawd thiab muaj pes tsawg tus neeg txawj ntse tag nrho yuav ua bhajan-kirtan hauv cov qhov tsua.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv ‘Aagata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିକି ଯେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର କେତେକ ଦୂର,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ସାଧୁସନ୍ଥ ନିକର।
ନାରଦ ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଶମ୍ଭୁ ଯେ ଅଗସ୍ତି ଉପଲସ୍ତି,
ଦୁର୍ବାସା କଶ୍ୟପ ଚ୍ୟବନ କୁମ୍ଭଋଷି ସନ୍ତତି।
ପରାଶର ବଶିଷ୍ଠାଦି ହୋ ବିଶ୍ୱାମିତ୍ରଙ୍କୁ ଘେନି,
ଷାଠିଏ ସହସ୍ର ଋଷି ହୋ ଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପୁଣି।
ଦେବଋଷି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଋଷି ଯେ ସର୍ବେ ମେଳି ହୋଇଣ,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ କରନ୍ତି କଳ୍କୀ ନାମ ପୁଣ।
ଦେବତାମାନେ ଯେ ସେଠାରେ ରାତ୍ରେ ଗୋଷ୍ଠୀ କରନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀର ଆଦ୍ୟ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ନିତ୍ୟେ ଜପୁଛନ୍ତି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Khaṇḍagiriki je Bhubaneshwara keteka dūra,
Khaṇḍagiri madhye achhanti sādhusantha nikara.
Nārada Mārkaṇḍa Shambhu je Agasti Upalasti,
Durbāsā Kashyapa Chyabana Kumbharuṣi santati.
Parāshara Bashiṣṭhādi ho Bishwāmitraṅku gheni,
ṣāṭhie sahasra ruṣi ho giri madhyare puṇi.
Debaruṣi Brahmaruṣi je sarbe meḷi hoiṇa,
gumphāre bhajana karanti Kaḷkī nāma puṇa.
Debatāmāne je seṭhāre rātre goṣṭhī karanti,
Kaḷkīra ādya mantraku nitye japuchhanti.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush tau hais txog Krishna-Garuda kev sib tham nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika. Garuda hais tias Khandagiri cheeb tsam tsis deb ntawm Bhubaneswar (nws nyob ze heev). Muaj ntau tus dawb huv hauv Khandagiri. Nyob ntawd, Narada, Markandeya, Shambhu, Agastya, Upalasti, Durvasa, Kashyapa, Chyavana, “Kumbharushi Santati” txhais tau tias Dridhasyu, Parashara, Va-shish-tha, Vishwamitra, thiab lwm yam, tag nrho kaum ob tus neeg txawj ntse thiab suav nrog lawv tag nrho 60,000 tus neeg txawj ntse, Devarishis, thiab Brahmarishis koom nrog npe-bhajan ntawm tus Tswv Kalki hauv cov qhov tsua. Cov vajtswv tuav kev sib ntsib hauv cov qhov tsua ntawd thaum hmo ntuj thiab txhua hnub hu tus Tswv Kalki tus mantra.

Nyob rau hauv nqe lus no, Garudaji tsis tsuas yog qhia txog cov neeg txawj ntse uas ua kev txi hauv Khandagiri cov qhov tsua tab sis kuj tau hais tias Khandagiri qhov tsua yuav tsis deb ntawm Bhubaneswar, los yog, yuav nyob ze heev.

Los ntawm 15 nqe lus saum toj no, nws yuav tsum tau qhia meej rau cov nyeem tias raws li Bhavishya Malika, Khandagiri—qhov chaw uas Kalki yuav nyob, qhov chaw uas suav tsis txheeb cov dawb huv uas tau ua tiav yuav nyob, qhov chaw uas Kalki lub npe bhajan-kirtan yuav mus ntxiv tsis tu ncua hauv cov qhov tsua, qhov chaw uas ib tus dej yuav ntws zais ntshis, qhov chaw uas qhov kev sib ntaus sib tua Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav tshwm sim—nyob tsuas yog hauv Bhubaneswar los yog Lingaraj cheeb tsam los yog Ekamra Van cheeb tsam, thiab tsis muaj lwm qhov chaw.

10 Lub Hom Phiaj ntawm Kalki Kev Los Ua Neeg

Raws li peb txhua tus paub, tus Tswv tshwm sim kaum zaug hauv ntiaj teb no los tsim kev ncaj ncees (Dharma). Txawm yog Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, los yog Dvapara Yuga, tus Tswv txoj kev tshwm sim hauv ntiaj teb tseem yog tib yam—kev tsim Dharma.

Nyob rau hauv Shrimad Bhagavad Gita, tus Tswv Krishna tau hais rau Arjuna –

Sanskrit Sloka:

परित्राणाय साधूनां विनाशाय च दुष्कृताम् ।
धर्मसंस्थापनार्थाय संभवामि युगे युगे ।।

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Rau kev tiv thaiv cov neeg ncaj ncees (cov neeg pe hawm), rau kev rhuav tshem cov neeg phem, thiab rau kev tsim kom muaj kev ncaj ncees ruaj khov, Kuv tshwm sim ib tiam dhau ib tiam.

Nyob rau hauv Bhavishya Malika cov ntawv kuj, nws tau hais tias rau kev rhuav tshem cov neeg phem, kev tiv thaiv cov neeg pe hawm thiab cov dawb huv, thiab kev rov tsim Dharma, tus Tswv Maha Vishnu yuav nqes los rau hauv ntiaj teb hauv Nws qhov thib kaum thiab kawg, Kalki kev tshwm sim.

Qhov kev sib tham ntawm tus Tswv Krishna thiab phooj ywg Arjuna hauv Dvapara Yuga tau hais txog li 600 xyoo dhau los hauv Kali Yuga los ntawm Mahapurush Achyutananda Das nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Chausathi Patala'. Mahapurush tau sau –

Odia Sloka:

ପାପ ଭାରା ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ସହି ନପାରି,
ମୋ ଆଗରେ କଲା ବେଗେ ଗୁହାରି।
କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ମୁହିଁ ହୋଇବି,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ବିନାଶିବି।
ବସି ନରପତି ହୃଦ ଦେଶରେ
ପ୍ରଜା ପାଳିବି ଉତ୍ତମ ଭାବରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Pāpa bhārā pṛthwī sahi napāri,
mo āgare kalā bege guhāri.
Kaḷkī abatāra muhiñ hoibi,
duṣṭa prāṇīmānaṅku bināshibi.
Basi narapati hṛda deshare,
prajā pāḷibi uttama bhābare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, ntiaj teb, vim tsis muaj peev xwm ris lub nra ntawm kev txhaum thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, yuav thov rau kuv. Tom qab ntawd kuv yuav tshwm sim hauv Kalki kev tshwm sim thiab yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab cawm cov neeg pe hawm. Nyob hauv Is Nrias teb, kuv yuav tswj hwm tag nrho lub ntiaj teb thiab yuav kav cov pej xeem kom ncaj ncees thiab tsim nyog.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau nyob rau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭୟଙ୍କର ଉତ୍ପାତ ହେବ ଧରିତ୍ରୀ ବକ୍ଷରେ ଜାଣ,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାରି ଭାରା ଉଶ୍ୱାସିବେ ପୁଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mahābhayaṅkara utpāta heba dharitrī bakṣhare jāṇa,
Kaḷkī rūpare mlechha saṃhāri bhārā uśwāsibe puṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, yuav muaj kev kub ntxhov loj heev hauv ntiaj teb. Kalki nws tus kheej yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab daws ntiaj teb lub nra ntawm kev txhaum.

Hais txog Krishna-Arjuna kev sib tham, Mahapurush sau dua nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika –

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳେ ମୁହିଁ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହେବି,
ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିଣ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନମାନଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷi śeṣha beḷe muñhi Kaḷkī rūpa hebi,
santhaṅku rakhiṇa duṣhṭajanamānanku nāśhibi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Shri Krishna hais tias thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, kuv yuav tshwm sim hauv Kalki kev tshwm sim. Tiv thaiv cov dawb huv, cov neeg dawb huv, thiab cov neeg siab dawb siab huv, kuv yuav rhuav tshem tag nrho cov neeg phem thiab cov neeg txhaum.

Hais txog tus Tswv cov lus dua ib zaug, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Chausathi Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ବେଶୀ ପାପ ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପେ ମୁଁ ଉଦୟ ହେବି,
ଓଡିଶା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ମନ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଯାଇ କରିବି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Beśī pāpa jetebeḷe heba Kaḷkī rūpe mu udaya hebi,
Oḍiśā madhyare sthāne sthāne mana parīkṣhā jāi karibi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tswv Ntuj hais tias thaum Kali Yuga, thaum kev txhaum mus txog nws qhov siab tshaj plaws, ces kuv yuav sawv hauv kuv tus qauv Kalki. Kuv yuav sim cov neeg pe hawm ntawm ntau qhov chaw hauv Odisha, txiav txim siab ntawm qhov yog thiab qhov tsis yog, thiab ua raws li ntawd.

Hais txog Tswv Ntuj cov lus, ib tug ntawm Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Balaram Das, tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Kali Aagata Bhavishyanta' –

Odia Sloka:

ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଯେ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ମାତିବେ,
ଖପର କରେ ଘେନିଥିବେ।
ରୋଧିର ବୃଷ୍ଟି ଯେ ହୋଇବ,
କେ ଅବା କାହିଁ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ଯାହାକୁ ରଖିବିଟି ଟିକିଏ ମୁହିଁ,
ସେ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନେ ଥିବେ ରହି।
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଲୋକ ଯେ ନାଶ ହେବେ,
ସନ୍ଥ ଯେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ ରହିବେ।
ଅନେକ ଉତ୍ପାତ ଲାଗିବ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଚହଳ ପଡିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chaṇḍī je Chāmuṇḍā mātibe,
khapara kare ghenithibe.
Rodhira bṛṣṭi je hoiba,
ke abā kāhiñ nāsha jiba.
Jāhāku rakhibiṭi ṭikie muhiñ,
se prāṇīmāne thibe rahi.
Duṣṭa loka je nāsha hebe,
santha je abashya rahibe.
Aneka utpāta lāgiba,
shūnyare chahaḷa paḍiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tswv Ntuj hais tias thaum tsim tsa Dharma thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, Niam Ashta Chandis thiab lwm tus Yoginis yuav mus ua kev rhuav tshem loj heev. Yuav muaj los nag ntshav los saum ntuj. Yuav muaj ntau yam kev kub ntxhov thiab kev puas tsuaj yuav los ntawm qhov chaw khoob, piv txwv li, los ntawm qhov chaw sab nraud. Tsuas yog cov uas kuv tiv thaiv thiaj yuav nyob nyab xeeb. Cov neeg phem yuav raug rhuav tshem, thiab cov neeg dawb huv thiab cov neeg ncaj ncees yuav raug cawm dim.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Kali Kalpa Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣhatrīmānanku nikṣhatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, Tswv Ntuj yuav hloov mus rau Nws tus qauv Kalki thiab mus ncig thoob ntiaj teb thiab yuav cawm Niam Ntiaj Teb los ntawm kev rhuav tshem cov neeg phem.

Hauv 'Kali Kalpa Gita' nws tus kheej, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau dua ib zaug ntxiv –

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନିଧନ ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ସଂହାରଣ,
କରିବେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଦେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ମହୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
କଳିକଳ୍ପ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mlechha nidhana duṣhṭa saṃharaṇa,
karibe niśhche deba Kaḷkī Rāma.
Emanta mahī je heba uśwāsa,
Kaḷikalpa kahe Achyuta dāsa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Tswv Kalki yuav cawm Ntiaj Teb los ntawm kev rhuav tshem cov Mlechhas (cov neeg qus/cov neeg phem) thiab rhuav tshem cov neeg phem.

10.1 Kev Los ntawm Tswv Kalki

Ib tug ntawm Panchasakhas, Tus Ntsuj Plig Loj Sishu Ananta Das, tau sau txog qhov no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika 'Chumbaka Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଆଶ୍ୱାସି ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷkī abatāra Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśhi bhaktaṅku āśwāsi dharmaku tumbhe pāliba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Tus Tswv Loj Kalki yuav yug los hauv Odisha. Tom qab ntawd, Tswv Ntuj yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab tiv thaiv cov neeg pe hawm kom rov tsim tsa Dharma.

11 Cov Kev Ua Kom Puas Tsuaj: Muaj Pib thiab Xaus Tseeb?

Raws li peb paub los ntawm cov lus hais hauv Bhabishya Malika, nyob rau lub sij hawm hloov pauv no ntawm Kali Yuga thiab Satya Yuga, yuav muaj cov xwm txheej rhuav tshem uas tsis tau muaj dua li thoob ntiaj teb raws li kev coj ntawm Tswv Kalki, los ntawm qhov uas tag nrho cov Mlecchas (cov neeg qus), cov neeg txhaum, cov neeg phem, thiab cov neeg tsis ncaj ncees yuav raug rhuav tshem. Bhabishya Malika kuj lees paub tias tag nrho cov neeg pe hawm yuav nyob nyab xeeb thaum lub sij hawm cov xwm txheej no thiab lawv tsis tas yuav ntshai.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chhayalisha Patala’, hais txog Tus Tswv Loj cov lus uas Tswv Ntuj qhia cov lus qhia txog kev rhuav tshem ib nrab (Khanda Pralaya) uas yuav los txog.

Tus Mahapurush sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

କାଳଭାବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟରେ,
ସୃଷ୍ଟି ନାଶ କରି ଲୀଳା କରେ ପାପ ଭାରେ।
ମାତ୍ର ମହାପ୍ରଳୟକୁ ରହିଥିବୁ ଟାକି,
ସେ କାଳରେ ଚରାଚର ନ ରହିବେ ବାକି।
ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ସହିତ ସଂହାରିଣୀ ଶକ୍ତି,
ଖଣ୍ଡା ଖପର ହାତରେ ବୁଲି ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି।
ରୋଗ ଶୋକ ଦୁଃଖ ଜାଲ ସଂସାରେ ପାତିବେ,
ପର ଆପଣାର ଭେଦ କିଛି ନ ରଖିବେ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ପବନ ଯେ ରୁଦ୍ର ମୂର୍ତ୍ତି ଧରି,
ଉପାଡ଼ିବ ସୁମେରୁ ସହିତ ଯେତେ ଗିରି।
ଚାରିମେଘ ଚାରିଦିଗେ ଆଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ କରିବେ,
ମହାଘୋର ବିପତ୍ତିକୁ ଆଣି ଘଟାଇବେ।
ଅନ୍ଧାର ହୋଇବ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ବଢ଼ିବ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ନକ୍ଷତ୍ର ଉଲକା ଛିଣ୍ଡିବ ନାହିଁ ଆଦି ଅନ୍ତ।
ଭୈରବୀ ରଡ଼ିବେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ କାନ୍ଦିବ ସକଳ,
ପାପମାତ୍ରା ପ୍ରବଳରେ ହେବ ଏହି ଫଳ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kālabhābe mahāshakti khaṇḍa praḷayare,
Sṛṣṭi nāsha kari līḷā kare pāpa bhāre.
Mātra mahāpraḷayaku rahithibu ṭāki,
Se kāḷare charāchara na rahibe bāki.
Chaṇḍī Chāmuṇḍā sahita saṃhāriṇī shakti,
Khaṇḍā khapara hātare buli dibārātri.
Roga shoka duḥkha jāla saṃsāre pātibe,
Para āpaṇāra bheda kichhi na rakhibe.
Aṇachāsha pabana je rudra mūrtti dhari,
Upāḍiba Sumeru sahita jete giri.
Chārimegha chāridige āchhanna karibe,
Mahāghora bipattiku āṇi ghaṭāibe.
Andhāra hoiba pṛthwī baḍhiba utpāta,
Nakṣatra ulakā chhiṇḍiba nāhiñ ādi anta.
Bhairabī raḍibe pṛthwī kāndiba sakaḷa,
Pāpamātrā prabaḷare heba ehi phaḷa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Lub Hwj Chim Loj Tshaj Plaws hauv daim ntawv ntawm Lub Sij Hawm (Kala) yuav ua rau muaj kev rhuav tshem ib nrab thiab rhuav tshem lub ntiaj teb. Thaum lub sij hawm rhuav tshem, txhua yam muaj txoj sia yuav raug cuam tshuam. Chandi-Chamunda thiab cov rog rhuav tshem yuav mus ncig nruab hnub nruab hmo tuav ntaj (Khanda) thiab pob txha taub hau (Khapar). Kev mob, kev txom nyem, kab mob, thiab kev tu siab yuav kis thoob plaws ntiaj teb. Txhua qhov sib txawv ntawm 'tus kheej' thiab 'cov neeg txawv' yuav ploj mus. Plaub caug cuaj cua, hloov mus rau daim ntawv phem ntawm Rudra, yuav rhuav tshem txhua lub roob suav nrog Sumeru loj. Plaub huab, hu ua Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, thiab Drona, yuav puag ncig plaub sab thiab tsim kev puas tsuaj loj heev. Kev tsaus ntuj nti yuav npog Ntiaj Teb; lub hnub thiab lub hli yuav tsis sawv. Thaum ntawd, cov hnub qub yuav poob los saum ntuj thiab ua rau muaj kev puas tsuaj. Hauv qhov tsaus ntuj ntawd, Niam Bhairavi yuav qw nrov nrov thiab rhuav tshem ntau tus neeg txhaum.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, hais txog Tswv Ntuj cov lus, sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

କାହିଁ ଜଳ ରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ଜଗତ ନାଶିବି,
କାହିଁ ପବନ ରୂପରେ ସକଳ ଗ୍ରାସିବି।
କାହିଁ ଯୋଗମାୟା ନେଇ ସଂହାର କରିବି,
କାହିଁ ନିଜେ ବିଶ୍ଵରୂପ ଧାରଣ କରିବି।
କାହିଁ ମଉନ ବ୍ରତ ମୁଁ ଧାରଣ କରିବି,
କାହିଁ କାଳରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ପାପୀଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kāhiñ jala rūpe muhiñ jagata nāshibi,
kāhiñ pabana rūpare sakaḷa grāsibi.
kāhiñ yogamāyā nei saṃhāra karibi,
kāhiñ nije bishwarūpa dhāraṇa karibi.
kāhiñ mauna brata muñ dhāraṇa karibi,
kāhiñ kāḷarūpe muhiñ pāpīṅku nāshibi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Tswv Kalki yuav ua rau muaj kev rhuav tshem loj heev hauv ntiaj teb no hauv Nws ntau yam qauv. Hauv qee qhov chaw, Nws yuav hloov mus rau daim ntawv dej thiab raus txhua yam; hauv lwm qhov, Nws yuav tshuab txhua yam mus rau hauv kev rhuav tshem cua. Hauv qee zaum, Nws yuav coj kev rhuav tshem thaum ua raws li kev cog lus ntsiag to, thiab hauv lwm qhov, Nws yuav tua cov neeg phem hauv daim ntawv ntawm Lub Sij Hawm. Hauv qee qhov chaw, Nws yuav hloov mus rau Nws Daim Ntawv Thoob Ntiaj Teb, thiab hauv lwm qhov, Nws yuav coj kev puas tsuaj los ntawm nws lub zog Yogamaya.

Raws li Bhabishya Malika, Tswv Kalki yuav coj kev rhuav tshem no los ntawm dej nyab loj heev, av qeeg loj heev, cua daj cua dub rhuav tshem, hluav taws nyab, kev puas tsuaj hauv qhov chaw sab nraud, kab mob sib kis txaus ntshai, thiab kev ua tsov rog thoob ntiaj teb nuclear.

11.1 Av qeeg

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ହିମାଳୟ ଏଭରେଷ୍ଟ ଟଳି ଯେ ପଡିବ,
ଯେବେ ଏହି ଧରା ପରେ ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Himālaya Everest ṭaḷi je paḍiba,
Jebe ehi dharā pare bhūmikampa heba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kev ua phem rhuav tshem ntawm Kalki thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, yuav muaj av qeeg muaj zog heev yuav ntaus Ntiaj Teb kom txog thaum 'Himalayas' thiab nws cov ncov roob xws li 'Everest' yuav tawg. Cov neeg pe hawm yuav nyob nyab xeeb, thaum cov neeg txhaum yuav raug rhuav tshem.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau dua hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପେ ପ୍ରକମ୍ପିତ ହୋଇବ ଧରଣୀ,
ମାଟି ତଳେ ଲୁଚିଯିବ କୋଠାବାଡି ପୁଣି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhūmikampe prakampita hoiba dharaṇī,
māṭi taḷe luchijiba koṭhābāḍi puṇi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tag nrho Ntiaj Teb yuav tshee hnyo hauv av qeeg, thiab vim yog cov av qeeg ntawd, cov tsev loj yuav raug faus rau hauv av.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika ntawm nws:

Odia Sloka:

ପାତାଳେ ବାସୁକୀ ଟେକିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ,
ତିନିଥର ଯେ କମ୍ପିବ ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Pātāḷe Bāsukī ṭekiba muṇḍa,
tini thara je kampiba brahmāṇḍa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, vim yog lub nra hnyav dhau ntawm kev ua txhaum thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, tus nab Vasuki, uas tuav Ntiaj Teb rau ntawm nws lub taub hau, yuav co nws lub taub hau rov qab. Vim yog qhov no, peb lub av qeeg loj heev yuav tshwm sim rau Ntiaj Teb no.

Ib tug neeg dawb huv loj yug hauv Odisha thaum xyoo pua 20, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ ଅପାର,
ନାଶ ହୋଇବେ ନାରୀ ନର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhūmikampa heba apāra,
nāśha hoibe nārī nara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, hauv theem kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, yuav muaj av qeeg suav tsis txheeb uas ntau tus txiv neej thiab poj niam yuav tuag.

11.2 Dej Nyab (Jala Pralaya)

Thaum tham txog kev txaus ntshai ntawm dej nyab, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତକୁ ଛାଡି ଦେଲେ ସବୁ ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଯିବ, ମହାସାଗର ଜଳରେ ଡୁବି ମରୁଥିବ।
ଧରଣୀ ଦେହରେ ଜଳ ମାରିବ ଲହରୀ, ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇଯିବ ଚଉଦିଗ ଭାରି।
ଅଛଇ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ଉତ୍ତର ମେରୁରେ, ତରଳିବ ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କ ରଶ୍ମିରେ।
ରୌଦ୍ରତାପେ ଯେତେବେଳେ ମେଦିନୀ କମ୍ପିବ, ସେତିକିବେଳେ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ତରଳିବ।
ବହିବ ପାଣିର ସୁଅ ସମୁଦ୍ରକୁ ପୁଣ, ଆଟଲାଣ୍ଟିକ ବଢ଼ିବ କାହିଁ କେତେ ଜାଣ।
ସମୁଦ୍ରର ପାଣି ଧୀରେ ବଢ଼ି ବଢ଼ି ଯିବ, ସାଗର ମାତିବ ଶେଷେ ଆଉ ଯାହା ଥିବ।
ଅନାଉ ଅନାଉ କାହିଁ ଜଳ ମାଡିଯିବ, ମାଟିର ଲକ୍ଷେ ଘନ ଫୁଟ ଉପରେ ରହିବ।
ଭୂଗୋଳର ସ୍ଥିତି ବଡ଼ ବିଚିତ୍ର ତୁ ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ରହିଛି ସର୍ବ ଉର୍ଦ୍ଧ୍ଵରେ ତ ପୁଣ।
ସୃଷ୍ଟିର କର୍ତ୍ତା ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତପତି ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ଭୂମିରେ କରିବେ ବୈକୁଣ୍ଠ ସ୍ଥାପନ।
ପାଣିର ଘେରରେ ଆଉ ସବୁ ମହାଦେଶ, ଧୀରେ ଜାଣ ବୁଡିଯିବ ସେ ମହାବିନାଶ।
ଦେଖିବ ଏ ଦୁଇ ଆଖି ସମୟ ଆସିଛି, ସାମନା କରି ପାରିଲେ ସମୟକୁ ଇଛି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bhāratku chāḍidele sabu rāṣṭra jiba, Mahāsāgara jalare ḍubi maruthiba.
Dharaṇī dehare jala māriba laharī, Jalārṇaba hoijiba chaudiga bhāri.
Achai baraphagaḍa uttara merure, taraḷiba dhīre dhīre sūryāṅka raśmire.
Raudratāpe jetebeḷe medinī kampiba, setikibeḷe baraphagaḍa taraḷiba.
Bahiba pāṇira sua samudruku puṇa, Āṭlāṇṭika baḍhiba kāhiñ kete jāṇa.
Samudrara pāṇi dhīre baḍhi baḍhi jiba, sāgara mātiba śeṣe āu jāhā thiba.
Anāu anāu kāhiñ jala māḍijiba, māṭira lakṣhe ghana phuṭa upare rahiba.
Bhūgoḷara sthiti baḍa bichitra tu jāṇa, Bhārata rahichi sarba urdhware ta puṇa.
Sruṣṭira kartā prabhu jagatpati jāṇa, Bhārata bhūmire karibe Baikuṇṭha sthāpana.
Pāṇira gherare āu sabu mahādeśa, dhīre jāṇa buḍijiba se mahābināśa.
Dekhiba e dui ākhi samaya āsichi, sāmnā karipārile samayaku ichhi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tsuas yog Is Nrias teb xwb, yuav luag txhua lub teb chaws yuav raug raus rau hauv dej hiav txwv. Yuav muaj dej nyab rau Ntiaj Teb, thiab tag nrho plaub sab yuav raug raus. Vim yog lub hnub kub zuj zus, cov dej khov ntawm North Pole yuav pib yaj, uas yuav cuam tshuam Atlantic Ocean tshaj plaws. Dej hauv hiav txwv yuav nce zuj zus, ua rau cov nroog ntug hiav txwv raug raus. Hauv qee qhov chaw, nthwv dej tsunami siab li roob yuav nce. Tag nrho thaj chaw yuav hloov. Tab sis Tus Tswv Loj yuav tiv thaiv Is Nrias teb, thiab qhov cuam tshuam ntawm dej nyab no yuav tsawg dua hauv Is Nrias teb. Tsuas yog Is Nrias teb xwb, tag nrho lwm lub teb chaws yuav raug dej nyab.

11.3 Cua Nyab (Pavan Pralaya)

Ib tug ntawm Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Patta Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମହୀନ ହେବେ ସକଳ ଏ ଜନ ଧର୍ମାଧର୍ମ ନ ରହିବ,
ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ବହିବ ଏମନ୍ତ ପୃଥିବୀ ଟଳଟଳ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dharmahīna hebe sakaḷa e jana dharmādharma na rahiba,
aṇachāśha bāyu bahiba emanta pruthibī ṭaḷaṭaḷa dhruba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, txhua tus yuav ua tsis ncaj ncees; tsis muaj leej twg yuav muaj kev ntseeg/kev ua hauj lwm ntxiv lawm. Qhov no yog vim li cas Tus Tswv Loj Kalki yuav tsim tsa Dharma. Plaub caug cuaj cua yuav tshuab hauv txoj kev uas Ntiaj Teb yuav tshee hnyo.

Tus Mahapurush sau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika ntawm nws:

Odia Sloka:

ପବନ ବହି ଅଣଚାଶ,
କ୍ଷିତିକି କରିଦେବ ଧ୍ଵଂସ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Pabana bahi aṇachāśha,
kṣhitiki karideba dhwaṃsa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kev ua rhuav tshem ntawm Tswv Kalki, plaub caug cuaj cua yuav tshuab thiab coj kev rhuav tshem rau Ntiaj Teb.

Tus neeg dawb huv dig muag yug hauv Odisha hauv xyoo pua 19, Bhima Bhoi, kuj tau sau txog qhov no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika. Tus Mahapurush sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପବନ ବହିବ ପର୍ବତ ଦୋହଲିଯିବ,
ଚାରିମେଘ ମିଳି ବର୍ଷିବେ ଚାରିଦିଗ ଅନ୍ଧାର ଘୋଟିବ।
ମୋ ଭାଇ, ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦେଖାଯିବ ନାହିଁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Aṇachāśha khaṇḍa pabana bahiba parbata dohalijiba,
chārimegha miḷi barṣhibe chāridiga andhāra ghoṭiba.
Mo bhāi, chandra sūrya dekhājiba nāhiñ.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, lub sij hawm yuav los txog thaum plaub caug cuaj cua yuav tshuab thiab txawm tias roob thiab toj siab yuav tshee hnyo. Plaub huab hu ua Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, thiab Drona yuav los nag ua ke. Lub hnub thiab lub hli yuav tsis pom saum ntuj, thiab kev tsaus ntuj nti yuav npog tag nrho plaub sab.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau dua:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳଟଳ ହେଉଥିବ ସପ୍ତଦ୍ଵୀପ ମହୀ,
ସେ ବେଳେ ଥଳକୂଳ ରହିବଟି ନାହିଁ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ହୋଇ ପବନ ବହିବ,
ସରକାର ବୁଦ୍ଧିବିଦ୍ୟା ଉଭେଇ ଯିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ṭaḷaṭaḷa heuthibā saptadwīpa mahī,
se beḷe thaḷakūḷa rahibāṭi nāhiñ.
aṇachāśha khaṇଡa hoi pabana bahiba,
sarakāra buddhibidyā ubhei jiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum tsim tsa Dharma thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, yuav muaj kev kub ntxhov thoob plaws Ntiaj Teb suav nrog xya lub teb chaws loj. Tshuab nrog kev npau taws heev, plaub caug cuaj cua yuav rhuav tshem txhua yam qauv. Thaum ntawd, tsis muaj leej twg yuav nyob nyab xeeb. Tsoom fwv lub hwj chim thiab nws txoj kev txawj ntse yuav tsis muaj txiaj ntsig. Tsuas yog cov uas ua raws li txoj kev Dharma thiab muab tag nrho rau Tus Tswv Loj thiaj yuav tau txais kev tiv thaiv.

Odia Sloka:

କର ପତ୍ର ଯୋଡି କହଇ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଗୁରୁପାଦେ ଶିର ଦେଇ,
ପ୍ରଳୟ କାଳର ବାରଣ କହିବା କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ ଯହିଁ।
ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଏମନ୍ତ କାଳ ଯେ ଦିନେ ଆସି ପହଞ୍ଚିବ,
ଦଶ ଗ୍ରାମ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଜଣେକ ରହିବେ ଦେବତାଙ୍କ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ।
ସେ କାଳେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ଭାଦ୍ରବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣିମା ଦିନ,
ଏମନ୍ତ ପବନ ବହିବ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ସକଳ ହୋଇବ ଲୀନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kara patra joḍi kahai Bāraṅga gurupāde śira dei,
praḷaya kāḷara bāraṇa kahibā kaḷi śeṣha heba jahiñ.
Śuṇare Bāraṅga emanta kāḷa je dine āsi pahañchiba,
daśha grāma madhye jaṇeka rahibe debatāṅka ābirbhāba.
Se kāḷe Bāraṅga aṇachāśha bāyu Bhādraba pūrṇimā dina,
emanta pabana bahiba Bāraṅga sakaḷa hoiba līna.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Thaum tus thwj tim Baranga nug txog kev ua rhuav tshem ntawm Kalki uas tshwm sim thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika tias tib neeg yuav tau pom ib hnub thaum tsuas yog ib tug neeg xwb, zoo li ib tug vaj tswv, yuav muaj txoj sia nyob ntawm 10 lub zos. Thaum ntawd, plaub caug cuaj cua yuav tshuab nrog kev ceev heev thiab rhuav tshem txhua yam qauv. Qhov ntawd yuav yog hnub hli puv ntawm lub hlis Bhadra.

11.4 Dej Nyab Hluav Taws (Agni Pralaya)

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua 'Gupta Kheda Malika' tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଧନ, ସମ୍ପତ୍ତି ଓ ଟଙ୍କା ଯାହା ଅଛି ଜମା,
ସବୁଯିବ ପୋଡି ଭାଇ ନିଆଁର ଗଞ୍ଜମା।
ଚଉଦିଗେ ନିଆଁ କାହୁଁ ଦଗ୍ଧି ଏ ଧରଣୀ,
ଓଦା କଞ୍ଚା ନ ମାନିବ ସବୁ ଖାଏ ଅଗ୍ନି।
ଅଗ୍ନିର କରାଳ ରୂପ ଜୀବାତ୍ମା ଦେଖିବ,
ହାଁ ହାଁ ହୋଇ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଛାଡିଯିବ।
ସମଗ୍ର ବିଶ୍ଵରେ ଧ୍ଵଂସ ଲୀଳୋ ଯେ ଚାଲିବ,
କେଉଁଠି କମ୍ ବେଶୀ ଜାଣେ ତା ଦଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dhana, sampatti o ṭaṅkā jāhā achi jamā,
sabujiba poḍi bhāi niāñra gañjamā.
Chaudige niāñ kāhuñ dagdhi e dharaṇī,
odā kañchā na māniba sabu khāe agni.
Agnira karāḷarūpa jībātmā dekhiba,
hāñ hāñ hoi tāra prāṇa chāḍijiba.
Samagra biśware dhwaṃsa līḷā je chāliba,
keuṇṭhi kam beśī jāne tā daiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, yuav pom kev seev cev hluav taws nyob rau txhua plaub sab. Lub ntiaj teb yuav kub hnyiab. Coob tus neeg yuav tuag. Cov khoom muaj nqis, vaj tse, thiab nyiaj txiag yuav kub hnyiab ua tshauv. Cov kev ua puas tsuaj no yuav tshwm sim thoob ntiaj teb, qee qhov chaw yuav tshwm sim tsawg dua thiab qee qhov chaw yuav tshwm sim ntau dua.

11.5 Kev Puas Tsuaj Hauv Chaw Ntuj/Huab Cua

Odia Sloka:

ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ଯାକ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

nānā utpāta jāka antarikhye heba,
bhakta binā anya kehi jāni na pāriba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua 'Chakada Madana' tias nyob rau lub sijhawm kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, thaum kev tsim tsa Dharma tab tom ua, yuav muaj ntau yam kev cuam tshuam hauv chaw ntuj. Yuav muaj ntau yam kev hloov pauv hauv cov hnub qub thiab cov hnub qub, nrog rau lawv txoj haujlwm thiab ceev. Tus Mahapurush tau sau tias tsuas yog cov neeg pe hawm Vajtswv xwb thiaj yuav paub txog cov no, cov neeg dog dig yuav tsis paub txog.

Tus Mahapurush tau rov sau txog qhov no nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷବେଳେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ରଣ ଗୋଳ ସେ କାଳେ ହୋଇବ ବହୁତ।
ଚାରିଦିଗମାନଙ୍କରେ ଲାଞ୍ଜାମାନ ପଡି,
ସେ କାଳେ ବହୁତ ଶବ୍ଦ ହେବ ଘଡ଼ଘଡ଼ି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷiyuga śheṣhabeḷe pruthbī utpāta,
raṇa goḷa se kāḷe hoiba bahuta.
chāridigamānaṅkare lāñjāmāna paḍi,
se kāḷe bahuta śhabda heba ghaḍaghaḍi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, nyob rau theem kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, yuav muaj ntau yam kev cuam tshuam thiab kev ua tsov rog hnyav heev nyob rau hauv ntiaj teb. Yuav muaj cov pob zeb ntuj/hnub qub poob rau txhua plaub sab thiab yuav hnov suab los ntawm qhov chaw khoob.

11.6 Kab Mob thiab Kev Sib Kis Loj

Cov Mahapurush tau sau nyob rau hauv ntau phau ntawv Malika tias thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, ntau yam kab mob thiab kev sib kis loj yuav ua rau tib neeg lub neej poob qis.
