Phau Malika tau hais meej tias cov kws kho mob yuav ua tsis tau zoo hauv kev kuaj mob thiab kho cov kab mob thiab kev sib kis loj ntawd, thiab cov tshuaj uas lawv muab yuav tsis muaj txiaj ntsig.

Cov Mahapurush kuj tau hais tias qhov tseeb, cov kab mob thiab kev sib kis loj no yuav raug coj los ntawm cov niam-hwj chim xws li Yogamaya, Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, Dakinis, Pisachinis, thiab lwm yam. Cov niam-hwj chim no yuav mus ncig thoob ntiaj teb hauv daim ntawv zais cia ua cov kab mob no thiab nkag mus rau hauv tib neeg lub cev.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Bigyanakalpa’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷ ପଡିବ ଲୋକେ ଯିବେ ବହୁ ମରି,
ଚଉଷଠି ରୋଗ ଭ୍ରମୁଥିବେ ଫେରି ଫେରି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Durbhikṣha paḍiba loke jibe bahu mari,
chauṣhaṭhi roga bhramuthibe pheri pheri.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum lub sijhawm tsim tsa Dharma thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, yuav muaj kev tshaib plab thiab kev qhuav dej, ua rau coob tus neeg tuag. Rau caum plaub hom kab mob yuav kis thoob ntiaj teb.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das rov sau dua nyob rau hauv phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ବାଡି ବେମାରୀରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Bāḍi bemārīre aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, ntau yam kab mob yuav ua rau coob tus neeg tuag.

Tus Mahapurush tau rov sau dua nyob rau hauv nws phau ‘Jaiphula Malika’ thiab ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚିହ୍ନା ରୋଗ ଘୋଟିବ,
ବଇଦର ବୁଦ୍ଧି ବଣା ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୌଷଧି କାମ ନ କରିବ।
ଅଚିହ୍ନା ବ୍ୟାଧି ଯେ ଜଗତ୍ରେ ଘୋଟିବ ବଇଦ ବଣା ହୋଇବେ,
ରୋଗ ନ ଚିହ୍ନିଣ ବଣା ହେଉଥିବେ ଅନେକ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ମରିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Achinhā roga ghoṭiba,
baidara buddhi baṇā hoiba lo jāiphula,
mahauṣadhi kāma na kariba.
Achinhā byādhi je jagatre ghoṭiba baida baṇā hoibe,
roga na chinhiṇa baṇā heuthibe aneka prāṇī maribe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, yuav muaj cov kab mob tsis paub txog tuaj uas cov kws kho mob txoj kev txawj ntse yuav tsis ua haujlwm. Cov kab mob no yuav tsis raug txheeb xyuas, thiab cov tshuaj yuav tsis muaj txiaj ntsig hauv kev kho mob, vim li ntawd coob tus neeg yuav tuag.

Tus Mahapurush sau nyob rau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika tias:

Odia Sloka:

ରୋଗ ରୂପେ ଯୋଗମାୟା ଶରୀରେ ଖେଳିବ,
କାହାର ଆୟତ ପୁଣି ସେଠାରେ ନ ଥିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Roga rūpe Yogamāyā sharīre kheḷiba,
kāhāra āyata puṇi seṭhāre na thiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, Niam Yogamaya yuav nkag mus rau hauv tib neeg lub cev hauv daim ntawv kab mob.

Tus Mahapurush sau ntxiv tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଦ ମହୌଷଧି କରିବନି କାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ମାଡି ଆସିବଟି ରୋଗ,
ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣିବୁ ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀକୁ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଯୋଗ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Gada mahauṣhadhi karibani kārjya māḍi āsibaṭi roga,
Se kāḷe jānibu alpa dine pruthbīku praḷaya yoga.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum muaj cov kab mob sib kis loj tuaj uas cov tshuaj kho mob tsis muaj txiaj ntsig hauv kev kho mob, ces yuav tsum nkag siab tias lub sijhawm kev puas tsuaj hauv ntiaj teb tab tom los txog.

Tus Mahapurush rov sau dua tias:

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ୟାଧି ହୋଇ ପିଶାଚୀ ଆସିବେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ନାଶିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Byādhi hoi piśhāchī āsibe,
prāṇīmānanku gupte nāśhibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov niam-hwj chim yuav zais cia nkag mus rau hauv lub cev hauv daim ntawv kab mob thiab rhuav tshem coob tus neeg. Tab sis nrog rau qhov no, tus Mahapurush kuj tau hais nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika txog txoj kev rau tib neeg lub neej kom dim ntawm cov kab mob thiab kev sib kis loj no.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Chausathi Patala’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମାରେ ଯେଉଁ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ରହିବେ,
ରୋଗ କବଳରେ କେବେହିଁ ସେମାନେ ନ ପଡିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Satya śhānti dayā kṣhamāre jeuñ prāṇī rahibe,
roga kabaḷare kebehiñ semāne na paḍibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov neeg uas coj cov txiaj ntsig zoo xws li qhov tseeb, kev thaj yeeb, kev hlub, thiab kev zam txim hauv lawv tus cwj pwm yuav dim ntawm cov kab mob no.

11.7 Kev Ua Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Jaiphula Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗିବ ମହାସମର,
ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅତି ପ୍ରବଳ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ନାରଖାର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Lāgiba mahāsamara,
deśha deśha madhye ati prabala lo jāiphula,
pruthibī hoijiba nārakhāra.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, yuav muaj kev sib ntaus sib tua loj heev nyob rau hauv ntiaj teb. Yuav muaj kev ua tsov rog hnyav heev ntawm cov teb chaws, ua rau ntiaj teb puas tsuaj.

Achyutananda Das ji tau rov sau dua nyob rau hauv ‘Jaiphula Malika’ nws tus kheej tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ପୁଣ,
ଭୟଙ୍କର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏହା କଳି ଶେଷ ବୋଲି ଜାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Deśha deśha madhye puṇa,
bhayaṅkara juddha heba pramāṇa lo jāiphula,
ehā kaḷi śeṣha boli jāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, nyob rau theem kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, yuav muaj kev ua tsov rog hnyav heev tawg tawm ntawm ntau lub teb chaws. Hauv lwm lo lus, thaum muaj kev ua tsov rog txaus ntshai pib ntawm cov teb chaws, yuav tsum nkag siab tias yog qhov kawg ntawm Kali Yuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣୁ ପରମାଣୁ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରି ଭୟଙ୍କର,
ଗୋଟିଏ ଅଣୁ ବୋମାର କରାମତି ସାର।
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଅଣୁବୋମା ଗର୍ଜିବ ମହୀରେ,
କାନ୍ଦିବ ଧରଣୀ ରାଣୀ ହା ହୁତାଶରେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Aṇu paramāṇu juddha bhāri bhayaṅkara,
goṭie aṇu bomāra karāmati sāra.
lakṣha lakṣha aṇubomā garjiba mahīre,
kāndiba dharaṇī rāṇī hā hutāśhare.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, yuav muaj kev ua tsov rog atomic-nuclear tshwm sim hauv ntiaj teb uas yuav txaus ntshai heev. Tag nrho lub ntiaj teb yuav tshee hnyo nrog kev siv ntau lab lub foob pob nuclear. Niam Ntiaj Teb yuav quaj.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das qhia tias nyob rau lub sijhawm tam sim no, yuav muaj kev ua tsov rog hnyav heev tshwm sim ntawm Is Nrias teb thiab Pakistan. Raws li tus Mahapurush, kev ua tsov rog no ntawm Is Nrias teb thiab Pakistan yuav hloov mus ua kev ua tsov rog thoob ntiaj teb vim tias ntau lub teb chaws yuav txhawb Pakistan hauv kev ua tsov rog no, thaum qee lub teb chaws yuav tawm tsam rau Is Nrias teb. Hauv kev ua tsov rog no, cov teb chaws loj hauv ntiaj teb yuav raug faib ua ob pawg.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau nyob rau hauv nws phau ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ଭାରତ ସପକ୍ଷ ହେବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଜାଣ,
ଇରାକ ଯେ ସମର୍ଥନ ଦେବ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chīna Pākisthāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa,
Ruṣhiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmāna Jāpāna.
Bhārata sapakṣha hebe mahāśhakti jāṇa,
Irāka je samarthana deba Pākisthāna.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, hauv kev ua tsov rog no, cov teb chaws xws li Tuam Tshoj, Amelikas, thiab Iraq yuav txhawb Pakistan, thaum cov hwj chim xws li Russia, Lub Teb Chaws Yelemees, thiab Nyij Pooj yuav tawm tsam rau Is Nrias teb.

Tus Mahapurush sau nyob rau hauv phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Bhabishyat Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ,
ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପୁଣି ଚୀନ୍ ସଇନୁ ତୁର୍କୀ ଇରାନ ଜାଣ,
ବ୍ରିଟିଶ ଯେ ସଇନ୍ୟ ସବେ ମିଳିଣ।
ପାକସ୍ଥିାନକୁ ବଳ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ସର୍ବେ ଆବର,
ପାକିସ୍ଥାନକୁ ଦେବେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଲାଗିବ ଘୋର।
ଏହା ଦେଖି ରୁଷିଆ ଆସି ହୋଇବ ଠିଆ,
ଭାରତ ସମରକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ସେ ଏକୁଟିଆ।
ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ଶସ୍ତ୍ର ସଇନ୍ୟ ଯୋଗାଇ ଦେବ ଜାଣ,
ଭାରତ ସହିତ ମିଶି କରିବ ରଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākisthānṭi raṅge,
mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Puṇi Chīna sainu Turkī Irāna jāṇa,
Briṭisha je sainya sabe miḷiṇa.
Pākisthānku baḷa astra sarbe ābara,
Pākisthānku debe juddha lāgiba ghora.
Ehā dekhi Ruṣiā āsi hoiba ṭhiā,
Bhārata samarakṣetre se ekuṭiā.
Astra shastra sainya jogāi deba jāṇa,
Bhārata sahita mishi kariba raṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus Mahapurush tau sau tias ib lub teb chaws hu ua Pakistan yuav koom nrog kev ua tsov rog hnyav heev nrog Is Nrias teb. Hauv kev ua tsov rog no, cov teb chaws xws li Tuam Tshoj, Qaib Ntxhw, Iran, Askiv, thiab Amelikas yuav pab Pakistan thiab muab kev txhawb nqa tub rog. Pom tag nrho cov no, Is Nrias teb tus phooj ywg zoo tshaj plaws, Russia, yuav tuaj ua ntej thiab ib leeg los pab Is Nrias teb. Nws yuav muab Is Nrias teb txhua yam riam phom, kev koom tes nyiaj txiag, thiab kev txhawb nqa tub rog thiab yuav tawm tsam kev ua tsov rog hnyav heev tawm tsam cov teb chaws yeeb ncuab rau Is Nrias teb.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau tias tsis yog Russia xwb, tab sis Nyij Pooj thiab Lub Teb Chaws Yelemees kuj yuav tawm tsam rau Is Nrias teb hauv kev ua tsov rog no. Hauv ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ nws tus kheej, nws sau ntxiv tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଜାପାନର ରାଗ ଅଛି ହିରୋଶିମା କଥା,
ପକାଇଥିଲା ବୋମା ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଜାଣିଥା।
ସେହି କ୍ରୋଧ ଲିଭିନାହିଁ ଜାପାନର ଜାଣ,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଭାରତ ପଟ ନେବେ ସେହି ପୁଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Jāpānara rāga achi Hirośhimā kathā,
pakāithilā bomā juddhe jāṇithā.
Sehi krodha libhināhiñ Jāpānara jāṇa,
juddhe Bhārata paṭa nebe sehi puṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Nyij Pooj yuav tsis hnov qab txog kev poob foob pob atomic rau nws los ntawm Amelikas. Nyij Pooj txoj kev npau taws rau Amelikas yuav tsis txias. Lub nplaim taws ntawm kev pauj kua zaub ntsuab yuav kub hnyiab hauv nws lub siab, vim li ntawd Nyij Pooj yuav tawm tsam Amelikas ntawm Is Nrias teb sab thaum lub sijhawm ua tsov rog thoob ntiaj teb thib peb. Raws li Nyij Pooj qhov kev thov, Lub Teb Chaws Yelemees kuj yuav tawm tsam ntawm Is Nrias teb sab.

12 Kev Tsov Rog Mahabharata uas Tseem Tshuav hauv Kali Yuga

Cov Panchasakhas tau sau nyob rau hauv cov ntawv Bhabishya Malika tias Kev Ua Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb uas tshwm sim thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga yuav yog qhov tseem tshuav ntawm kev ua rog Mahabharata. Cov Mahapurushas tau qhia meej tias kev ua rog Mahabharata 18 hnub thaum kawg ntawm Dwapara Yuga tsis tau ua tiav tag nrho vim tias, thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, Vaj Ntxwv Krishna tau txiav txim siab tsis ua qhov kawg ntawm kev ua rog thaum ntawd, tab sis yuav ua kom tiav thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga.

Tsis yog li ntawd xwb, cov Mahapurushas tau sau ntxaws txog qhov kev ua tsov rog no hauv lawv cov ntawv Malika thiab qhia ntau yam, xws li thaum twg nws yuav tshwm sim, muaj pes tsawg tus neeg yuav tuag thaum nws tshwm sim, nws yuav kav ntev npaum li cas, nws yuav tshwm sim li cas, leej twg yuav ua tus coj, leej twg yuav koom los ntawm Is Nrias teb sab, nws yuav xaus qhov twg, thiab yuav muaj dab tsi tshwm sim tom qab qhov kev ua tsov rog no, thiab lwm yam.

12.1 Qhov kev ua tsov rog no yuav tshwm sim thaum twg?

Odia Sloka:

ଯୁଗଶେଷ କଳି ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଯମ ମାଆ ସାତ ଭଉଣୀ ଧରିଥିବେ ଖପର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Yugaśheṣha kaḷi Bhārata juddha hoiba ghora,
Yama māā sāta bhauṇī dharithibe khapara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav tiav thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga. Hauv qhov kev ua tsov rog no, "Yama Maa Sata Bhauni" los yog Xya Leej Niam Vajtswv (Saptamatruka) uas nyob hauv Biraja Khetra (Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, thiab Narasimhi) yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg txhaum.

Piav qhia tias qhov kev ua tsov rog no yuav tshwm sim thaum kawg ntawm Kali Yuga, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna Shani āsi deuthibā ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
Dasha aṇā jiba chha-a aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, qhov kev sib ntaus sib tua uas tseem tshuav ntawm Is Nrias teb (Mahabharata) yuav pib thaum Meena-Shani (Pisces-Saturn) sib koom, uas yuav ua rau ntau tus neeg tuag.

12.2 Qhov kev ua tsov rog yuav tshwm sim li cas thiab yuav sib ntaus qhov twg thaum kawg?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika tias:

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଓଳିଏ ବାକି ଅଛି ଜାଣ,
କଳିଯୁଗରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇ ପୂରଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mahābhārata juddha oḷie bāki achi jāṇa,
Kaḷiyugare biśhwajuddha hoi pūraṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, paub tias ib feem ntawm kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata tseem tshuav, uas yuav tiav raws li Kev Ua Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb hauv Kali Yuga.

Tus Mahapurush sau ntxiv hauv nws phau ‘Jaiphula Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ପୁଣି ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କହେ ଯବନ ବାହି ଆସିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha,
Oḍiśhā deśhare puṇi hoiba lo jāiphula,
kahe jabana bāhi āsiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav tshwm sim hauv Odisha, thiab thaum lub sijhawm ntawd, ntau tus tub rog ntawm cov teb chaws yeeb ncuab yuav nkag mus rau hauv Is Nrias teb.

Saint Achyutananda Das sau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଳିଯୁଗରେ ମହାଭାରତଟି ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳରୁ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପତ୍ତି ହୋଇବ ଠିକେ ଠିକେ ଏହା ଶୁଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

E kaḷiyugare Mahābhārataṭi Utkaḷa bhūiñ pramāṇa,
Utkaḷaru sabu utpatti hoiba ṭhike ṭhike ehā śhuṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav sib ntaus hauv Kali Yuga, uas yuav tshwm sim hauv thaj av Utkala los yog Odisha.

12.3 Qhov kev ua tsov rog no yuav kav ntev npaum li cas?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das kuj tau kwv yees hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’ tias qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav no yuav kav tag nrho 13 lub hlis.

Tus Mahapurush sau tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଯେ ରହିଥିବ ଶୁଣ ହେତୁ ଦ୍ୱାରେ,
ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ସେତିକି ବେଳରେ।
ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ,
ସେ ଓଳିକେ ତେର ମାସ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Olie je rahithiba shuṇa hetu dwāre,
Bhārata juddha hoiba setiki xelare.
Sehi olikare nātha kali shesha hexa,
Se olike tera māsa bhoga je hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav kav tag nrho kaum peb lub hlis, thiab thaum qhov kev ua tsov rog ntawd tiav, qhov cuam tshuam ntawm Kali Yuga rau lub ntiaj teb no yuav xaus tag nrho.

12.4 Leej twg yuav koom los ntawm Is Nrias teb sab thaum kawg?

Cov Mahapurushas tau sau tias thaum kawg ntawm qhov kev ua tsov rog no, thaum ib sab yuav muaj cov tub rog loj heev ntawm cov teb chaws yeeb ncuab, lwm sab, tag nrho cov tub rog siab loj ntawm Mahabharata era thiab ntau txhiab tus neeg dawb huv, cov kws tshawb fawb, thiab cov pom kev yuav nyob hauv qab kev coj ntawm tus Tswv Kalki nws tus kheej.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ‘Bhabishyat Malika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ବେଳେ ଭାରତ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହାରିଯିବ ତ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ତ।
ଭାରତ ନରନାରୀ ସର୍ବେ ଡାକିବେ ହରି,
ରକ୍ଷାକର ପ୍ରଭୁ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଗଲୁଟି ସରି।
ଆହେ କୁଞ୍ଜବିହାରୀ ବାରେ ଶୁଣ ଗୁହାରି,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ତେଜି ଥରେ ଆସ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ଦେଖ ନେତ୍ର ଫେଡ଼ିତ ପୁଣ୍ୟଭୂମି ଭାରତ,
ବିଦେଶୀଙ୍କ ହସ୍ତେ ହୁଏ କି ହନ୍ତସନ୍ତ।
ଭାରତର କାଳର ଶୁଣି ସେ ଆଦିମୂଳ,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ପୁର ତେଜି ହେବେ ବାହାର।
କଳ୍କୀ ଦେବ ନାମ ବହି ସେ ନାରାୟଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳ ଦେଶ କଟକରେ ଦେବେ ଦର୍ଶନ।
ସେହି ବେଳରେ ଜାଣ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବଳରାମ,
ଭୀମ ସହ ବାହାରିବେ ବୀର ଅର୍ଜୁନ।
ଭୀଷ୍ମ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଆବର ଅଶ୍ୱତ୍ଥାମା ଯେ ବୀର,
କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଶଲ୍ୟ ଜୟଦ୍ରଥ ହେବେ ବାହାର।
ବେଲାଲସେନ ବୀର ପୁଣି ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବାଳବୀର ଅଭିମନ୍ୟୁ ଘେନି ସଙ୍ଗର।
ବୀର ବବ୍ରୁବାହନ ବାହାରିବଟି ଜାଣ,
ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ଘଟୋତ୍କଚ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନିଣ।
ସର୍ବେ ହୋଇଣ ମେଳି ବିଦେଶୀୟଙ୍କୁ ଦଳି,
ଦେବେ ମୁହୂର୍ତ୍ତକ ମଧ୍ଯେ ଲଗାଇ କଳି।
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଶକତି ବଳେ ଅପଲକ ନେତ୍ରରେ,
ଚାହିଁ ବିଜ୍ଞାନ ଶକ୍ତିକି ଜିଣିବେ ହେଳେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ehibeḷe Bhārata nishche hārijiba ta,
hāhākāra paḍijiba chāriāḍe ta.
Bhārata naranārī sarbe dākibe Hari,
rakhākara prabhu nishche galuṭi sari.
Āhe Kuñjabihārī bāre shuṇa guhāରି,
Baikuṇṭha teji thare āsa Shrī Hari.
Dekha netra pheḍita puṇyabhūmi Bhārata,
bideshīṅka haste hue ki hantasanta.
Bhāratara kāḷara shuṇi se ādimūḷa,
Baikuṇṭhapura teji hebe bāhāra.
Kalkī deba nāma bahi se Nārāyaṇa,
Utkaḷa desha Kaṭakare debe darshana.
Sehibeḷere jāṇa Shrī Kṛṣṇa Baḷarāma,
Bhīma saha bāhāribe bīra Arjuna.
Bhīṣma Droṇa ābara Ashwatthāmā je bīra,
Karṇa Shalya Jayadratha hebe bāhāra.
Belāḷasena bīra puṇi hebe bāhāra,
bāḷabīra Abhimanyu gheni saṅgara.
Bīra Babhrubāhana bāhāribaṭi jāṇa,
Ekalabya Ghaṭotkacha saṅge gheniṇa.
Sarbe hoiṇa meḷi bideshīyaṅku daḷi,
debe muhūrttaka madhye lagāi kaḷi.
Mantra shakti baḷe apalaka netrare,
chāhi bijñāna shaktiki jiṇibe heḷe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum Kev Ua Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb Thib Peb, pom cov yeeb ncuab txawv teb chaws tawm tsam Is Nrias teb los ntawm plaub sab, cov neeg Is Nrias teb yuav ntshai heev thiab quaj qw hauv kev nyuaj siab. Vim tsis muaj kev pab, lawv txhua tus yuav nco txog tus Tswv thiab thov kev tiv thaiv. Lawv yuav hais tias—Au tus Tswv, thov mloog peb cov lus thov, cawm peb, tawm hauv Vaikuntha los rau hauv ntiaj teb los tiv thaiv peb.

Vim cov lus thov khuv xim ntawm cov neeg pe hawm thiab pom kev tawm tsam Is Nrias teb los ntawm cov yeeb ncuab txawv teb chaws, tus Tswv Brahman Narayana (Maha Vishnu) nws tus kheej, hauv nws tus Kalki incarnation, yuav tshwm sim hauv Cuttack, Odisha, thiab muab darshan rau cov neeg pe hawm.

Tus Mahapurush tau sau tias nrog rau tus Tswv Kalki tib lub sijhawm ntawd, Bhima, Arjuna, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babhruvahana, Ekalavya, thiab Ghatotkacha, uas twb tau yug los lawm, yuav sib ntaus ntawm Is Nrias teb sab thiab ua rau cov teb chaws yeeb ncuab swb tag. Lub zog mantra-vajtswv ntawm cov tub rog siab loj no yuav ua pov thawj tias zoo dua li lub zog tshawb fawb niaj hnub no ntawm cov yeeb ncuab.

Hais txog tib lub ntsiab lus no, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରସ୍ତାବିତ କଳି ଭାରତ ସରିବ ହେବ ଭାରତ ସମର।
ତିନିଯୁଗ ଯୋଦ୍ଧା ଏକ ଯୁଗରେ ମହାସମର ଲାଗିବ,
କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକ୍ତ ମନୋସ୍କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Prastābita kaḷi Bhārata sariba heba Bhārata samara.
Tinijuga joddhā eka jugare mahāsamara lāgiba,
kahai Achyuta bhakta hebe mukta manoskāmanā pūriba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata yuav sib ntaus hauv Kali Yuga, uas cov tub rog siab loj ntawm tag nrho peb lub sijhawm dhau los yuav koom.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau ntxiv hauv ‘Chakada Madana’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ସମର ହେବ ଦେଲି ଚେତାଇ,
ସ୍ୱୟଂ ପରଂବ୍ରହ୍ମ ମୂରତି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତସାଇଁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Oḷie Bhārata samara heba deli chetāi,
swayaṃ Parambrahma mūrati prabhu Jagatasāiñ.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav tshwm sim hauv qab kev coj ntawm tus Tswv Brahman Narayana (Kalki incarnation) nws tus kheej.

Hais txog lub ntsiab lus no, ib tug ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, kuj tau sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Nidagha Nighanta’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ରହି ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ,
ଆଦେଶ ଦେବେଟି ମାଧବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Oḷie rahi juddha heba,
ādeśha debeṭi Mādhaba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav yuav tiav, thiab Madhava nws tus kheej, tus Tswv Kalki loj, yuav muab txoj cai pib qhov kev ua tsov rog no.

Ib tug neeg dawb huv loj ntawm Odisha yug hauv xyoo pua 20, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, kuj tau sau txog lub ntsiab lus no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Baishnaba Gita’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ତୋପର ତପର ସମର,
ହୋଇବ ଅତି ଭୟଙ୍କର।
ତପକୁ ତୋପ ନ ପାରିବ,
ତପର ବଳ ବେଶୀ ହେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Topara tapara samara,
hoiba ati bhayaṅkara.
tapaku topa na pāriba,
tapara baḷa beśhī heba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, yuav muaj kev sib ntaus sib tua hnyav heev ntawm lub zog phom loj ntawm Is Nrias teb cov teb chaws yeeb ncuab thiab lub zog ascetic (Tapasya) ntawm Is Nrias teb cov neeg dawb huv thiab cov kws tshawb fawb coob leej, uas lub zog foob pob niaj hnub no ntawm cov yeeb ncuab yuav tsis muaj zog ua ntej Is Nrias teb lub zog ascetic, thiab thaum kawg Is Nrias teb yuav yeej hauv qhov kev ua tsov rog loj no.

12.5 Qhov kev ua tsov rog yuav tiav qhov twg hauv Is Nrias teb?

Cov Mahapurushas tau sau hauv cov ntawv Malika tias thaum cov tub rog txawv teb chaws mus txog Odisha, lawv yuav sib ntaus ntawm ntau qhov chaw hauv Odisha, xws li feem ntau hauv Birat Gadi ntawm Cuttack, Choudwar, Jobra, Barabati, thiab Kaliaboda; hauv Puri; hauv Jajpur; hauv Gohira Tikiri ntawm Bhadrak; hauv Paradip; hauv Balasore, thiab lwm yam.

Txawm hais tias qhov kev ua tsov rog no yuav tshwm sim ntawm tag nrho cov chaw no hauv Odisha, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau hais meej tias nws yuav xaus tag nrho ntawm Khandagiri hauv Bhubaneswar thaum tus Tswv loj nws tus kheej rhuav tshem tag nrho cov tub rog ntawm cov teb chaws yeeb ncuab nrog nws Sudarshana Chakra.

Tus Mahapurush sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kalpatika’ tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ ବହୁତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରୂପ ଧରି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକ୍ର ଧରି ବିନାଶିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Oḍiśhā deśhare Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre bahuta juddha hoiba,
Ananta Keśharī rūpa dhari prabhu chakra dhari bināśhiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, yuav muaj kev ua tsov rog hnyav heev ntawm Khandagiri hauv Odisha. Ntawm tib qhov chaw ntawd, tus Tswv Kalki loj yuav rhuav tshem ntau tus tub rog nrog nws Sudarshana Chakra.

12.6 Yuav muaj dab tsi tshwm sim los ntawm qhov kev ua tsov rog no thiab yuav muaj dab tsi tshwm sim tom qab nws?

Hauv ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau ib zaj paj huam uas nws thawj feem yog raws li hauv qab no:

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Sehi oḷikare nātha kaḷi śheṣha heba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, sai li sai tau thaum qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata no tiav, qhov cuam tshuam ntawm Kali Yuga yuav xaus.

Tom qab ntawd Mahapurush Achyutananda Das kuj sau ib zaj paj huam hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’, uas nws thawj kab hais tias:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଯେ ସରିବ ପୃଥିବୀ ଶାନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha je sariba pruthibī śhānta hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, thaum qhov kev ua tsov rog Mahabharata uas tseem tshuav tiav, lub ntiaj teb yuav muaj kev thaj yeeb. Tom qab ntawd tag nrho cov kev kub ntxhov hauv ntiaj teb yuav xaus.

12.7 Kev piav qhia txog lub tebchaws muaj hwj chim loj xws li Amelikas thiab Tebchaws Europe txoj kev nyob hauv Kev Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb

Cov Panchasakha Mahapurushas tau sau rau hauv lawv cov ntawv Malika tias Kev Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb Zaum Peb yuav hnyav heev uas txhua lub tebchaws yuav raug cuam tshuam. Txhua lub tebchaws uas niaj hnub no suav hais tias yog lub ntiaj teb cov tub rog muaj zog tshaj plaws, qhov kev tsov rog kawg ntawm Kali Yuga no yuav ua rau lawv puas tsuaj tag.

Piav qhia txog Amelikas txoj kev nyob hauv qhov kev tsov rog no, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das tau sau rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika hu ua ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଭାଇ ମୂଳୁ ଧ୍ୱଂସ ଯିବ,
ଗର୍ବରେ ଜଳୁଛି ରାଜା ବିଶ୍ୱ ମୁଖ୍ୟ ହେବ।
ଧରାକୁ ମଣୁଛି ସରା କହେ ମୁହିଁ ଶ୍ରେଷ୍ଠ,
ଉଡ଼ାଇବି ପୃଥିବୀକୁ କରେ ମୁଁ ଶପଥ।
କାହାରି ଗର୍ବ ସହି ନ ପାରିବେ ଧାତା,
ଜଳାଇ ଦେବେ ତାହାକୁ ଉଡ଼ାଇବେ ମଥା।
ନ୍ୟୁୟର୍କ ସହର ଧ୍ୱଂସ ପଦା ହୋଇଯିବ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଅଥର୍ବ ନିର୍ବେଦ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Āmerikā rāṣhṭra bhāi mūḷu dhwaṅsa jiba,
garbare jaḷuchi rājā biśhwa mukhya heba.
dharāku maṇuchi sarā kahe muiñ śhreṣhṭha,
uḍāibi pruthibīku kare muñ śhapatha.
kāhāri garba sahi na pāribe dhātā,
jaḷāi debe tāhāku uḍāibe mathā.
New York sahara dhwaṅsa padā hoijiba,
hāhākāra paḍijiba atharba nirbeda.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, ib lub tebchaws hu ua Amelikas yuav suav hais tias nws yog tus thawj coj ntawm lub ntiaj teb. Nws yuav poob rau hauv kev khav theeb thiab kev qia dub, nws yuav hu nws tus kheej tias yog tus zoo tshaj plaws thiab hem tias yuav rhuav tshem lwm lub tebchaws hauv kev tsov rog. Tus Mahapurush tau hais ntxiv tias tus Tswv Loj Kawg Nkaus tsis kam lees leej twg txoj kev khav theeb thiab yog li ntawd nws yuav rhuav tshem Amelikas txoj kev khav theeb. Amelikas lub nroog New York yuav raug rhuav tshem, thiab lub tebchaws ntawd yuav raug kev puas tsuaj tag nrho ua pov thawj rau kev puas tsuaj hnyav.

Hauv ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ ntawd, Saint Achyutananda Das sau dua:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ଇଉରୋପ ମହାଦେଶ ଜାଣ,
ସ୍ଥିତି ହରାଇବ ତାର ବିଷାକ୍ତ ପବନ।
ସବୁରି ଧ୍ୱଂସର କାରଣ ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ନିଶ୍ଚିହ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଗର୍ବି ଅଟେ ପୁଣ।
ନିଜର ଇଜ୍ଜତ ପୃଥିବୀ ପରେ ଦେଖାଇଲା,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧକୁ ତ ଜୀବନରେ ସିଦ୍ଧହସ୍ତ କଲା।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Āmerikā Iuropa mahādesha jāṇa,
Sthiti harāiba tāra biṣākta pabana.
Saburi dhwaṃsara kāraṇa Āmerikā jāṇa,
Nishchinha hoiba juddhe garbi aṭe puṇa.
Nijara ijjata pṛthwī pare dekhāilā,
Juddhaku ta jībanare siddhahasta kalā.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, qhov ua rau Kev Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb Zaum Peb yuav yog ib lub tebchaws hu ua ‘Amelikas’. Lub tebchaws ntawd yuav tsim nws tus kheej txoj kev hwm thiab kev khav theeb thoob plaws ntiaj teb thiab tau txais kev txawj ntse hauv kev ua tsov rog. Tus Mahapurush tau sau ntxiv tias vim yog qhov kev khav theeb no, nws lub npe thiab nws txoj kev yuav raug rhuav tshem hauv Kev Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb Zaum Peb. Tom qab kev tsov rog, huab cua nyob ntawd yuav dhau los ua tshuaj lom, thiab vim yog qhov ntawd, ntau tus neeg yuav tuag hauv Amelikas thiab Tebchaws Europe.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau rau hauv nws phau ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶେଷେ ଇଉରୋପ ଦେଶ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ହୋଇବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ,
ବଞ୍ଚି ରହିବେ କେବଳ ଭକ୍ତ ଅଂଶ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Śheṣhe Iuropa deśha juddhe hoiba dhwaṅsa,
bañchi rahibe kebaḷa bhakta aṅśha.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, hauv qhov kev tsov rog no, Tebchaws Europe yuav raug rhuav tshem tag nrho. Tsuas yog cov neeg pe hawm tus Tswv xwb thiaj yuav dim, tsis muaj leej twg yuav dim hauv cov tebchaws ntawd.

Hauv ‘Jaiphula Malika’, Saint Achyutananda Das sau:

Odia Sloka:

ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଦେଶ,
ହାଡ଼ ମାଳ ହୋଇଯିବ ବିଶେଷ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୀ ଦୁଲୁ ଦୁଲୁ ହେବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Paśhchima uttara deśha,
hāḍa māḷa hoijiba biśheṣha lo jāiphula,
mahī dulu dulu heba dhwaṅsa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov tebchaws nyob rau sab Hnub Poob thiab sab Qaum Teb yuav raug puas tsuaj hauv Kev Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb.

Achyutananda Das ji sau dua hauv ‘Jaiphula Malika’ ntawd:

Odia Sloka:

ଜର୍ମାନୀ ରୁଷିଆ ଆଦି,
ଇଟାଲୀ ଜାପାନ ତୁରସ୍କ ଗାଦି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସେହି ସରବେ ହୋଇବେ ରଦ୍ଦି।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Jarmānī Ruṣhiā ādi,
Itālī Jāpāna Turuska gādi lo jāiphula,
sehi sarabe hoibe raddi.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, lub tebchaws Yelemes, Lavxias, Ltalis, Nyij Pooj, thiab Qaib Ntxhw, thiab lwm yam, yuav raug puas tsuaj hnyav heev los ntawm Kev Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb.

Raws li peb tau hais lawm, tag nrho cov xwm txheej saum toj no yuav tshwm sim feem ntau thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani yoga, piv txwv li, ntawm xyoo 2025 thiab 2028.

Thaum Lub Peb Hlis 29, 2025, Saturn tau nkag mus rau hauv lub hnub qub Pisces (Meena). Qee zaum txav mus rau tom qab (Vakri) lossis rov qab, thiab qee zaum txav mus rau pem hauv ntej (Margi) lossis ncaj nraim, Saturn yuav nyob hauv lub hnub qub Pisces mus txog Lub Ob Hlis 23, 2028. Lub caij no, txij Lub Rau Hli 3, 2027, mus txog Lub Kaum Hli 20, 2027, yuav muaj kev hloov luv luv ntawm Saturn hauv lub hnub qub Aries (Mesha).

Tam sim no muaj ib lo lus nug tseem ceeb tshwm sim ntawm no. Nws tau kwv yees li 10 lub hlis txij thaum Meena-Shani yoga pib, tab sis cov xwm txheej rhuav tshem no tseem tsis tau pib. Vim li cas? Tseeb tiag, muaj kev puas tsuaj ntuj tsim thoob plaws ntiaj teb, kev tsov rog kuj tseem tab tom tshwm sim ntawm ntau lub tebchaws, tab sis lawv tsis tshwm sim ntawm qib loj uas cov Panchasakhas tau kwv yees rau hauv cov ntawv Malika! Ib lo lus nug pheej tshwm sim hauv peb ntau tus lub siab tias thaum twg cov xwm txheej no yuav hnyav zuj zus!

Thaum twg tiag tiag Kev Puas Tsuaj Loj yuav pib? Bhabishya Malika hais li cas txog qhov no? Malika yeej hais tias cov kev ua si rhuav tshem yuav tshwm sim hauv Meena-Shani, tab sis Malika piav qhia tias cov kev ua si rhuav tshem no yuav pib thaum kawg ntawm xyoo 2026 es tsis yog xyoo 2025.

Ib tug ntawm cov Panchasakhas, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, tau sau txog cov kev kub ntxhov uas tsis tau muaj dua los thoob plaws ntiaj teb thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani hauv nws cov ntawv Malika thiab tau muab cov ntaub ntawv txog lawv lub sijhawm tseeb. Hauv ib phau ntawv Malika, nws tau sau:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଅକାଳେ ମଡ଼କ ପଡ଼ିବ ସେହି ସମୟରେ ଜାଣ।
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପରେ କମ୍ପିବ ସକଳ ମେଦିନୀ,
କାଳ ଭୈରବୀ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଯେ ରଡ଼ି।
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ତୃତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଜଳ ପ୍ରଳୟ ହୋଇବ ଭଲେ ତୁହି ଜାଣ।
ହୁତୁ ହୁତୁ ହୋଇ ଜଳୁଥିବେ ହିଙ୍ଗୁଳା ମାତିବେ,
ବଡ଼ ବଡ଼ କୋଠା ଘର ଜାଳି ଯେ ଦେବେ।
ଚୋର ଟାଉଟର ସଂଖ୍ୟା ପ୍ରବଳ ବଢ଼ିବ,
ସେଥିପାଇଁ ଦେବୀଙ୍କର କୋପେ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ନ ଦେଖିଲା ନେତ୍ର ପୁଣି ସେ ସମୟେ ଦେଖିବ,
ତ୍ରାହି ତ୍ରାହି ବୋଲି ପୁଣି ଡାକ ଛାଡୁଥିବ।
ହରି ଆଶ୍ରା କର ତୁହି ନିସ୍ତାର ପାଇବୁ,
ଦାସ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ କହେ ବିହନ ହୋଇବୁ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Mīna śhani yogare je dwitīya charaṇa,
akāḷe maḍaka paḍiba sehi samayare jāṇa.
bhūmikampare kampiba sakaḷa medinī,
kāḷa bhairabī chāḍibe śhūnyare je raḍi.
mīna śhani yogare je tṛutīya charaṇa,
jaḷa pralaya hoiba bhale tuhi jāṇa.
hutu hutu hoi jaḷuthibe hiṅguḷā mātibe,
baḍa baḍa koṭhā ghara jāḷi je debe.
chora ṭāuṭara saṅkhyā prabala baḍhiba,
sethipāiñ debīṅkara kope nāśha jiba.
na dekhilā netra puṇi se samaye dekhiba,
trāhi trāhi boli puṇi ḍāka chāḍuthiba.
Hari āśhrā kara tuhi nistāra pāibu,
dāsa Yaśhobanta kahe bihana hoibu.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, hauv ob xyoos thiab ib nrab ntawm Meena-Shani yoga, thaum lub sijhawm theem ob (xyoo 2026-2027) thiab theem peb (xyoo 2027-2028), yuav muaj kev tuag tsis txog sijhawm ntau heev, thiab kev kub ntxhov yuav kav thoob plaws txhua qhov chaw. Yuav muaj av qeeg hnyav, tag nrho lub ntiaj teb yuav tshee hnyo. Yuav muaj suab quaj Bhairavi, piv txwv li, Niam Bhairavi yuav mam li nco dheev ua suab nrov heev los ntawm qhov chaw khoob. Yuav pom kev ntshai ntawm dej nyab loj heev hauv ntiaj teb. Vim yog Niam Hingula txoj kev npau taws, cov tsev loj, tsev loj, thiab lwm yam, yuav kub hnyiab hnyav heev. Tus Mahapurush tau sau tias txhua tus neeg phem yuav raug kev npau taws ntawm cov Niam Vajtswv. Thaum ntawd, tib neeg yuav pom txhua yam uas lawv lub qhov muag tsis tau pom txog thaum ntawd, piv txwv li, txhua yam yuav tshwm sim hauv txoj kev tsis tau muaj dua los. Txhua tus neeg yuav quaj thov kev hlub. Cov neeg uas tso siab rau tus Tswv Shri Hari txhais taw thiab nrhiav nws txoj kev tiv thaiv, tus Mahapurush tau hais tias lawv ib leeg yuav pom kev cawm dim ntawm txhua yam no thiab dhau los ua cov noob ntawm Satya Yuga.

Hauv nqe lus saum toj no, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das tau hais tias cov kev ua si rhuav tshem no yuav pib tiag tiag txij thaum theem ob ntawm Meena-Shani yoga, piv txwv li, xyoo 2026-2027.

Thaum lub sijhawm Kali Yuga no, ntau tus Mahapurushas tau yug los hauv Odisha ib ntus. Thaum xyoo pua 20, ib tug Mahapurush yug hauv Odisha tau qhia tawm cov lus kwv yees uas tus Tswv Loj Kawg Nkaus nws tus kheej, Niam Yogamaya, thiab cov vajtswv thiab vajtswv poj niam tau hais rau cov neeg pe hawm. Tus Mahapurush tau sau:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଆଦେଶ ଦେବୀଙ୍କୁ ହେବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Binśha chabisha sāla sariba,
Śhrī Hari ādeśha debīṅku heba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tom qab kawg ntawm xyoo 2026 los txog lossis xyoo 2026 xaus, tus Tswv Kalki yuav muab lus txib rau txhua lub hwj chim vajtswv (Niam Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, cuaj lab Katyayanis) kom pib kev rhuav tshem.

Tus Mahapurush tom qab ntawd tau sau ntxiv:

Odia Sloka:

ସମୟ ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ ଯେବେ,
ସବୁ ଦେବା ଦେବୀ ବାହାରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Samaya binśha chabisha sāla sariba jebe,
sabu debā debī bāhāre biśhwajuddha karibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tom qab xyoo 2026, txhua tus vajtswv thiab vajtswv poj niam yuav ua suab nrov hu rau kev sib ntaus sib tua Deva-Asura. Kev pib ntawm kev tsov rog (Kev Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb) yuav tshwm sim hauv ntiaj teb.

Nws tsim nyog sau cia tias hauv ob nqe lus saum toj no, tus Mahapurush tau sau meej meej tias “Xyoo 2026”.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau rau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଛଅ ସାତ ମିଶାଇଣ ତେର ଯୋଗ କର,
ଏହିଠାରୁ ଘୋର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରତ ବକ୍ଷର।
ସେ ବେଳେ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟଟି ହେବ ଜାଣ,
ଛିନ୍ନଛତ୍ର ଏ ପୃଥିବୀ କହିଲି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chha-a sāta mishaiṇa tera yoga kara,
ehi thāru ghora juddha bhārata bakṣhara.
se beḷe khaṇḍa pralayaṭi heba jāṇa,
chhinnachhatra e pruthibī kahili pramāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, muab rau (6) nrog xya (7) ua ke thiab tom qab ntawd ntxiv kaum peb (13) rau qhov sib ntxiv, piv txwv li, 6+7+13=26, los ntawm qhov kev tsov rog hnyav yuav pib hauv Is Nrias teb. Tom qab lub sijhawm ntawd, yuav muaj xwm txheej ntawm kev rhuav tshem ib nrab (Khanda Pralaya) thiab lub ntiaj teb yuav pib raug puas tsuaj.

Nws tsim nyog sau cia tias ntawm no Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, tsis zoo li ob nqe lus saum toj no, tsis tau sau “Xyoo 2026” tab sis tsuas yog sau 26. Tej zaum ntawm no, los ntawm 26, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das qhov qhia yog rau xyoo 2026 xwb.

Tus Mahapurush yug hauv xyoo pua 20 sau dua:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ପରେ ତ କରିବୁ ବିଚାର,
ମନେ ନ କରିବୁ ଯଦି ଯିବୁ ଯମପୁର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Binśha chabisha pare ta karibu bichāra,
mane na karibu jadi jibu Yamapura.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hauv nqe lus saum toj no, Niam Yogamaya hais tias tom qab xyoo 2026, txhua tus yuav raug txiav txim. Txhua tus neeg uas tsis ntseeg, phem, ua txhaum, thiab lwm yam, yuav raug rhuav tshem tag nrho.

Yog li ntawd, yuav tsum nkag siab meej tias cov kev ua si rhuav tshem Kalki uas Malika tau piav qhia yuav tshwm sim thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani yoga nws tus kheej, tab sis cov no yuav tsis tshwm sim hauv xyoo 2025 tab sis yuav pib txij thaum kawg ntawm xyoo 2026.

Cov kev ua si rhuav tshem no yuav kav ntev npaum li cas, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau txog qhov no hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Kaliyuga Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚବିଶ ଠାବରୁ ଅଣତିରିଶ ଯାଏ ପ୍ରମାଦ ଅଛି ଅପାର,
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେଟି ହୋ ଗରୁଡ଼ ମହାକଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଭିତରେଟି ଗରୁଡ଼ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସରିବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Chabiśha ṭhābaru aṇatiriśha jāe pramāda achi apāra,
aṇatiriśha aṅkareṭi ho Garuḍa mahā Kaḷkī abatāra.
aṇatiriśha bhitareṭi Garuḍa sabu utpāta sariba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov kev ua si rhuav tshem yuav pib txij tus lej 24 thiab yuav kav mus txog tus lej 29. Txhua yam kev ua si rhuav tshem yuav xaus rau tus lej 29.

Nco ntsoov tias hauv nqe lus saum toj no, tus Mahapurush tau tham txog tus lej 24, thiab tsis yog xyoo 2024. Tab sis tus Mahapurush tau ua kom meej meej tias cov kev ua si rhuav tshem no, pib txij tus lej 24 thiab kav mus txog tus lej 29, yuav txuas ntxiv mus rau tag nrho 6 xyoos. Tus Mahapurush tsis tau qhia meej tias cov no yuav pib xyoo twg thiab yuav xaus xyoo twg, tab sis tau hais tias txij tus lej 24 mus txog tus lej 29, piv txwv li, rau tag nrho 6 xyoos, cov kev ua si rhuav tshem no yuav txuas ntxiv.

Vim hais tias los ntawm cov nqe lus saum toj no peb twb paub lawm tias cov kev ua si rhuav tshem no yuav pib txij thaum kawg ntawm xyoo 2026, peb tuaj yeem yooj yim xaus lus tias tus lej 24 ntawm ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ yog qhia txog xyoo 2026-2027. Raws li ntawd, tus lej 29 ntawm tib zaj lus no ntawm tib phau ntawv Malika no yog qhia txog xyoo 2031-2032.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, los ntawm nws phau Malika Pothi, tau piav qhia dua –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହା ତ ହେଉଛି କାଳର ଭୟ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ତହିଁ,
ଏହା ତ କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳଟି ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳ କରୁଅଛନ୍ତି ହେତୁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଆଉ ବା ଏକତିରିଶ ସେ ତ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ।
ମହାମାରୀ ସେ ତ ଆସିବ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଆସ,
ତେତିଶି ମସିହା ସୁଦ୍ଧାଟି କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଠା ଠିକଣା ଯେ ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହି,
ଏଭଳି ସମୟ ଆସିବ ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କାହାରି ପତା ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ,
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନୀମାନେ ବାଟଟି ପାଇବେ ନାହିଁରେ ଜାଣ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Ehā ta heuchhi kāḷara bhaya Achyutī tahiñ,
ehā ta Kaḷkī kheḷaṭi dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kaḷkī kheḷa karuachhanti hetu pramāṇa,
āu bā ekatiriśa se ta heture jāṇa.
Mahāmārī se ta āsiba kahe Achyutī āsa,
Tetiriśhi masihā suddhāṭi kahe dīna Achyuta.
Ṭhā ṭhikaṇā je rahiba nāhiñ Achyutī kahi,
ebhaḷi samaya āsiba dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kāhāri patā rahiba nāhiñ heture jāṇa,
Baijñānīmāne bāṭaṭi pāibe nāhiñ re jāṇa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tus Tswv Kalki yuav ua nws txoj kev ua si (nws cov kev ua si rhuav tshem). Cov kev ua si rhuav tshem no yuav txuas ntxiv mus txog xyoo 2033. Lub caij no, yuav muaj ntau yam kab mob sib kis, uas yuav ua rau tib neeg lub neej kub ntxhov. Ib lub sijhawm yuav los txog thaum yuav muaj kev kub ntxhov thoob plaws txhua qhov chaw. Txhua tus neeg yuav nyob hauv kev txom nyem. Txawm tias cov kws tshawb fawb txoj kev paub yuav tsis muaj txiaj ntsig.

Txawm li cas los xij, Achyutananda Das ji kuj tau hais hauv nws phau ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ tias cov haujlwm rhuav tshem tseem ceeb yuav xaus rau tus lej 27.

Tus Mahapurush sau –

Odia Sloka:

ସତାଇଶ ଅଙ୍କେ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଦିଗରୁ ସମୁଦ୍ର ଆସିବ ମାଡ଼ି,
ଉତ୍ତରରୁ ଗଙ୍ଗା ଉଛୁଳି ପଡ଼ିଣ ତହିଁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଦେବ ଧାଡ଼ି।
ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଦିଗରୁ ଜଳ ସ୍ରୋତ ଏକ ଆସି ତହିଁ ଯିବ ମିଶି,
ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟର ସୃଜନା କରିବ ପାପୀ ତହିଁ ଯିବେ ଭାସି।
ପୁଣ୍ୟାତ୍ମାମାନଙ୍କୁ ସ୍ୱୟଂ ବଳଦେବ ଉଦ୍ଧାର କରି ରଖିବେ,
ଭାରତର ଗିରି ପର୍ବତ ପାହାଡ଼େ ନେଇ ଯତନେ ପାଳିବେ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Satāisha aṅke dakhina digaru samudra āsiba māḍi,
uttararu Gaṅgā uchhuḷi paḍiṇa tahiñ saṅge deba dhāḍi.
Pashchima digaru jala srota eka āsi tahiñ jiba mishi,
khaṇḍa praḷayara sṛujanā kariba pāpī tahiñ jibe bhāsi.
Puṇyātmāmānaṅku swayaṃ Baḷadeba uddhāra kari rakhibe,
Bhāratara giri parbata pāhāḍe nei jatane pāḷibe.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, rau tus lej 27, nthwv dej hiav txwv yuav los ntawm Sab Qab Teb thiab yuav ntsib tus dej Ganges uas ntws dhau los ntawm Sab Qaum Teb. Tib lub sijhawm, nthwv dej loj heev ntawm tsunami kuj yuav los ntawm Sab Hnub Poob thiab ua rau muaj kev puas tsuaj. Hauv qhov kev rhuav tshem ib nrab no, ntau tus neeg ua txhaum yuav tuag. Leej twg yog cov neeg dawb huv thiab cov neeg ncaj ncees, Baladev Nws tus kheej yuav cawm lawv thiab khaws lawv kom nyab xeeb nrog kev saib xyuas ntawm cov roob thiab toj siab hauv Is Nrias teb.

Raws li tus Mahapurush, qhov no yuav yog qhov kev puas tsuaj kawg ntawm cov kev puas tsuaj loj uas yuav tshwm sim rau tus lej 27, piv txwv li, hauv xyoo 2029-2030 raws li qhov kev suav tau hais saum toj no.

Tam sim no cia peb sim nkag siab txog lub sijhawm no nrog qee cov nqe lus ntxiv. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv ‘Bhabishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ସତୁରି ଯାଏ ଠୁଳ,
ଲାଗି ରହିବ ତୃତୀୟ ମହାସମର।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଚମକ ପଡିବ ସ୍ଵର୍ଗ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ପାତାଳ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Pāñcha barṣha kāḷa saturi jāe ṭhuḷa,
lāgi rahiba tṛutīya mahāsamara.
pāñcha barṣha kāḷa juddha hoiba ghora,
chamaka paḍiba swarga martya pātāḷa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb Zaum Peb yuav kav ntev li tsib xyoos. Yuav muaj kev sib ntaus sib tua hnyav rau tsib xyoos; Ntuj, Ntiaj Teb, thiab Ntiaj Teb Hauv Qab yuav tshee hnyo.

Achyutananda Das ji sau hauv lwm phau ntawv Malika ntawm nws –

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କହନ୍ତି ବାସବ ଶୁଣ,
ଚବିଶ ଠାରୁ ସତେଇଶ ଜାଣ।
ଏଥି ଭିତରେ ଯେ ହୋଇବ ଗୋଳ,
କାହାରି ନ ରହିବ ଥଳକୁଳ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Śhrī Hari kahanti Bāsaba śhuṇa,
chabiśha ṭhāru sataiśha jāṇa.
ethi bhitare je hoiba goḷa,
kāhāri na rahiba thaḷakuḷa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Hauv nqe lus saum toj no, Vaj Ntxwv Shri Krishna, thaum tham nrog Indra, hais tias yuav muaj kev sib ntaus sib tua hnyav txij li tus lej 24 mus rau tus lej 27.

Nws yog qhov tseeb los ntawm ob nqe lus sau saum toj no tias kev sib ntaus sib tua tseem ceeb lossis Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb Zaum Peb yuav kav ntev li 4-5 xyoos. Qhov ntawd yog, pib xyoo 2026-2027 thiab xaus rau xyoo 2029-2030.

Yog li ntawd, qhov kev rhuav tshem tseem ceeb kawg nkaus uas Mahapurush tau hais hauv ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ hauv tus lej 27 lossis xyoo 2029-2030 yog phim zoo kawg nkaus nrog ob nqe lus saum toj no.

Los ntawm cov kev sib tham saum toj no, yog tias muaj kev xaus luv luv:

Xyoo 2026 – 2027: Lub sijhawm kev puas tsuaj yuav pib.
Xyoo 2029 – 2030: Cov xwm txheej hnyav tshaj plaws thiab kev puas tsuaj loj yuav xaus.
Xyoo 2031 – 2032: Tag nrho cov xwm txheej puas tsuaj yuav xaus.

Nco ntsoov: Cov kev ua phem tseem ceeb uas yuav xaus rau xyoo 2029 suav nrog tag nrho cov uas feem ntau tau tham txog, xws li – av qeeg loj, dej nyab hnyav, cua daj cua dub, hluav taws kub, pob zeb poob, kab mob-kev sib kis, Is Nrias teb kev sib ntaus sib tua nrog cov teb chaws yeeb ncuab, Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb, Kali-Mahabharata kev sib ntaus sib tua, kev rhuav tshem Hirakud thiab lwm lub pas dej, tsaus ntuj rau xya hnub thiab xya hmo, Bhairavi Dak, thiab lwm yam.

13 Pib ntawm Satyayuga

Yuav nkag mus rau Satyayuga, nws yog qhov tsim nyog rau tib neeg taug kev ntawm Dharma. Nws yuav tsum hais qhov tseeb thiab ua raws li qhov tseeb xwb; nws yuav tsum tsis txhob xaiv txoj kev npau taws tab sis txoj kev thaj yeeb. Nws yuav tsum muaj kev hlub, kev ua siab zoo, thiab kev khuv leej rau txhua tus tsiaj. Nws yuav tsum tsis txhob ua rau ib tus tsiaj twg mob siab los ntawm nws lub siab, nws cov lus, thiab nws tus cwj pwm. Nws yuav tsum dhau los ua tus xav zoo rau txhua tus tsiaj yam tsis muaj laj thawj, nws tus cwj pwm yuav tsum zam txim rau lwm tus, nws yuav tsum yog tus neeg yooj yim thiab ncaj ncees, yuav tsum tsis txhob muaj kev ntxub ntxaug rau leej twg, thiab yuav tsum paub yuav ua li cas tswj nws lub siab. Nws yuav tsum yog tus pe hawm Vajtswv, ib txwm tsom nws lub siab rau Vajtswv, siv sijhawm hu nkauj qhuas Vajtswv, hu Vajtswv lub npe, koom nrog kev sib sau ua ke (Satsang), thov Vajtswv peb zaug ib hnub (Trisandhya), ua raws li cov vaj lug kub, thiab nyeem Shrimad Bhagavat Mahapurana (paub tias yog Mahapurana ntawm 18 Puranas) txhua hnub. Nws yuav tsum yog tus nyeem lossis hnov Vajtswv cov kev ua si. Nws yuav tsum noj zaub mov huv thiab Sattvic (vegetarian). Nrog rau kev ua nws lub luag haujlwm hauv ntiaj teb, nws yuav tsum koom nrog Yoga, hu npe, thiab lwm yam. Nws yuav tsum ib txwm hwm nws niam nws txiv thiab cov laus, qhia kev hlub rau cov hluas, thiab tsim kev phooj ywg nrog cov phooj ywg.

Txhua tus ntawm peb xav nkag mus rau Satyayuga los ntawm kev khiav tawm ntawm cov kev ua phem uas yuav los, kev puas tsuaj ntuj tsim, thiab Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb, thiab los ntawm kev tau txais Vaj Ntxwv Kalki txoj kev hlub. Tab sis puas yog kev nkag mus rau Satyayuga yooj yim li ntawd? Tsis yog. Kev nkag mus rau Satyayuga tsis yog zoo li ua ib qho tshuaj hauv ib khob thiab haus nws kom leej twg haus nws li no yuav tau nkag mus rau Satyayuga!

Yuav nkag mus rau Satyayuga, txhua tus tib neeg yuav tsum hloov pauv hauv nws lub neej. Nws yog qhov tsim nyog tias nrog rau kev ua haujlwm hauv ntiaj teb thiab lub luag haujlwm, nws pib ua lub neej sab ntsuj plig.

Piav qhia tias lub sijhawm yuav los yuav zoo li cas rau cov neeg pe hawm, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ହଟି ଯାଇ ସତ୍ୟର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଭକତମାନେ ରହିବେ ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଉଲ୍ଲାସ।
ବହୁ କଷ୍ଟ ପାଇଛନ୍ତି ଭକତ ମୋହର,
ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନରେ ଦୁଃଖ କଷ୍ଟ ଯିବ ଦୂର।
ଏ କଳି ହଟିବ ଯୁଗ ଅନନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରଣ ହୋଇବ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Kaḷiyuga haṭi jāi satyara prakāśha,
bhakatamāne rahibe atyanta ullāsa.
bahu kaṣhṭa pāichhanti bhakata mohara,
alpa dinare duḥkha kaṣhṭa jiba dūra.
e kaḷi haṭiba juga ananta hoiba,
dīna Achyutī kāmanā pūraṇa hoiba.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, cov neeg pe hawm tau raug kev txom nyem ntau hauv Kaliyuga, tab sis tom qab ib lub sijhawm luv luv thaum Kaliyuga xaus thiab lub teeb ntawm lub hnub nyoog nyob mus ib txhis (Ananta Yuga) tshwm sim, cov neeg pe hawm yuav zoo siab heev. Tom qab ntawd tag nrho lawv cov kev nyuaj siab thiab kev mob yuav ploj mus.

Saint Bhima Bhoi sau hauv ib phau ntawv Malika ntawm nws ‘Stuti Chintamani’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ନିବାରିବେ ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିବେ ପାଳିବେ ଜନ ପରଜା,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ ଅନାଦି ହୋଇବେ ରାଜା।
ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ ଭକତ ସକଳ ହରି ହରିବେ ଯେ ଦୁଃଖ,
ହରିବୋଲ ହୁଳହୁଳି ଶବଦରେ ପୂରିବ ଏହି ତ୍ରୈଲୋକ୍ୟ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Duṣṭa nibāribe santhaṅku rakhibe pāḷibe jana parajā,
Kaḷiyuga sheṣe satya upujiba Anādi hoibe rājā.
Haraṣa hoibe bhakata sakaḷa hari haribe je duḥkha,
Haribola huḷuhuḷi shabadare pūriba ehi traiḷokya.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, Tus Tswv Siab Tshaj Plaws yuav rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab tiv thaiv cov neeg dawb huv thiab cov neeg pe hawm, thiab saib xyuas cov pej xeem hauv Ntiaj Teb. Kaliyuga yuav xaus thiab Satyayuga yuav los txog, thiab Vaj Ntxwv Kalki Nws Tus Kheej yuav yog tus kav tag nrho lub ntiaj teb. Tus Tswv yuav tshem tawm cov kev nyuaj siab ntawm txhua tus neeg pe hawm thiab ua rau lawv zoo siab. Cov neeg pe hawm yuav zoo siab, thiab tag nrho peb lub ntiaj teb yuav nrov nrog kev hu npe 'Hari'.

13.1 Thaum twg Satyayuga yuav pib?

Bhabishya Malika hais tias Ananta Yuga lossis Adi Satyayuga yuav pib xyoo 2033. Tus Mahapurush sau hauv nws phau ntawv Malika –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ଡାହାଣରେ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ରଖ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ଯବନ ଗଣ ବିନାଶଟି ଧ୍ରୁବ,
ଦୁଇ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କରୁ ଆରମ୍ଭ।
ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ଅସତ୍ୟ ମାନ ହଟି ଯାଉଥିବ,
ଅଧର୍ମର ନାଶେ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରଭାବୀ ହୋଇବ।
ତେତିଶିରେ ଚାରି ପାଦ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମା ତହୁଁ ବିକଶିବ।
ସରଳ ସଜ୍ଜନ ସାଧୁ ସନ୍ଥ ଜନ ମେଳ,
ହେବା ପାଇଁ ଏ ଧରା ରେ ଆସିଗଲା ବେଳ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Dui ḍāhāṇare shūna eka tini rakha,
sampūrṇṇa satya prakāsha hoiba pratyakṣa.
Mlechha jabana gaṇa bināshaṭi dhruba,
dui shūna eka tini aṅkaru ārambha.
Anyāya asatya māna haṭi jāuthiba,
adharmara nāshe dharma prabhābhī hoiba.
Tetishire chāri pāda dharma prakāshiba,
satya shānti dayā kṣamā tahuñ bikaśhiba.
Saraḷa sajjana sādhu santha jana meḷa,
hebā pāiñ e dharā re āsigalā beḷa.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus Mahapurush sau tias txij xyoo 2013, kev rhuav tshem Kaliyuga thiab lub teeb ntawm Qhov Tseeb yuav pib maj mam. Txij thaum ntawd los, kev rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab cov neeg txhaum yuav pib. Lub zog ntawm kev dag, kev tsis ncaj ncees, thiab kev tsis ncaj ncees yuav txo qis, thiab Dharma txoj kev cuam tshuam yuav pib nce. Tus Mahapurush ntxiv sau tias los ntawm kev ua li no, xyoo 2033, lub teeb ntawm tag nrho plaub tus ncej ntawm Dharma yuav tshwm sim, txhais tau tias kev tsim tsa Dharma tiav yuav tshwm sim. Txij thaum ntawd los, cov txiaj ntsig ntawm Qhov Tseeb, Kev Thaj Yeeb, Kev Ua Siab Zoo, thiab Kev Zam Txim yuav pib tsim kom tiav. Yuav muaj kev sib koom ua ke ntawm txhua tus neeg zoo, siab zoo thiab dawb huv, cov neeg dawb huv, cov neeg txawj ntse.

Tus Mahapurush sau dua –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଗୁପତୁ ଗୁପତ, ତେତିଶି ଅଙ୍କେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ଭକତମାନେ ଯେ ଜାଣିବେ, ସମସ୍ତେ ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ।
ସଂସାର ଉଶ୍ଵାସ ହୋଇବ, କାଳର ଭୟ ଯେ ଛାଡିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ବସ୍ତ୍ରରେ ସୁଖୀ ହେବେ, ଧର୍ମ ପଥକୁ ଆଚୋରିବେ।
ଶସ୍ୟ ଯେ ବହୁତ ହୋଇବ, ଲକ୍ଷ୍ମୀ କୃପାରୁ ସବୁ ହେବ।
ସତ୍ୟରେ ସର୍ବେ ଆତଜାତ, ସତ୍ୟ କଳି ଯେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ପୁରାଣ ଭାଗବତ ମାନ, କରିବେ ଗାନ ସଂକୀର୍ତ୍ତନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶ ଯିବ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
କହନ୍ତି ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ କରତା, ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନଥିବେ ଏକ ଜାଣ, ଭକ୍ତ ଭଗବାନ ମିଳନ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Atyanta gupatu gupata, Tetishi aṅke heba khyāta.
Bhakatamāne je jāṇibe, samaste haraṣa hoibe.
Saṃsāra ushwāsa hoiba, kāḷara bhaya je chhāḍiba.
Anna bastrare sukhī hebe, dharma pathaku āchoribe.
Shasya je bahuta hoiba, Laxmī kṛpāru sabu heba.
Satyare sarbe ātajāta, satya kaḷi je heba khyāta.
Purāṇa Bhāgabata māna, karibe gāna saṅkīrttana.
Kaḷi kalmaṣa nāsha jiba, Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Kahanti Brahmāṇḍa karatā, ananta jugara byabasthā.
Mlechha nathibe eka jāṇa, bhakta Bhagavān milana.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Tus Mahapurush hais tias qhov no yog ib qho zais cia heev thiab nws yuav tshwm sim hauv tus lej 33 (xyoo 2033). Tsuas yog cov neeg pe hawm thiaj yuav paub qhov no. Lawv yuav zoo siab heev paub qhov no. Lub ntiaj teb yuav dim ntawm kev txhaum; yuav tsis muaj kev ntshai lossis kev tsis meej pem hauv leej twg lub siab. Txhua tus yuav zoo siab, txhua tus yuav muaj zaub mov thiab khaub ncaws txaus, txhua tus yuav ua raws li txoj kev Dharma. Tom qab ntawd yuav muaj kev sau qoob loo zoo heev, thiab qhov no yuav yog vim Niam Lakshmi txoj kev hlub. Txhua tus yuav taug kev ntawm Qhov Tseeb; lub hnub nyoog nyob mus ib txhis yuav los txog. Txhua tus yuav nyeem Puranas thiab Bhagavat thiab hu nkauj qhuas Vajtswv. Kaliyuga yuav raug rhuav tshem tag nrho; cov lus no yuav tsis tshee hnyo txhua. Tus Mahapurush ntxiv hais tias qhov no yuav yog lub hnub nyoog nyob mus ib txhis thiab qhov no yuav yog kev npaj ntawm lub hnub nyoog nyob mus ib txhis (Adya Satyayuga). Yuav tsis muaj ib tus neeg tsis ncaj ncees, phem, lossis mlechha hauv Ntiaj Teb. Tsuas yog cov neeg pe hawm thiaj yuav nyob thiab tag nrho lawv yuav koom nrog Vajtswv.

Ib tus Mahapurush yug hauv xyoo pua 20 (uas peb tau tham txog ua ntej), thaum hais txog Tus Tswv Siab Tshaj Plaws, Niam Yogamaya, thiab cov vajtswv thiab vajtswv poj niam, sau –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତିନି ଚାରି ତିନି ଧାଡିରେ ତ ଜାଣ,
ଏହି ଦିନ ଠାରୁ ହେବ ସତ୍ୟର ନିୟମ।
ଗୁରୁବାର ଦିନ ଥିବ ଶୁଣୁଥାଅ ନର,
ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ସତ୍ୟ ଭୋଗ ତ ଆମର।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Tini tini chāri tini dhāḍire ta jāṇa,
ehi dina thāru heba satyara niyama.
Gurubāra dina thiba śhuṇuthāa nara,
sehi dinathāru satya bhoga ta amara.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Ntawm no tus Mahapurush thov kom sau “3” plaub zaug ib zaug ntxiv. Qhov ntawd yog 3333. Tus Mahapurush hais tias txij hnub no mus, Satyayuga yuav pib hauv Ntiaj Teb. Ntawm no los ntawm 3333, tus Mahapurush txhais tau tias – hnub thib peb, lub hlis thib peb, thiab peb caug peb xyoos, piv txwv li, Lub Peb Hlis 3, 2033. Tus Mahapurush ntxiv sau tias hnub ntawd yuav yog Hnub Thursday thiab Satyayuga yuav pib txij hnub ntawd.

Tus Mahapurush sau dua ib zaug ntxiv –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତାରିଖ ତ ତିନି ମାସ ବିଂଶ ଶାଳ,
ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କ ତ ପରେ ସତ୍ୟ ସତ୍ୟ ବୋଲ।

Kev Sau Ua Lus Lwm Yam:

Tini tārikha ta tini māsa biṃśha śhāḷa,
tetishā aṅka ta pare satya satya bola.

Lub Ntsiab Lus:
Qhov ntawd yog, tom qab hnub thib peb ntawm lub hlis thib peb ntawm tus lej peb caug peb ntawm xyoo nees nkaum, piv txwv li, Lub Peb Hlis 3, 2033, Satyayuga yuav los.

Yog li ntawd, tsis muaj kev tsis ntseeg tam sim no tias lub sijhawm tshiab (Ananta Yuga lossis Adi Satyayuga) yuav pib xyoo 2033. Nws yuav yog lub sijhawm ntawm tus neeg pe hawm thiab Vajtswv xwb. Hauv lwm lo lus, tsuas yog cov neeg pe hawm thiaj yuav nkag mus rau lub sijhawm no. Tus Tswv yuav tiv thaiv Ntiaj Teb nrog Qhov Tseeb, Dharma, thiab Kev Ncaj Ncees, thiab txhua tus neeg pe hawm yuav xyaum Qhov Tseeb, Kev Thaj Yeeb, Kev Ua Siab Zoo, thiab Kev Zam Txim hauv lawv lub neej.

14 Cov ntsiab lus

Bhabishya Malika yog ib phau ntawv sau los ntawm ntau tus Panchasakhas yug txog 600 xyoo dhau los thiab qee tus lwm tus Odia cov neeg dawb huv thiab cov txiv neej zoo yug hauv xyoo pua 17, 18, 19, thiab 20. Cov ntawv Malika feem ntau yog sau los ntawm Panchasakhas ntawm Vaj Ntxwv Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Cov Panchasakhas no tshwm sim los ua Vaj Ntxwv cov phooj ywg ze hauv txhua lub sijhawm nrog cov npe sib txawv, thiab hauv Kaliyuga ib yam nkaus, txog 600 xyoo dhau los, cov Panchasakhas no tau pom qhov ze ntawm Vaj Ntxwv tus kheej, Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Cov npe ntawm cov Panchasakhas no yug hauv Kaliyuga yog – Achyutananda Das, Balaram Das, Jashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, thiab Jagannath Das.

Panchasakhas raug suav tias yog ib feem ntawm Vaj Ntxwv tus kheej (Anshavatar), thiab txog 600 xyoo dhau los, cov ntawv Malika tau sau los ntawm lawv tsuas yog raws li Vaj Ntxwv txoj lus txib. Lub hom phiaj tseem ceeb ntawm kev sau cov ntawv Malika yog kev noj qab nyob zoo rau cov neeg pe hawm – los txhawb cov neeg pe hawm lub siab uas tsaug zog thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga thiab cawm lawv los ntawm kev coj lawv. Cov Panchasakhas tau sau meej meej hauv lawv cov ntawv tias cov ntawv no yuav tsum tsis txhob qhia rau leej twg lwm tus tshwj tsis yog cov neeg pe hawm. Cov Panchasakhas kuj tau qhia meej tias tsuas yog cov neeg uas tau pe hawm Vaj Ntxwv txij thaum yug los ua ntej thiab lub sijhawm dhau los thiaj yuav txais tau cov lus ntawm Malika.

Vim Malika yog Mahaprabhu Tus Kheej lub suab thiab tau sau los ntawm Panchasakhas tsuas yog raws li Vaj Ntxwv txoj lus txib, yog li ntawd txhua lo lus ntawm Malika yog zoo li kab sau rau hauv pob zeb uas tsis tuaj yeem ua yuam kev thiab tsis tuaj yeem hloov pauv. Tsis muaj qhov xav tsis thoob hauv qhov no tias ntau qhov kev kwv yees ntawm Malika sau rau lub sijhawm tam sim no tau ua pov thawj tias yog 100% tseeb. Piv txwv li – kev kwv yees txog Is Nrias teb txoj kev ywj pheej, kev kwv yees txog Gandhiji yug, kev kwv yees txog kev tsim lub teb chaws hu ua Pakistan, kev kwv yees txog kev tsim Hirakud dam hauv Odisha, thiab lwm yam.

Bhabishya Malika tham txog cov ncauj lus ntxaws ntxaws xws li Kaliyuga xaus, Kalki Avatar, kev rhuav tshem cov neeg phem thiab cov neeg txhaum, kev tiv thaiv cov neeg pe hawm, kev tsim tsa Dharma, Ananta Yuga tuaj txog, thiab lwm yam. Malika hais tias lub hnub nyoog Kaliyuga, uas yog 4,32,000 xyoo, yuav raug txo mus rau tsuas yog 5,000 xyoo vim muaj ntau yam kev txhaum uas tau ua hauv Kaliyuga. Tom qab 5,000 xyoo dhau mus, Vaj Ntxwv Jagannath yuav tawm ntawm Nws Nilachala Dham, Puri, thiab yug los ua tib neeg lub cev hauv Biraja Kshetra, Jajpur ntawm Odisha, thiab tom qab ntawd nyob hauv Khandagiri, Bhubaneswar. Raws li Malika, qhov no yuav yog Vajtswv tus Kalki Avatar.

Malika hais tias thaum kawg ntawm Kaliyuga, cov neeg pe hawm yuav tau txais ntau yam cim los ntawm Puri Jagannath lub tuam tsev thiab lwm lub tsev teev ntuj thiab tuam tsev hauv Is Nrias teb, pom qhov no lawv yuav nkag siab tias Kaliyuga tau xaus thiab Vaj Ntxwv Kalki tau yug los. Piv txwv li, lub chij tseem ceeb ya tawm ntawm Jagannath lub tuam tsev, pob zeb poob rov qab los ntawm lub tuam tsev, Nilachakra tig, tus dav zaum ntau zaus saum lub tuam tsev, thiab lwm yam. Nws paub tias tag nrho cov cim no tau pom los ntawm cov neeg pe hawm hauv ob peb xyoos dhau los.

Malika taw qhia txog Vaj Ntxwv Kalki yug hauv qee xyoo ntawm 2005-2008. Raws li Malika, Kalki Avatar lub hom phiaj yog rhuav tshem cov neeg phem, tiv thaiv cov neeg pe hawm, thiab rov tsim tsa Dharma hauv Ntiaj Teb. Malika hais tias qhov Yuga-parivartana no yuav pib xyoo 2026-2027 thaum lub sijhawm Meena-Shani yoga los ntawm Vaj Ntxwv Kalki thiab yuav xaus tag nrho xyoo 2031-2032. Lub sijhawm no, tag nrho cov kev ua phem loj yuav tshwm sim rau xyoo 2029.

Malika kuj hais tias thaum Vaj Ntxwv yuav tsim tsa Dharma hauv Ntiaj Teb, Puri Vaj Ntxwv lub npe yuav yog Shri Dibyasingha Deb, txhais tau tias tag nrho Vaj Ntxwv cov kev ua phem yuav tshwm sim thaum Puri Vaj Ntxwv Dibyasingha Deb kav. Raws li Malika, cov kev ua phem no feem ntau yuav yog los ntawm Tsib Yam (Panchabhuta) kev rhuav tshem thiab Tsov Rog Thoob Ntiaj Teb.

Malika hais tias tom qab tag nrho cov kev ua phem xaus tag nrho rau xyoo 2031-2032, Adi-Satyayuga lossis Anantayuga yuav pib xyoo 2033.
